### [1] [DocArena: Turning Raw Documents into Controllable Training Environments for Document Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26122)
*Jiamian Wang,Ruiyi Zhang,Tong Yu,Jing Shi,Samyadeep Basu,Rajiv Jain,Zhiqiang Tao,Tong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DocArena，一种全自动数据策展流水线，将原始多模态文档转化为搜索智能体的训练环境，无需人工标注。通过MLLM视觉感知、跨页面信息分布分析和级联质量保证，构建了覆盖16个领域49种语言的DocArena-79K数据集。结合解耦视觉感知与策略模型的Doc-Search智能体架构，训练出的智能体在多模态文档检索和QA任务上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的搜索智能体训练方法依赖文本为主的训练环境，难以构造可控、可扩展且支持多模态数据的训练环境。为解决这一问题，提出了全自动的数据策展方法。

Method: 提出DocArena全自动数据策展流水线：(1) 通过MLLM视觉感知对文档进行结构化索引；(2) 分析跨页面信息分布以构造推理密集型QA对；(3) 通过MLLM执行级联质量保证操作。同时设计Doc-Search智能体架构，将视觉感知与策略模型解耦，使基于文本的大模型能够作为多模态文档检索和QA的推理骨干。

Result: 在统一评估框架下，6个多模态文档场景和7个文本QA基准上的实验表明，基于DocArena数据训练的智能体在检索准确率和QA质量上均达到最优。对智能体搜索行为的进一步分析证实了构建训练环境的有效性和可控性。

Conclusion: DocArena提供了一种可控、可扩展、支持多模态数据的训练环境构建方案，有效提升了搜索智能体的检索和问答能力，且训练环境具有良好的可控性。

Original Abstract: Recent methods train search agents via reinforcement learning from (question, answer, evidence) tuples without requiring expert trajectories. The tuples serve as the training environment, and whose properties directly shape what search strategies and generalization abilities the agent can develop. While prior works have made encouraging progress in improving training data quality, existing environments remain predominantly text-based and existing approaches can struggle to construct training environments that are controllable, scalable, and account for multimodal data. Given this, we propose DocArena, a fully automated data curation pipeline building on the practical need for multimodal document search and question-answering. It transforms raw document collections into training environments for search agents without any human annotation. The pipeline first structures and indexes documents through MLLM-based visual perception, then profiles and leverage the cross-page information distribution to construct reasoning-intensive QA pairs, as well as performs cascaded quality assurance operations via MLLM. We introduce DocArena-79K with QA pairs from 8,336 documents spanning 16 domains and 49 languages. We further design a Doc-Search agent infrastructure that decouples visual perception from the policy model, allowing text-based LLMs to serve as the reasoning backbone for multimodal document retrieval and QA. Under a unified evaluation framework where only the policy model differs, experiments on six multimodal document scenarios and seven text-based QA benchmarks show that agents trained on DocArena data achieve the best performance on both retrieval accuracy and QA quality. Further analysis on agent search behaviors confirms the effectiveness and controllability of the constructed training environment.

中文摘要翻译: 近期的方法通过从（问题，答案，证据）元组中进行强化学习来训练搜索智能体，无需专家轨迹。这些元组作为训练环境，其属性直接塑造了智能体能够发展的搜索策略和泛化能力。虽然先前工作在提升训练数据质量方面取得了令人鼓舞的进展，但现有环境仍以文本为主，且现有方法在构建可控、可扩展并支持多模态数据的训练环境方面面临挑战。为此，本文提出DocArena，一种基于多模态文档搜索和问答实际需求的全自动数据策展流水线。该流水线无需任何人工标注，即可将原始文档集合转化为搜索智能体的训练环境。该流水线首先通过基于多模态大语言模型的视觉感知对文档进行结构化索引，然后分析并利用跨页面信息分布来构建推理密集型问答对，并通过多模态大语言模型执行级联质量保证操作。我们推出了DocArena-79K数据集，其问答对来自覆盖16个领域和49种语言的8,336份文档。我们还设计了一种Doc-Search智能体基础设施，将视觉感知与策略模型解耦，使基于文本的大语言模型能够作为多模态文档检索和问答的推理骨干。在仅改变策略模型的统一评估框架下，在六个多模态文档场景和七个基于文本的问答基准上的实验表明，基于DocArena数据训练的智能体在检索准确率和问答质量上均取得了最优性能。对智能体搜索行为的进一步分析证实了所构建训练环境的有效性和可控性。

</details>


### [2] [Predicting Fruit Quality with a Hybrid Machine Learning and Image Processing Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26165)
*Amir Reza Hashemi,Shahram Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合图像处理与深度学习（CNN）的混合方法，通过逻辑回归融合两种模型的输出，实现水果新鲜度的实时分类，在苹果和橙子数据集上达到90%以上准确率，且无需高计算资源。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 水果腐败导致重大经济损失，现有方法在实时性和计算资源方面存在局限，亟需一种低成本、高效率且可实际部署的水果新鲜度评估方案。

Method: 首先开发图像处理算法量化腐败程度（0-100分），同时训练CNN进行二分类（新鲜/腐败）；然后通过逻辑回归融合两者的结果以提升准确率；最终利用该逻辑回归模型使图像处理算法直接输出二分类结果，从而在实时应用中去除对CNN的依赖。

Result: 在苹果和橙子数据集上实现实时性能，准确率超过90%；不依赖高计算资源；主要局限是要求水果在白色或透明背景上单独放置。

Conclusion: 将简单图像处理技术与机器学习相结合，能够在农业领域提供实用且高效的解决方案，未来可通过引入先进分割模型自动去除背景来进一步改进。

Original Abstract: Fruit spoilage is a significant issue in agriculture, leading to substantial economic losses. Addressing this, our study introduces a hybrid approach combining image processing and deep learning to assess fruit freshness. We developed an image processing algorithm that quantifies spoilage on a scale from 0 (fully fresh) to 100 (fully rotten). Alongside, we trained a convolutional neural network (CNN) to perform binary classification (fresh or rotten) using a large dataset of fruit images. The outcomes of both methods were synthesized using logistic regression to enhance the accuracy of freshness predictions. Subsequently, this logistic regression model was utilized to enable the image processing algorithm to provide binary classification based on its percentage output, thus eliminating the need for the CNN in real-time applications. Our approach, which does not require high computational resources, achieved real-time performance and was validated with over 90% accuracy on a dataset comprising apples and oranges. The primary limitation lies in the requirement for fruits to be isolated on a background that must be either white or transparent, suggesting future improvements could include advanced segmentation models to automate background removal. This study's results highlight the potential of integrating simple image processing techniques with machine learning to provide practical solutions in the agricultural sector.

中文摘要翻译: 水果腐败是农业中的一个重要问题，会导致重大的经济损失。针对这一问题，我们的研究引入了一种结合图像处理与深度学习的混合方法来评估水果的新鲜度。我们开发了一种图像处理算法，能够将腐败程度量化为从0（完全新鲜）到100（完全腐烂）的分数。同时，我们训练了一个卷积神经网络（CNN），使用大规模水果图像数据集进行二分类（新鲜或腐烂）。通过逻辑回归对两种方法的结果进行综合，以提升新鲜度预测的准确性。随后，利用该逻辑回归模型使图像处理算法能够基于其百分比输出直接进行二分类，从而在实时应用中消除对CNN的需求。我们的方法不需要高计算资源，实现了实时性能，并在包含苹果和橙子的数据集上以超过90%的准确率得到验证。该方法的主要局限性在于要求水果在白色或透明背景上单独放置，这表明未来的改进方向可以包括引入先进的分割模型以自动去除背景。本研究的结果凸显了将简单图像处理技术与机器学习相结合，为农业领域提供实用解决方案的潜力。

</details>


### [3] [Beyond Single-Source Cognitive Taskonomy:Multi-Source Task Relations through fMRI Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26279)
*Junfeng Xia,Wendu Li,Mengjiao Zhang,Jie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用掩码fMRI重建和布尔整数规划，将基于fMRI的认知任务图谱从单源迁移扩展为多源迁移，发现在多源设置下工作记忆状态具有最高的全局分配优先级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于重建的任务图谱主要研究单个源任务到单个目标任务的一对一迁移，缺乏对多源迁移及预算约束下任务分配的研究，因此需要将单源迁移扩展到多源迁移场景。

Method: 使用掩码fMRI重建作为自监督目标，在23个HCP任务状态下训练了1,127个任务特定和迁移模型，并采用布尔整数规划(BIP)分析预算约束下的任务分配。

Result: 单源迁移具有方向性和范式结构化特征，运动状态在运动范式内迁移良好但对非运动目标支持有限；多源迁移依赖于源集组成，表明多对一任务关系无法完全由成对任务图谱捕捉；BIP在不同预算下反复将直接监督分配给0-back和2-back工作记忆状态。

Conclusion: 揭示了跨范式受限的运动簇以及工作记忆状态在特定全局分配目标下的高优先级，将基于重建的fMRI任务图谱从一对一迁移扩展到了多对一任务关系和预算约束任务依赖。

Original Abstract: Cognitive tasks are organized by shared and specialized neural processes. Masked fMRI reconstruction provides a common self-supervised objective for quantifying transfer relations among task states, but existing reconstruction-based taskonomies mainly study one-to-one transfer from a single source task to a target. Here, we extend an fMRI cognitive taskonomy from single-source to multi-source transfer across 23 Human Connectome Project task states and use Boolean Integer Programming (BIP) to analyze budget-constrained task allocation. We train 1,127 task-specific and transfer models. Single-source transfer is directional and paradigm structured: motor states transfer well within the motor paradigm but provide limited support to most non-motor targets, consistent with a shared sensorimotor execution system and effector-specific representations. Multi-source transfer depends on the composition of the source set, suggesting that many-to-one task relations are not fully captured by pairwise taskonomy alone. Across supervision budgets, BIP repeatedly allocates direct supervision to several 0-back and 2-back working-memory states, although these states are not consistently the strongest individual sources. This pattern may reflect the integration of perceptual, attentional, and executive processes in working-memory tasks. Together, these findings reveal a cross-paradigm-limited motor cluster and working-memory states with high priority under the specified global allocation objective. Our study extends reconstruction-based fMRI taskonomy from one-to-one transfer to many-to-one task relations and budget-constrained task dependencies.

中文摘要翻译: 认知任务由共享和专门的神经过程组织。掩码fMRI重建为量化任务状态之间的迁移关系提供了一个通用的自监督目标，但现有的基于重建的任务图谱主要研究从单个源任务到目标的一对一迁移。在此，我们将fMRI认知任务图谱从单源迁移扩展到多源迁移，跨越23个人类连接组项目任务状态，并使用布尔整数规划(BIP)分析预算约束下的任务分配。我们训练了1,127个任务特定和迁移模型。单源迁移具有方向性和范式结构化特征：运动状态在运动范式内迁移良好，但大多数非运动目标提供的支持有限，这与共享的感觉运动执行系统和效应器特异性表征相一致。多源迁移依赖于源集的组成，表明多对一任务关系无法完全由成对任务图谱单独捕捉。在不同监督预算下，BIP反复将直接监督分配给几个0-back和2-back工作记忆状态，尽管这些状态并非始终是最强的单个源。这一模式可能反映了工作记忆任务中感知、注意和执行过程的整合。总之，这些发现揭示了跨范式受限的运动簇以及在特定全局分配目标下具有高优先级的工作记忆状态。我们的研究将基于重建的fMRI任务图谱从一对一迁移扩展到多对一任务关系和预算约束任务依赖。

</details>


### [4] [LCG: Long-Context Consistent Image Generation with Sparse Relational Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26171)
*Zihao Wang,Yijia Xu,Haoze Zheng,Xuran Ma,Haokun Gui,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出长上下文生成（LCG）框架，通过稀疏关系注意力（SRA）和路由一致性约束（RCC）实现多图像文本到图像生成中的语义和外观一致性，并构建了大规模长上下文一致性数据集（LCCD）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单图像生成模型质量高，但在漫画、故事板等需要序列输出的场景中难以保持跨图像的一致性，缺乏面向长上下文多图像生成的专用方法。

Method: 1）稀疏关系注意力（SRA）：选择性关注扩展视觉上下文中的核心特征，保证语义和布局信息传播的计算可追踪性；2）路由一致性约束（RCC）：利用身份感知掩码对齐生成分支间的结构模式，缓解多角色场景中的外观漂移；3）构建LCCD数据集，含60万训练序列和1千测试序列，每序列6-20张图像。

Result: LCG在提示对齐和角色一致性上优于对比基线方法，尤其在多角色场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: LCG框架有效解决了长上下文多图像生成中的一致性问题，SRA和RCC机制分别保证了计算效率和语义对齐，LCCD数据集为后续研究提供了基准资源。

Original Abstract: Recent image generation models achieve impressive quality in single-image synthesis, but often fail to maintain consistency across sequential outputs, as required in comics, storyboards, and visual narratives. We propose Long-Context Generation (LCG), a framework for long-context multi-image text-to-image generation, to improve consistency and scalability in long-context multi-image generation. LCG employs the Sparse Relational Attention (SRA) mechanism to selectively attend to core features across extended visual contexts, ensuring that the propagation of semantic and layout information remains computationally tractable. To enforce semantic alignment, we introduce the Routing Consistency Constraint (RCC), which leverages identity-aware masks to align structural patterns across generation branches, effectively mitigating drift in appearance even in complex multi-character scenes. To support training and evaluation in this setting, we construct the Long-Context Consistency Dataset (LCCD), a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising character-centric multi-image sequences spanning varied situational contexts. LCCD contains 600K training sequences and a separate 1K test set, with each sequence containing 6 to 20 images. The experiments demonstrate that LCG outperforms the compared baselines in prompt alignment and character consistency for long-context image generation, including multi-character scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的图像生成模型在单图像合成方面取得了令人瞩目的质量，但在漫画、故事板和视觉叙事等需要序列输出的场景中，往往难以保持跨图像的一致性。我们提出了长上下文生成（LCG）框架，这是一种用于长上下文多图像文本到图像生成的方法，旨在提升长上下文多图像生成中的一致性和可扩展性。LCG采用稀疏关系注意力（SRA）机制，选择性关注扩展视觉上下文中的核心特征，确保语义和布局信息的传播在计算上可追踪。为了强制语义对齐，我们引入了路由一致性约束（RCC），该约束利用身份感知掩码对齐生成分支间的结构模式，即使在复杂的多角色场景中也能有效缓解外观漂移。为了支持该设置下的训练和评估，我们构建了长上下文一致性数据集（LCCD），这是一个大规模合成数据集，包含以角色为中心的多图像序列，涵盖多样的情境上下文。LCCD包含60万条训练序列和一个独立的1千条测试集，每个序列包含6到20张图像。实验结果表明，LCG在长上下文图像生成（包括多角色场景）的提示对齐和角色一致性方面优于对比基线方法。

</details>


### [5] [Self-Supervised Tree-level Biomass Estimation in Urban Environments From Airborne LiDAR and Optical Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26194)
*Jose Bermudez,Zilong Zhong,Dominic Cyr,Camile Sothe,Alemu Gonsamo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于航空LiDAR和正射影像的城市树木冠层级地上生物量估算框架，利用双流交叉注意力网络进行语义分割，结合多尺度分水岭分割和异速生长模型，在加拿大安大略省810km²区域实现了2018-2023年的冠层级生物量制图，无需人工标注，碳储量变化达39 Gg C。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 城市树木生物量在空间上缺乏精细化的显式量化，现有方法依赖于清查数据或粗分辨率产品，无法解析单个树冠或细尺度异质性。因此需要开发一种能实现冠层级、管理相关分辨率且无需人工标注的生物量估算框架。

Method: 采用落叶期航空LiDAR（8-10 pulses/m²）和近红外RGB正射影像（0.16-0.20 m），使用基于规则伪标签训练的双流交叉注意力网络生成建筑、针叶树和阔叶树的语义标记；通过多尺度分水岭分割进行树冠勾绘；利用冠层面积-高度幂律代理模型（校准自Lambert等人的物种特异性异速生长方程，共21,921棵清查树木）估算地上生物量；采用深度集成不确定性图指导高不确定性树冠使用合并异速方程。

Result: 语义分割的全局/平均精确率、召回率和Dice分数分别为0.86、0.83和0.84。对于18,713个清查-分割匹配对，清查冠层几何的AGB预测R²=0.609，操作化分割下R²=0.570。聚集到30m分辨率，2018年总AGB储量为1.73 Tg，2023年为1.81 Tg（811-850 Gg C），尼亚加拉断崖区域密度达~140 Mg/ha，五年净碳增益39 Gg C。

Conclusion: 该框架利用标准省级数据，无需人工标注，能生成公开的双时相冠层级城市树木生物量数据库，实现了管理相关分辨率下林外树木的生物量估算，并识别出树冠勾绘是剩余不确定性来源。

Original Abstract: Urban tree biomass remains less spatially explicitly quantified than biomass in managed forests because many estimates rely on inventories or coarse products that cannot resolve individual crowns or fine-scale heterogeneity. We present a crown-level above-ground biomass (AGB) framework for an 810~km$^2$ landscape in Ontario, Canada, using leaf-off airborne LiDAR (8--10~pulses~m$^{-2}$) and near-infrared RGB orthophotography (0.16--0.20~m) from 2018 and 2023. A dual-stream cross-attention network trained on rule-based pseudo-labels produced semantic marks for buildings, needleleaf trees, and deciduous trees, supporting crown delineation and functional-type assignment. On independently annotated withheld tiles, global/mean precision, recall, and Dice scores were 0.86, 0.83, and 0.84. Crowns were delineated with multiscale watershed segmentation in mapped tree areas, and AGB was estimated from a crown area--height power-law proxy calibrated to species-specific allometry (Lambert et al., 2005) for 21,921 inventory trees. For 18,713 inventory--segment matched pairs from a 90,726-tree held-out test set, AGB prediction achieved $R^2=0.609$ using inventory crown geometry and $R^2=0.570$ under operational segmentation, identifying crown delineation as the remaining uncertainty source. Aggregated to 30~m, estimates yielded total AGB stocks of 1.73~Tg in 2018 and 1.81~Tg in 2023 (811--850~Gg~C), local densities up to ${\sim}140$~Mg~ha$^{-1}$ along the Niagara Escarpment, and a net carbon gain of 39~Gg~C over five years. Deep-ensemble uncertainty maps highlighted high-epistemic-uncertainty areas linked to underrepresented land covers and guided assignment of uncertain crowns to a pooled allometric equation. The framework uses standard provincial data, requires no manual annotation, and produces a public bitemporal crown-level AGB database for trees outside forests at management-relevant resolution.

中文摘要翻译: 城市树木生物量在空间上的显式量化仍远不如人工林生物量，因为许多估算依赖于无法解析单个树冠或细尺度异质性的清查数据或粗分辨率产品。我们利用2018和2023年落叶期航空LiDAR（8-10 pulses/m²）和近红外RGB正射影像（0.16-0.20 m），提出了加拿大安大略省810 km²景观区域的冠层级地上生物量（AGB）框架。一个基于规则伪标签训练的双流交叉注意力网络生成了建筑、针叶树和阔叶树的语义标记，支持树冠勾绘和功能类型分配。在独立标注的 withheld 瓦片上，全局/平均精确率、召回率和Dice分数分别为0.86、0.83和0.84。在映射的树木区域内采用多尺度分水岭分割勾绘树冠，并通过校准自21,921棵清查树木的物种特异性异速生长方程（Lambert et al., 2005）的冠层面积-高度幂律代理模型估算AGB。对于来自90,726棵保留测试集的18,713个清查-分割匹配对，使用清查冠层几何的AGB预测R²=0.609，操作化分割下R²=0.570，识别出树冠勾绘是剩余不确定性来源。聚集到30 m分辨率，估算结果显示2018年总AGB储量为1.73 Tg，2023年为1.81 Tg（811-850 Gg C），尼亚加拉断崖沿线局部密度高达~140 Mg/ha，五年净碳增益为39 Gg C。深度集成不确定性图突出了与代表性不足的土地覆盖相关的高认知不确定性区域，并指导了将不确定树冠分配到合并异速方程。该框架使用标准省级数据，无需人工标注，生成了一个公开的双时相冠层级林外树木生物量数据库，具有管理相关的分辨率。

</details>


### [6] [A multi-task spatiotemporal deep neural network for predicting penetration depth and morphology in laser welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26260)
*Sen Li,Haichao Cui,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Xinhua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CNN和状态空间模型的多任务深度学习框架，利用熔池图像和焊接参数同时预测激光穿透焊接中的穿透状态、深度和焊缝截面形貌，在测试集上分别达到99.35%的穿透状态准确率、1.79mm的深度误差和95.65%的截面重建准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在激光穿透焊接中，穿透状态和焊缝形貌的评估对焊接质量至关重要，现有方法难以同时高精度预测多个关键质量指标。

Method: 提出多任务深度学习模型，集成CNN和状态空间模型提取顶部熔池图像的时空特征，并融合焊接参数，构建了基于深度学习的时空信息高效处理框架；同时设计了可靠的数据集构建方法以增强模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 穿透状态预测准确率达99.35%，穿透深度预测误差为1.79mm，焊缝横截面重建准确率为95.65%。

Conclusion: 该研究为激光穿透焊接系统的原位质量控制策略提供了新的见解和方法。

Original Abstract: In laser penetration welding, the assessment of penetration state and weld seam morphology plays a crucial role in determining the weld quality. This paper presents a comprehensive introduction of the innovative muti-task deep learning model that has the capability to predict penetration state, depth, and weld seam morphology with high accuracy. The monitoring platform relies on weld pool images captured during the laser welding process using a complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor camera. The proposed model integrates spatiotemporal features extracted from top weld pool images along with welding parameters, establishing a deep learning framework based on convolutional neural networks and state space models for more efficient extraction and processing of spatial-temporal information. Furthermore, a reliable method for constructing the dataset is proposed to enhance both robustness and generalization capability of the developed model. Validation results on the test set demonstrate that prediction accuracy for penetration state can reach 99.35%, while prediction error for penetration depth is 1.79 millimeter, and accuracy of reconstructing the weld cross-section is 95.65%. This study provides new insights and methodologies for in-situ quality control strategies in laser penetration welding systems.

中文摘要翻译: 在激光穿透焊接中，穿透状态和焊缝形貌的评估对焊接质量起着决定性作用。本文全面介绍了一种创新的多任务深度学习模型，该模型能够高精度地预测穿透状态、深度和焊缝形貌。监测平台基于互补金属氧化物半导体相机在激光焊接过程中捕获的熔池图像。所提出的模型集成了从顶部熔池图像中提取的时空特征以及焊接参数，构建了基于卷积神经网络和状态空间模型的深度学习框架，以实现对时空信息更高效的提取和处理。此外，还提出了一种可靠的数据集构建方法，以增强所开发模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。在测试集上的验证结果表明，穿透状态预测准确率可达99.35%，穿透深度预测误差为1.79毫米，焊缝横截面重建准确率为95.65%。本研究为激光穿透焊接系统的原位质量控制策略提供了新的见解和方法。

</details>


### [7] [TaskTok: Delving into Task Tokens for Task-driven Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26615)
*Hongjae Lee,Sojung Kang,Jaeseong Yu,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TaskTok框架，通过选择性恢复与任务相关的token子集（而非全部token），在保持高效计算的同时提升下游视觉任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有任务驱动图像恢复方法使用生成先验对所有隐层token进行无差别更新，导致计算效率低且可能改变语义信息。论文发现token序列中与任务相关的线索呈索引级不均匀分布，因此无需更新所有token。

Method: 提出TaskTok框架，包含一个可学习的token开关（用于选择任务相关token）和一个轻量级token精炼模块（用于恢复所选token），仅选择性恢复任务相关token。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测任务上的大量实验表明，TaskTok显著提升了任务性能，同时具有高计算效率。

Conclusion: 不是所有视觉信息对机器感知同等重要，选择性精炼token子集足以实现任务驱动目标，TaskTok在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Original Abstract: While traditional image restoration focuses on perceptual quality, Task-Driven Image Restoration (TDIR) aims to maximize the performance of downstream high-level vision tasks. Recent approaches leveraging generative priors have shown promise for TDIR; however, they typically suffer from computational inefficiency and potential semantic alteration by indiscriminately updating all latent tokens. In this paper, we posit that not all visual information is equally important for machine perception. Through an analysis of the latent token space, we observe that task-relevant cues are unevenly distributed across the token sequence, exhibiting index-wise specialization. This suggests that selectively refining a subset of tokens can be sufficient for task-driven objectives. Leveraging this insight, we propose TaskTok, a novel framework that selectively restores only task-relevant tokens via a learnable token switch and a lightweight token refinement module. Extensive experiments across image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection demonstrate that TaskTok significantly enhances task performance with high computational efficiency. The source code is available at https://github.com/jimmy9704/TaskTok

中文摘要翻译: 传统图像恢复侧重于感知质量，而任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR）旨在最大化下游高级视觉任务的性能。近期利用生成先验的方法在TDIR上展现出潜力，但这类方法通常对所有隐层token进行无差别更新，导致计算效率低下并可能改变语义信息。本文提出，并非所有视觉信息对机器感知都同等重要。通过对隐层token空间的分析，我们观察到任务相关线索在token序列中呈不均匀分布，表现出索引级特化。这表明选择性精炼token子集足以实现任务驱动目标。基于这一发现，我们提出了TaskTok——一个通过可学习的token开关和轻量级token精炼模块选择性恢复仅与任务相关token的新型框架。在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测上的大量实验表明，TaskTok以高计算效率显著提升了任务性能。源代码已公开于https://github.com/jimmy9704/TaskTok。

</details>


### [8] [FracEvent: Event-Camera Simulation via Fractional-Relaxation Pixel Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26636)
*Langyi Chen,Chuanzhi Xu,Haoxian Zhou,Pengfei Ye,Ziyu Luo,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Xiaoming Chen,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FracEvent是一种新型事件相机模拟器，通过分数阶松弛电压动力学建模像素级生命周期，改善了生成事件的时间结构，在图像重建和光流估计等下游任务中优于现有模拟方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实事件数据难以大规模采集（需要专用传感器、精确同步和任务特定标注），现有模拟器虽然有效但过于简化了每个像素生命周期产生的时间结构，导致事件时序扭曲，削弱了下游迁移性能。

Method: FracEvent采用分数阶松弛电压动力学建模像素级生命周期。给定对数强度轨迹后，驱动一个紧凑的松弛模态栈，将各模态响应组合为电压状态，通过在连续电压轨迹上定位阈值交叉来发射ON/OFF事件，并在更新参考时保留底层记忆模态。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FracEvent改善了生成事件的时间结构，并在图像重建和光流估计的下游迁移任务中显著优于其他模拟基线方法。

Conclusion: FracEvent通过更精确地建模像素的暂态响应动力学，提升了事件模拟的真实性和时序保真度，证明了其在事件相机模拟中的实用价值。

Original Abstract: Event cameras asynchronously report brightness changes with microsecond-level temporal resolution, but real event data remain difficult to collect at scale because specialized sensors, careful synchronization, and task-specific annotations are required. Event-camera simulation is therefore important to event-based vision tasks. Most practical simulators build on contrast-threshold event generation, some with additional filtering, stochastic noise, or hand-tuned sensor parameters. While effective, such formulations often simplify the temporal structure produced by the lifecycle of each pixel, which can distort event timing and weaken downstream transfer. We introduce FracEvent, an event simulator that models this pixel-level lifecycle with fractional-relaxation voltage dynamics. Given a log-intensity trajectory, FracEvent drives a compact stack of relaxation modes, combines their responses into a voltage state, emits ON/OFF events by localizing threshold crossings on the continuous voltage trajectory, and updates the reference while retaining the underlying memory modes. This retained state links residual voltage response to later event timing. We evaluate FracEvent through event-stream comparison and downstream transfer on image reconstruction and optical flow estimation. Across multiple datasets, FracEvent improves the temporal structure of generated events and achieves stronger downstream-transfer results than competing simulator baselines, showing its practical value for event-camera simulation.

中文摘要翻译: 事件相机能够以微秒级时间分辨率异步报告亮度变化，但由于需要专用传感器、精确同步和任务特定标注，真实事件数据难以大规模采集。因此，事件相机模拟对基于事件的视觉任务至关重要。大多数实用模拟器基于对比度阈值事件生成机制，部分还加入了额外滤波、随机噪声或手工调整的传感器参数。这些方法虽然有效，但通常简化了每个像素生命周期产生的时间结构，从而扭曲事件时序并削弱下游迁移性能。本文提出FracEvent，一种通过分数阶松弛电压动力学建模像素级生命周期的事件模拟器。给定对数强度轨迹，FracEvent驱动一个紧凑的松弛模态栈，将各模态响应组合为电压状态，通过在连续电压轨迹上定位阈值交叉来发射ON/OFF事件，并在更新参考的同时保留底层记忆模态。这种保留的状态将残余电压响应与后续事件时序联系起来。我们通过事件流比较以及图像重建和光流估计的下游迁移任务评估FracEvent。在多个数据集上，FracEvent改善了生成事件的时间结构，并在下游迁移结果上显著优于竞争模拟基线方法，展现了其在事件相机模拟中的实用价值。

</details>


### [9] [GeMoE: Gating Entropy is All You Need for Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing in MoE-based Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26287)
*Chaoxiang Cai,Minghe Weng,Jie Li,Yibo Jiang,Longrong Yang,Zequn Qin,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对传统MoE中Top-k静态路由策略无法根据输入自适应选择专家数量、导致资源利用次优的问题，本文提出将令牌路由视为信息编码任务，基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则和门控熵与MDL的关联，设计了门控熵驱动的自适应路由方法GeMoE，在保持99.5%平均性能的同时将专家激活稀疏度提升36.5%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统MoE方法采用Top-k静态路由策略，未能考虑输入变化并自适应选择专家数量，导致资源利用次优。

Method: 将令牌路由视为信息编码任务，将动态路由建模为最小描述长度（MDL）问题；通过验证MoE场景中MDL与门控熵之间的关联，提出基于门控熵的不确定性感知自适应路由方法GeMoE，利用门控熵评估令牌复杂度，自适应决定每个令牌应激活的专家数量。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和基准上，相比原始静态路由，GeMoE平均性能保持率达99.5%，同时专家激活稀疏度平均提升36.5%。

Conclusion: GeMoE通过显式建模模型复杂度与性能之间的权衡，实现了优于传统静态或启发式动态路由方法的效果，在几乎不损失性能的前提下大幅提升了计算效率。

Original Abstract: With the increase in model parameters and training data, the instruction following and generalization capabilities of Large VisionLanguage Models (LVLMs) have been significantly improved. Based on the Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture, LVLMs expand their parameter capacity while maintaining the inference cost. However, traditional MoE methods employ a Top-k static routing strategy, which fails to account for variations in the input and adaptively select the number of experts, resulting in suboptimal resource utilization. In this paper, we propose viewing token routing as an information encoding task, framing dynamic routing as a Minimum Description Length (MDL) problem in encoding By validating the connection between MDL and gating entropy in the MoE scenario, we introduce Gating Entropy-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing (GeMoE) for MoE. Unlike traditional static or heuristic-based dynamic routing methods, GeMoE explicitly models the trade-off between model complexity and performance. By using gating entropy to assess the complexity of tokens, GeMoE adaptively determines the number of experts each token should engage. On a wide range of backbones and benchmarks, our method achieves 99.5% average performance retention compared to the original static routing, while improving average expert activation sparsity by 36.5%.

中文摘要翻译: 随着模型参数和训练数据的增加，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的指令遵循能力和泛化能力得到了显著提升。基于混合专家（MoE）架构，LVLMs在保持推理成本的同时扩展了参数容量。然而，传统MoE方法采用Top-k静态路由策略，未能考虑输入变化并自适应选择专家数量，导致资源利用次优。在本文中，我们提出将令牌路由视为一项信息编码任务，将动态路由建模为编码中的最小描述长度（MDL）问题。通过在MoE场景下验证MDL与门控熵之间的关联，我们引入了基于门控熵的不确定性感知自适应路由方法GeMoE。与传统的静态或基于启发式的动态路由方法不同，GeMoE显式地建模了模型复杂度与性能之间的权衡。通过利用门控熵评估令牌的复杂度，GeMoE自适应地决定每个令牌应参与的专家数量。在多种骨干网络和基准上，我们的方法相比原始静态路由实现了99.5%的平均性能保持率，同时将平均专家激活稀疏度提升了36.5%。

</details>


### [10] [Event-based Gaze Control System for Accurate Real-time Spin Estimation in Professional Ball Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26780)
*Yunpu Hu,Fabian Schilling,Valentina Cavinato,Asude Aydin,Agis Politis,Ricardo Tapiador Morales,Kirk Y. W. Scheper,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于事件相机的主动视觉系统，结合高速振镜和可调焦长焦镜头，实现球类运动中旋转球的实时跟踪与高精度旋转测量，在乒乓球等项目中达到2.1%的转速误差和4.0°的轴误差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在球类运动中，旋转对球的轨迹有重要影响，但使用传统相机在比赛条件下估计球的旋转非常困难，因为球体积小、速度快、旋转快，导致运动模糊和跟踪困难。

Method: 系统由事件相机（高时间分辨率、无运动模糊）、高速俯仰/偏航振镜（保持球在视野内）和可调焦长焦镜头（提高空间分辨率并保持对焦）组成。跟踪采用结合2D事件检测和3D球定位系统的混合方法。旋转估计提出离线s-CMax方法（球面上对比度最大化）和在线方法（不确定性感知CNN + GPU加速批处理对比度最大化）。

Result: 离线s-CMax在静态球上达到2.1%转速误差和4.0°轴误差；在线方法在专业乒乓球比赛中达到8.8%转速误差和6.4°轴误差，延迟3 ms，吞吐量750 Hz。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的主动视觉系统能够有效在真实比赛场景中实时跟踪并高精度估计球的旋转，为未来体育分析提供了新的技术路径。

Original Abstract: Spin plays a crucial role in many ball sports due to its effect on the trajectory of the ball. Vision-based estimation of the ball's spin during a game with conventional cameras is challenging due to the ball's small size, high speed, and fast rotation. To address these challenges, we propose an event-based active vision system that can track unmodified balls and measure their spin in real-time. The system consists of an event camera for its high temporal resolution and minimal motion blur, high-speed pan/tilt galvanometer mirrors to keep the ball in the field of view, and a low-latency focus-tunable telephoto lens to increase the spatial resolution on the ball and keep it in focus. To track the ball, we use a hybrid approach that combines 2D event-based detection for centering and 3D positions from a ball localization system for re-initialization. For high-accuracy spin estimation, we propose an offline method that performs contrast maximization on the sphere (s-CMax). This method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on static balls across multiple sports (table tennis, baseball, tennis, and golf), with mean magnitude and axis errors of 2.1% and 4.0 degrees, respectively. We then develop a low-latency online method for table tennis as a case study in real-time applications. This method uses an uncertainty-aware convolutional neural network trained on pseudo-ground-truth spin labels from the offline approach, combined with a GPU-accelerated batch implementation of contrast maximization for refinement. We demonstrate reliable tracking and spin estimation with a three-view setup during professional table tennis matches, with high accuracy (8.8% magnitude and 6.4 degrees axis mismatch), 3 ms latency, and 750 Hz throughput.

中文摘要翻译: 旋转在许多球类运动中起着至关重要的作用，因为它会影响球的轨迹。在比赛过程中使用传统相机基于视觉估计球的旋转非常具有挑战性，因为球体积小、速度快且旋转快。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种基于事件的主动视觉系统，能够实时跟踪未修改的球并测量其旋转。该系统包括事件相机（因其高时间分辨率和极小的运动模糊）、高速俯仰/偏航振镜（使球保持在视野内）以及低延迟可调焦长焦镜头（提高球上的空间分辨率并保持对焦）。为了跟踪球，我们使用了一种混合方法，结合了用于对中的二维事件检测和用于重新初始化的球定位系统三维位置。对于高精度旋转估计，我们提出了一种离线方法，该方法在球面上执行对比度最大化（s-CMax）。该方法在多个运动项目（乒乓球、棒球、网球和高尔夫）的静态球上达到了最先进的精度，平均转速幅度误差为2.1%，轴误差为4.0°。然后，我们开发了一种用于乒乓球的低延迟在线方法，作为实时应用的案例研究。该方法使用不确定性感知卷积神经网络，该网络基于离线方法生成的伪地面真值旋转标签进行训练，并结合GPU加速的批处理对比度最大化实现进行精细化。我们通过三视角设置在专业乒乓球比赛中展示了可靠的跟踪和旋转估计，具有高精度（8.8%的幅度误差和6.4°的轴误差）、3毫秒延迟和750 Hz吞吐量。

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Aesthetics: Quantifying Information Loss in Turbid Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26295)
*Vasiliki Ismiroglou,Stefan H. Bengtson,Tasos Benos,Thomas B. Moeslund,Malte Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB)数据集和PCD指标，用于研究极端浑浊水下环境对计算机视觉模型的影响。PCD指标基于相位一致性图，与实例分割模型性能强相关，而传统指标几乎不相关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 水下浑浊环境会严重降低能见度，但计算机视觉模型在浑浊条件下的性能退化机制尚不明确；现有研究依赖合成浑浊数据集，无法真实反映实际信息损失。

Method: 构建TUB数据集（1,320张极端浑浊水下图像+16,000+高置信度真值分割掩码）；提出基于相位一致性图的PCD指标，该指标对对比度不敏感，用于量化真实浑浊环境中的结构信息损失。

Result: PCD指标与实例分割模型在真实和合成浑浊图像上的性能均呈强相关，而领域内常用指标几乎不相关。

Conclusion: PCD是比现有指标更有效的浑浊水下环境结构信息损失度量，能够可靠预测模型性能退化。

Original Abstract: Visibility in underwater environments degrades rapidly under turbid conditions, yet the effects on computer-vision models remain unclear. This issue is compounded by reliance on synthetic turbidity datasets, which may misrepresent real-world information loss. To address this gap, we introduce the Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB) dataset, comprising 1,320 images captured under extreme turbidity and over 16,000 high-confidence ground-truth segmentation masks. We additionally propose PCD, a metric derived from phase congruency maps that is invariant to contrast and aims to capture the loss of structural information in real turbidity. We show that PCD correlates strongly with the performance of instance segmentation models on both real and synthetic turbid images, whereas common metrics in the field show weak to no correlation at all. The dataset and relevant code can be found on the project page: https://vap.aau.dk/pcd

中文摘要翻译: 水下环境的能见度在浑浊条件下会迅速恶化，但其对计算机视觉模型的影响仍不清楚。这一问题因依赖合成浑浊数据集而进一步加剧，因为这些数据集可能无法真实反映实际的信息损失。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了浑浊水下基线（TUB）数据集，包含1,320张在极端浑浊条件下拍摄的图像以及超过16,000个高置信度的真实分割掩码。此外，我们提出了PCD指标，该指标基于相位一致性图推导而来，对对比度具有不变性，旨在捕捉真实浑浊环境中结构信息的损失。我们证明，PCD与实例分割模型在真实和合成浑浊图像上的性能均呈强相关，而该领域常用指标几乎不相关。数据集及相关代码可在项目页面获取：https://vap.aau.dk/pcd

</details>


### [12] [Pseudo-Text-Conditioned 3D Grounding DINO for Organ Localization in Abdominal CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27084)
*Siqi Chen,Han Gong,Keyi Hou,Jingxuan Yang,Sheethal Bhat,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CT-3GDINO，一种轻量级3D目标检测器，利用冻结的伪文本类标记替代真实文本编码器，实现腹部CT中5个器官的固定定位，在RSNA/RATIC数据集上达到0.5830 mAP，为伪文本条件3D器官定位建立了开源基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 腹部CT中可靠的器官定位能为下游创伤分析提供空间先验，现有方法缺乏轻量级且可泛化的3D固定器官检测方案。

Method: 基于Grounding-DINO风格的查询架构，使用冻结的伪文本类标记替代真实文本编码器，结合Swin3D视觉骨干网络、双向特征增强、伪文本引导的查询选择以及跨模态解码器，预测肝脏、脾脏、左肾、右肾和肠道五个器官的归一化3D边界框。

Result: 在193个RSNA/RATIC CT体素上训练和评估，最佳多尺度模型从头训练达到0.5830整体top-1类别平均mAP（IoU阈值0.1-0.7），IoU 0.1时AP达0.9649（粗定位强），IoU 0.7时AP仅0.1552（精确对齐有限）。

Conclusion: CT-3GDINO建立了伪文本条件3D器官定位的开源基线，性能在粗定位上表现强劲，但严格框对齐仍有局限，未来工作可聚焦于定位感知预训练、更丰富的多模态条件以及创伤聚焦检测。

Original Abstract: Reliable organ localization in abdominal CT can provide spatial priors for downstream trauma analysis. We propose CT-3GDINO, a lightweight 3D detector that adapts a Grounding-DINO-style query-based architecture to fixed organ localization using frozen pseudo-text class tokens instead of a real text encoder. The model combines a Swin3D visual backbone, bidirectional feature enhancement, pseudo-text-guided query selection, and a cross-modality decoder to predict normalized 3D boxes for liver, spleen, left kidney, right kidney, and bowel. We train and evaluate on 193 matched RSNA/RATIC CT volumes with segmentation-derived boxes. The best multi-scale model, trained from scratch, achieves 0.5830 overall top-1 class-wise mAP over 3D IoU thresholds from 0.1 to 0.7, outperforming fixed- and trainable-backbone classification-pretrained variants with 0.5570 and 0.4657 mAP. Performance is strong for coarse localization, with 0.9649 AP at IoU 0.1, but remains limited for strict box alignment, with 0.1552 AP at IoU 0.7. These results establish CT-3GDINO as an open-source baseline for pseudo-text-conditioned 3D organ localization and motivate future work on localization-aware pretraining, richer multimodal conditioning, and injury-focused detection.

中文摘要翻译: 腹部CT中可靠的器官定位可以为下游创伤分析提供空间先验。我们提出CT-3GDINO，一种轻量级3D目标检测器，它采用Grounding-DINO风格的基于查询的架构，并使用冻结的伪文本类标记替代真实文本编码器，从而适配到固定器官定位任务。该模型结合了Swin3D视觉骨干网络、双向特征增强、伪文本引导的查询选择以及跨模态解码器，用于预测肝脏、脾脏、左肾、右肾和肠道的归一化3D边界框。我们在193个配对的RSNA/RATIC CT体素上进行训练和评估，这些体素带有由分割导出的边界框。最佳的多尺度模型从头开始训练，在0.1到0.7的3D IoU阈值上达到0.5830的总体top-1类别平均mAP，优于固定骨干网络和可训练骨干网络的分类预训练变体（分别为0.5570和0.4657 mAP）。在粗定位上性能强劲，IoU为0.1时AP达到0.9649，但在严格框对齐方面仍然有限，IoU为0.7时AP仅为0.1552。这些结果使CT-3GDINO成为伪文本条件3D器官定位的开源基线，并激励了未来在定位感知预训练、更丰富的多模态条件以及创伤聚焦检测方面的研究。

</details>


### [13] [Layer-Specific Prompt Fusion Discovery via Differentiable Search in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26379)
*Xi Xiao,Xingjian Li,Yunbei Zhang,Cheng Han,Tianming Liu,Tianyang Wang,Runmin Jiang,Jihun Hamm,Xiao Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉提示微调中图像令牌与提示令牌的融合方式，提出通过可微架构搜索联合优化可学习提示及其融合方案，实验表明混合融合方案能更有效利用ViT层语义，在34个数据集上取得一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉提示微调方法普遍采用拼接或加法作为提示与图像令牌的融合方案，但单一融合方案是否最优、混合融合方案是否更有益这一基础问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 将任务建模为双层优化问题，利用可微架构搜索（DARTS）联合优化可学习提示及其融合方案。在搜索空间中引入仿射变换和交叉注意力两种新融合方案，与拼接和加法共同构成候选池。

Result: 在VTAB-1k、FGVC和HTA三个基准的34个数据集上，方法一致优于VPT-Deep及其变体，在准确率-延迟-参数量之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 提示与图像令牌的融合方式在视觉提示微调中至关重要，混合融合方案能更有效地利用ViT各层的语义信息，为视觉提示微调研究提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: Visual prompt tuning has emerged as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning approach for adapting large-scale Vision Transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks. As its learnable prompts are applied in input and feature spaces, prior to jointly going through attention in transformer layers, the most commonly used scheme for fusing image and prompt tokens is concatenation or addition. In this paper, we aim to study a fundamental yet essential problem in visual prompt tuning: whether a single fusion scheme tends to yield better results, and whether that would be beneficial to develop a hybrid fusion scheme. To this end, we formulate the task as a bi-level optimization problem, and solve it leveraging differentiable architecture search. In this context, the learnable prompts and their fusion schemes are jointly optimized. To enrich the search space in the architecture search, we propose two additional fusion schemes, namely, affine transformation and cross-attention, in addition to concatenation and addition. Extensive experiments on 34 datasets spanning VTAB-1k, FGVC, and HTA show consistent gains over prompt-tuning baselines. With a frozen ViT backbone, our method delivers a favorable accuracy--latency--parameter trade-off compared with VPT-Deep and recent variants. Our findings reveal that how prompts fuse with image tokens plays a significant role in visual prompt tuning, and a hybrid fusion fashion can more effectively leverage layer semantics of ViTs, contributing a novel perspective for visual prompt-tuning research.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉提示微调已成为将大规模视觉Transformer（ViT）适配到下游任务的一种参数高效微调方法。其可学习提示应用于输入和特征空间，在联合通过Transformer层的注意力机制之前，最常用的图像令牌与提示令牌融合方案是拼接或加法。本文旨在研究视觉提示微调中一个基础但关键的问题：单一融合方案是否倾向于产生更好结果，以及开发混合融合方案是否有益。为此，我们将该任务建模为双层优化问题，并利用可微架构搜索进行求解。在此框架下，可学习提示及其融合方案得以联合优化。为丰富架构搜索的搜索空间，我们在拼接和加法之外，提出了两种额外融合方案：仿射变换和交叉注意力。跨越VTAB-1k、FGVC和HTA三个基准的34个数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法相较于提示微调基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。在冻结ViT骨干网络的情况下，与VPT-Deep及其近期变体相比，我们的方法在准确率-延迟-参数量之间实现了更优的权衡。研究结果揭示，提示与图像令牌的融合方式在视觉提示微调中起着重要作用，且混合融合方式能够更有效地利用ViT的层语义，为视觉提示微调研究贡献了一个新颖视角。

</details>


### [14] [What Do Deepfake Benchmarks Measure? An Audit Using Frozen Self-Supervised Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26384)
*Samuel Pagon,Yixuan Shen,Vishal Asnani,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探测（Linear Probe）方法审计了视频、图像和音频三个模态的深度伪造检测基准，发现通用自监督表示能够接近专用检测器的性能，表明现有基准更多衡量的是通用模态理解能力而非真正的取证理解能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在基准测试中表现良好，但在现实场景中频繁失败。这种反馈循环导致检测器越来越复杂，但若基准本身不反映真实世界的深度伪造，这种复杂性可能是在解决错误的问题。因此，需要先回答：这些基准到底在测量什么？

Method: 使用一种刻意简化的诊断方法：在冻结的通用自监督表示上训练线性探针（Linear Probe），将其性能与专用深度伪造检测器进行比较。如果线性探针能够接近专用检测器的性能，则表明基准主要奖励的是通用模态理解而非取证理解。同时分析了生成器级难度与Frechet几何的关系。

Result: 在视频、图像和音频三个模态上，通用自监督表示上的线性探针性能均接近专用检测器。生成器级别的难度部分可由同一表示空间中的Frechet几何解释。

Conclusion: 提出了深度伪造检测的基准审计视角：在将高分数解读为取证理解能力的证据之前，有必要先问基准中有多少部分已被通用表示解决。

Original Abstract: As deepfake generators approach perceptual indistinguishability, reliable detection becomes critical. Yet, detectors that score well on benchmarks routinely fail in the wild. A concerning feedback loop has emerged: benchmarks drive increasingly complex, engineered detectors, yet if those benchmarks do not reflect real-world deepfakes, this complexity may be solving the wrong problem entirely. This raises a prior question: what are these benchmarks actually measuring? We conduct an audit of video, image, and audio deepfake benchmarks using a deliberately simple diagnostic. If a linear probe on frozen, general-purpose self-supervised representations can approximate the performance of a bespoke detector, the benchmark is largely rewarding general modality understanding rather than forensic understanding. This has two implications: the benchmark may not reflect realistic threat models, and it raises the question of whether the bespoke detectors the probe approaches are truly learning forensic understanding. We observe, across three modalities, linear probes on general-purpose self-supervised representations closely approach the performance of bespoke detectors. We further show that generator-level difficulty is partly explained by Frechet geometry in the same representation space. Together, these results support a benchmark-audit view of deepfake detection: before high scores are read as evidence of forensic understanding, it is worth asking how much of the benchmark is already solved by general-purpose representations.

中文摘要翻译: 随着深度伪造生成器趋于感知上的不可区分性，可靠的检测变得至关重要。然而，在基准测试上得分很高的检测器在现实场景中却屡屡失败。一个令人担忧的反馈循环已经出现：基准推动着越来越复杂的、精心设计的检测器，但如果这些基准不能反映真实的深度伪造，这种复杂性可能完全是在解决错误的问题。这引出了一个前置问题：这些基准实际上在衡量什么？我们使用一种刻意简化的诊断方法对视频、图像和音频深度伪造基准进行了审计。如果在冻结的通用自监督表示上的线性探针能够接近专用检测器的性能，则该基准在很大程度上奖励的是通用模态理解能力而非取证理解能力。这有两个含义：该基准可能无法反映现实威胁模型，并且引发了一个问题——线性探针所逼近的专用检测器是否真正学习了取证理解。我们观察到，在三个模态上，通用自监督表示上的线性探针性能均接近专用检测器。我们进一步表明，生成器级别的难度部分可由同一表示空间中的Frechet几何来解释。综合来看，这些结果支持深度伪造检测的基准审计视角：在将高分解读为取证理解能力的证据之前，有必要先问一下基准中有多少部分已经被通用表示解决。

</details>


### [15] [Staying VIGILant: Mitigating Visual Laziness via Counterfactual Visual Alignment in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26387)
*Xi Xiao,Chen Liu,Chih-Ting Liao,Yunbei Zhang,Qizhen Lan,Yuxiang Wei,Lin Zhao,Janet Wang,Jianyang Gu,Muchao Ye,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIGIL框架，通过强化学习后训练引入几何约束最大化视觉输入与生成文本之间的互信息，以解决多模态大语言模型的视觉懒惰问题，减少幻觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽然继承了强大的推理能力，但容易产生与视觉输入矛盾的幻觉。机制研究表明这是由于"视觉懒惰"：模型内部编码了正确的视觉证据，却在生成时过度依赖语言先验。现有对齐方法（如DPO）主要基于文本优化结果级奖励，偏向语言捷径，导致响应常与视觉证据矛盾。

Method: 提出VIGIL（Visual Information Gain In aLignment）框架，是一种基于强化学习的后训练对齐方法。核心是引入几何约束，显式最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息。具体通过惩罚"盲目自信"实例来实现——在掩码文本-视觉注意力以创建反事实盲目状态时，模型仍保持不当确定性。

Result: 在幻觉和推理基准上持续优于最近的对齐方法；仅使用25%的偏好数据即达到全量数据的最先进方法性能；在无显式边界框监督下展现出涌现的空间定位能力；不牺牲纯文本能力。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过将优化焦点从数值奖励拟合转向因果视觉基础，有效缓解了MLLMs的视觉懒惰和幻觉问题，并以更少数据实现更优性能，同时带来空间定位能力的涌现。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend large language models (LLMs) with visual perception, enabling joint reasoning over images and text. Despite inheriting strong reasoning capabilities from LLMs, they remain prone to hallucinations that contradict their visual inputs. Mechanistic studies indicate that this weakness stems from visual laziness: MLLMs encode the correct visual evidence internally, but overly rely on strong language priors during response. Existing alignment methods, such as direct preference optimization, primarily optimize outcome-level rewards based on text. This introduces an optimization bias toward linguistic shortcuts, leading to responses that often contradict the visual evidence. To address this, we propose Visual Information Gain In aLignment (VIGIL), a reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training framework that shifts the focus from numerical reward fitting to causal visual grounding. VIGIL introduces a geometric constraint that explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the visual input and the generated response. We achieve this by penalizing "blind confidence" instances where the model remains improperly certain even when textual-visual attention is masked to create a counterfactual blind state. Extensive experiments show that VIGIL consistently outperforms recent alignment methods across hallucination and reasoning benchmarks without compromising text-only capabilities. Our approach matches the full-data performance of state-of-the-art methods using only 25% of the preference data and even demonstrates emergent spatial grounding capabilities without explicit bounding box supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过视觉感知扩展了大语言模型（LLMs），实现了对图像和文本的联合推理。尽管继承了LLMs强大的推理能力，它们仍然容易产生与视觉输入相矛盾的幻觉。机制研究表明，这一弱点源于视觉懒惰：MLLMs在内部编码了正确的视觉证据，但在生成响应时过度依赖强语言先验。现有的对齐方法（如直接偏好优化）主要基于文本优化结果级奖励，这引入了对语言捷径的优化偏差，导致响应常常与视觉证据矛盾。为解决这一问题，我们提出VIGIL（Visual Information Gain In aLignment），一种基于强化学习（RL）的后训练框架，将焦点从数值奖励拟合转向因果视觉基础。VIGIL引入了几何约束，显式最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息。我们通过惩罚"盲目自信"实例来实现这一点——即当文本-视觉注意力被掩码以创建反事实盲目状态时，模型仍保持不当确定性的情况。大量实验表明，VIGIL在不牺牲纯文本能力的前提下，在幻觉和推理基准上持续优于最近的对齐方法。我们的方法仅使用25%的偏好数据即达到最先进方法的全数据性能，并且在没有显式边界框监督的情况下，展现出涌现的空间定位能力。

</details>


### [16] [DinoLink: A Token-Centric Representation Compression Framework for Bandwidth-Constrained Collaborative V2X Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26398)
*Tianle Zhu,Haohua Que,Handong Yao,Hongyi Xu,Zhipeng Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DinoLink 是一个基于 token 压缩的语义通信框架，通过双稀疏架构（显著性感知选择器 + 残差向量量化）大幅减少 V2X 网络中的传输数据量，实现 139 倍比特率压缩，同时在 nuScenes 上保持 32.8% mAP，在 LoRa 等窄带环境下获得 34.5 倍加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: V2X 网络带宽严重受限，直接传输原始像素流进行远程高精度感知难以实现，亟需一种带宽高效的通信方案。

Method: 提出 DinoLink 框架，采用双稀疏架构：(1) 显著性感知选择器剪枝冗余背景 token；(2) 残差向量量化模块将特征压缩为紧凑的码本索引。仅传输轻量级索引和位置先验信息，实现 token 级离散语义通信。

Result: 在 nuScenes 数据集上，相比未压缩传输实现 139 倍比特率降低，同时保持 32.8% mAP；在 LoRa 等窄带环境下实现 34.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: DinoLink 为受限 V2X 场景下的高保真远程感知提供了一种鲁棒且带宽高效的前端解决方案。

Original Abstract: High-precision remote perception is often hindered by the severe bandwidth constraints of Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) networks. We propose \textit{DinoLink}, a token-centric compression framework that replaces raw pixel streaming with discrete semantic communication for vehicle-cloud collaborative inference. DinoLink employs a dual-sparsity architecture: a saliency-aware selector prunes redundant background tokens, while a Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ) module collapses features into compact codebook indices. By transmitting only lightweight indices and positional priors, DinoLink achieves a $139\times$ bitrate reduction compared to uncompressed transmission while maintaining a competitive 32.8\% mAP on the nuScenes dataset. Deployment simulations further demonstrate a $34.5\times$ acceleration in narrow-band environments, such as LoRa. Our results substantiate DinoLink as a robust, bandwidth-efficient frontend for high-fidelity remote perception in constrained V2X scenarios. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/UGA-MOBILITY-LAB/dino_link.

中文摘要翻译: 高精度远程感知常常受到车联网（V2X）网络严重带宽限制的阻碍。本文提出 DinoLink，一种基于 token 压缩的框架，它将原始像素流替换为离散语义通信，用于车-云协同推理。DinoLink 采用双稀疏架构：一个显著性感知选择器用于剪枝冗余背景 token，同时一个残差向量量化模块将特征压缩为紧凑的码本索引。通过仅传输轻量级索引和位置先验，DinoLink 相比未压缩传输实现了 139 倍的比特率降低，同时在 nuScenes 数据集上保持了具有竞争力的 32.8% mAP。部署仿真进一步表明，在 LoRa 等窄带环境中可获得 34.5 倍的加速。我们的结果证实，DinoLink 是一种在受限 V2X 场景下实现高保真远程感知的鲁棒且带宽高效的前端方案。代码已开源：https://github.com/UGA-MOBILITY-LAB/dino_link。

</details>


### [17] [Neural Voxel Dynamics: Learning Implicit 3D Physics via Volumetric Feature Advection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26410)
*Zican Wang,Niloy Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督框架，通过将2D视频特征提升到3D体素潜在空间，隐式学习物理动力学，无需显式物理引擎监督即可模拟刚体运动、流体等 heterogeneous 现象。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成式视频模型虽有高视觉保真度，但缺乏3D几何基础，导致物理不一致性和物体永久性保持失败。

Method: 从V-JEPA提取语义特征，利用单目深度先验将其反投影到体素化网格中，形成3D体素潜在空间；在该空间中进行体积特征平流，学习以动作为条件的转移算子，将物理视为时空状态平流问题。

Result: 在CLEVERER、PhysInOne、PhysGaia等多个基准上展现出良好的长期结构稳定性和物理合理性。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了一条可扩展的路径，使模型仅通过被动观察单目视频就能内化物理世界的3D不变性，迈向通用的动态世界模型。

Original Abstract: We present a self-supervised framework for learning implicit 3D physical dynamics directly from video-derived supervisory signals. While current generative video models achieve high visual fidelity, they lack a 3D geometric foundation, often resulting in physical inconsistencies and a failure to maintain object permanence. We address this by shifting the predictive bottleneck from 2D image space to a `lifted' 3D Volumetric Latent Space. Our method unprojects semantic features from a Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) into a voxelized grid, grounded by monocular depth priors. This lifting enables a Volumetric Feature Advection to learn an action-conditioned transition operator that treats physics as a spatio-temporal state advection problem, i.e., learn implicit 3D physics. Unlike state-of-the-art hybrid models that rely on explicit classical simulators for training and/or inference, our architecture tracks material states implicitly within high-dimensional V-JEPA features. This allows for the emergent simulation of heterogeneous phenomena (e.g., rigid body motion in fluid flow) within a single, unified pipeline. Supervised solely via end-to-end video-derived signal plus action conditions, without access to physics engine internal states, labels, or surrogate models, our model demonstrates good long-term structural stability and physical plausibility on multiple benchmarks (CLEVERER, PhysInOne, PhysGaia). We believe that this work opens a scalable pathway toward general-purpose dynamic world models that internalize the 3D invariants of the physical world solely through passive observation of monocular videos.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个自监督框架，直接从视频衍生的监督信号中学习隐式三维物理动力学。虽然当前的生成式视频模型实现了高视觉保真度，但它们缺乏三维几何基础，常常导致物理不一致性和物体永久性保持的失败。我们通过将预测瓶颈从二维图像空间转移到"提升的"三维体素潜在空间来解决这一问题。我们的方法将来自视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）的语义特征反投影到由单目深度先验支撑的体素化网格中。这种提升使得体积特征平流能够学习一个以动作为条件的转移算子，将物理视为时空状态平流问题，即学习隐式三维物理。与依赖显式经典模拟器进行训练和/或推理的最先进混合模型不同，我们的架构在高维V-JEPA特征中隐式地追踪材料状态。这使得在单一统一流程中能够涌现地模拟异质现象（例如流体中的刚体运动）。仅通过端到端的视频衍生信号和动作条件进行监督，无需访问物理引擎内部状态、标签或代理模型，我们的模型在多个基准（CLEVERER、PhysInOne、PhysGaia）上展现出良好的长期结构稳定性和物理合理性。我们相信，这项工作开辟了一条可扩展的路径，通往通用的动态世界模型，使其仅通过被动观察单目视频就能内化物理世界的三维不变性。

</details>


### [18] [Methane-Plume Segmentation From Hyperspectral Satellite Imagery Via Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26416)
*Brayan Quintero,Jeferson Acevedo,Samuel Traslaviña,Hoover Rueda-Chacón*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合物理特征引导的甲烷增强机制的多模态深度学习模型，在MPDataset上以更低计算成本取得优于现有方法的甲烷羽流分割性能（MIoU +0.92，MPrecision +0.87，Recall +1.01），实现精度与效率的平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 准确检测和分割地球观测图像中的甲烷羽流对大规模温室气体监测至关重要，但现有方法在精度与计算效率之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出一种多模态深度学习模型，核心是特征引导的甲烷增强（FGME）机制，该机制将具有物理意义的甲烷线索注入到基于Transformer的RGB表示中，并在多个语义尺度上进行融合。

Result: 在MPDataset上，该方法在MIoU、MPrecision和Recall上分别提升+0.92、+0.87和+1.01，同时计算成本显著低于其他高性能架构。

Conclusion: 高效的多模态融合策略在真实遥感应用中实现准确且可扩展的甲烷羽流分割具有巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: Efficient detection of methane plumes is crucial for understanding and mitigating global warming, as accurately identifying and segmenting them in earth observation imagery remain essential for large-scale monitoring. In this work, we propose a multimodal deep learning model that integrates a feature-guided methane enhancement (FGME) mechanism which injects physically meaningful methane cues into transformer-based RGB representations at multiple semantic scales. Our method is evaluated on the MPDataset, where it outperforms the state-of-the-art with improvements of +0.92 in MIoU, +0.87 in MPrecision and +1.01 in Recall. Notably, these gains are obtained with a substantially lower computational cost than other high-performing architectures, resulting in a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for large-scale methane monitoring. These results highlight the potential of efficient multimodal fusion strategies for accurate and scalable methane plume segmentation in real-world remote sensing applications.

中文摘要翻译: 高效检测甲烷羽流对于理解和减缓全球变暖至关重要，因为在地球观测图像中准确识别和分割甲烷羽流仍是大规模监测的关键。本文提出一种多模态深度学习模型，该模型集成了特征引导的甲烷增强（FGME）机制，该机制将具有物理意义的甲烷线索在多个语义尺度上注入基于Transformer的RGB表示中。我们的方法在MPDataset上进行了评估，在MIoU、MPrecision和Recall上分别比现有最优方法提升了+0.92、+0.87和+1.01。值得注意的是，这些提升是在计算成本远低于其他高性能架构的情况下获得的，从而在大规模甲烷监测中实现了良好的精度-效率权衡。这些结果凸显了高效多模态融合策略在真实遥感应用中实现准确且可扩展的甲烷羽流分割的潜力。

</details>


### [19] [Active Adversarial Perturbation-driven Associative Memory Retrieval for RGB-Event Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26455)
*Xiao Wang,Xufeng Lou,Zikang Yan,Lan Chen,Sibao Chen,Yaowei Wang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出APRTrack，一种针对RGB-Event多模态跟踪的层次化扰动与检索框架，通过模态级和空间级对抗扰动模拟真实信号退化，并设计分层路由机制避免特征坍塌，同时利用足迹引导的通道校准Hopfield检索模块实现可靠的历史信息补偿，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实场景中RGB-Event多模态跟踪面临两种关键挑战：1) 恶劣环境下某模态可能完全失效（模态级退化）；2) 目标因遮挡、边缘截断或前景杂乱而出现局部缺失（空间级退化）。现有融合方法难以应对这些结构化信号退化问题。

Method: 1) 层次化扰动框架：构建模态级和空间级两个对抗扰动分支，分别模拟全模态失效和局部目标缺失；2) 层次化路由机制：解耦两种扰动类型的训练流程，消除叠加退化约束导致的特征坍塌；3) 足迹引导的通道校准Hopfield检索模块：基于查询与记忆库之间的关联足迹评估检索置信度，在Hopfield匹配前校准检索度量空间，实现可控的历史特征补偿。

Result: 在FE108、COESOT、VisEvent和FELT四个数据集上的大量实验证明了所提策略在RGB-Event视觉目标跟踪中的有效性。

Conclusion: APRTrack通过层次化扰动与检索框架有效解决了RGB-Event跟踪中目标部分缺失和模态退化两大挑战，显著提升了复杂环境下的跟踪鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: RGB-Event tracking improves localization robustness by fusing RGB appearance textures and dense temporal motion cues from event sensors. While this multi-modal scheme broadens tracking applicability, real-world scenes suffer diverse structured signal degradations that hinder traditional multi-modal fusion. In harsh environments, either modality can lose reliability drastically, and targets frequently appear incomplete due to occlusion, edge truncation and foreground clutter.To tackle the above challenges, we present a hierarchical perturbation and retrieval framework tailored for RGB-Event tracking with robustness against partial target missing and modal degradation, termed APRTrack. To mimic real-world signal corruption, APRTrack constructs structured degradation via two adversarial perturbation branches at the modality and spatial levels, which separately simulate full-modal failure and localized target region absence. A hierarchical routing mechanism is designed to disentangle the training pipelines of the two perturbation types, effectively eliminating feature collapse induced by superimposed degradation constraints. Furthermore, we devise Footprint-guided Channel-calibrated Hopfield Retrieval (FCHR) for reliable historical information compensation. This module evaluates retrieval confidence based on association footprints between queries and memory banks, and calibrates the retrieval metric space prior to Hopfield matching, realizing controllable historical feature compensation bounded to target regions. Extensive experiments on FE108, COESOT, VisEvent, and FELT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed strategies for the RGB-Event visual object tracking. The source code and pre-trained models will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenEvTracking

中文摘要翻译: RGB-Event跟踪通过融合RGB外观纹理和来自事件传感器的密集时间运动线索，提高了定位鲁棒性。虽然这种多模态方案拓宽了跟踪的适用性，但真实场景中存在的多样化结构化信号退化阻碍了传统的多模态融合。在恶劣环境下，任一模态都可能急剧丧失可靠性，且目标常因遮挡、边缘截断和前景杂乱而出现不完整。为应对上述挑战，我们提出了一种专为RGB-Event跟踪设计的层次化扰动与检索框架——APRTrack，该框架对目标部分缺失和模态退化具有鲁棒性。为模拟真实信号损坏，APRTrack通过模态级和空间级两个对抗扰动分支构建结构化退化，分别模拟全模态失效和局部目标区域缺失。设计了一种层次化路由机制来解耦两种扰动类型的训练流程，有效消除了叠加退化约束导致的特征坍塌。此外，我们设计了足迹引导的通道校准Hopfield检索模块（FCHR）用于可靠的历史信息补偿。该模块基于查询与记忆库之间的关联足迹评估检索置信度，并在Hopfield匹配之前校准检索度量空间，实现了受限目标区域的可控历史特征补偿。在FE108、COESOT、VisEvent和FELT数据集上的大量实验证明了我们提出的策略在RGB-Event视觉目标跟踪中的有效性。源代码和预训练模型将在https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenEvTracking上发布。

</details>


### [20] [Forget, Anticipate and Adapt: Test Time Training for Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26515)
*Rajat Modi,Sebastian Noel,Xin Liang,Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出帧遗忘网络（FFN），解决测试时训练（TTT）在长视频场景下的计算效率问题。FFN仅使用滑动窗口内的三帧（退出帧、当前帧、下一帧），并定义“惊喜度”指标衡量帧间新信息量，实现自适应窗口调整。在长达3小时的EpicTours数据集上，FFN在密集分割、视频分类等任务上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TTT方法在长视频场景存在两个问题：1）基于滑动窗口的TTT更新计算量随窗口大小线性增长，处理数小时视频时计算不可行；2）对时序上相似帧也执行TTT更新，浪费大量计算资源。因此需要一种高效、可扩展的TTT方法。

Method: 1）帧遗忘网络（FFN）：滑动窗口内仅保留三帧——退出帧、当前帧和下一帧，通过这种稀疏帧策略保持时序上下文，使模型可处理数小时长视频；2）定义数学上的“惊喜度”指标，量化新帧相对于历史帧包含的新信息量，以此为核心设计自适应窗口调整算法，动态决定何时执行TTT更新及窗口大小。

Result: 在自主构建的EpicTours数据集（长达3小时的城市导览视频）上验证了FFN的有效性，在密集分割、视频分类任务上表现优异，并展示了模型在深度估计任务上的泛化能力，以及处理多小时的超长视频的能力。

Conclusion: FFN通过仅使用三帧和自适应窗口机制，显著降低了长视频TTT的计算开销，同时保持了时序上下文建模能力，为长视频场景下的高效测试时自适应提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Test Time Training (TTT) is a mechanism in which a model adapts to an incoming test-sample by performing some self-supervised (SSL) task and updating its weights even during inference. This procedure does not require labels at test-time. This paper focuses on TTT for long-videos. A major concern with existing approaches is: 1) they perform TTT updates using a sliding window containing frames in the past, whose compute increases linearly with the size of window. This becomes computationally intractable when the videos are hours long. 2) TTT is performed even when temporally close frames look similar, thereby consuming a lot of compute.
  We present the Frame Forgetting Network (FFN) that: 1) operates on only three frames within the sliding window, namely the frame that exits, the current frame and the frame after that. The model still manages to retain temporal context and work for hours long-videos; 2) mathematically define a surprise metric: how much new information the incoming frame contains with respect to the past seen frame. This facilitates determining how to modify the effective window size during TTT and constitutes the core mechanism of an adaptive windowing algorithm. Additionally, we curate a dataset EpicTours containing up to 3 hour long videos of walking city-tours, whereas earlier datasets on this problem were only 5 min long. We demonstrate FFNs empirical effectiveness on dense-segmentation, video classification tasks, generalization to depth-estimation, and multi-hour long videos.

中文摘要翻译: 测试时训练（TTT）是一种机制，模型通过执行某种自监督（SSL）任务并在推理过程中更新权重，从而自适应地处理输入的测试样本。该过程在测试时不需要标签。本文聚焦于长视频场景下的TTT。现有方法的主要问题在于：1）它们使用包含过去帧的滑动窗口执行TTT更新，其计算量随窗口大小线性增长。当视频长达数小时时，这在计算上变得不可行。2）即使时序上相近的帧看起来相似，也执行TTT更新，从而消耗大量计算资源。
我们提出帧遗忘网络（FFN），其特点是：1）仅对滑动窗口内的三帧进行操作，即退出帧、当前帧和下一帧。该模型仍然能够保留时序上下文，并适用于数小时长的视频；2）从数学上定义了一个惊喜度指标：新帧相对于已见过的历史帧包含多少新信息。这有助于确定在TTT过程中如何调整有效窗口大小，并构成了自适应窗口算法的核心机制。此外，我们构建了一个名为EpicTours的数据集，包含长达3小时的城市导览行走视频，而此前该问题的数据集只有5分钟长。我们通过密集分割、视频分类任务、对深度估计的泛化能力以及多小时的超长视频处理，实证展示了FFN的有效性。

</details>


### [21] [From Hallucination to Grounding: Diagnosing Visual Spatial Intelligence via CRISP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26535)
*Zhixing Li,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRISP是一个用于评估VLM空间智能的结构化诊断范式，通过一致性（隐式感知与显式推理的对齐）来解耦语言先验与真实空间推理能力，揭示了闭源模型存在感知-推理脱节而开源模型缺乏多跳组合推理的根本瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的VLM评估常将语言先验与真正的空间推理混为一谈，无法准确诊断模型的空间智能缺陷。

Method: 提出CRISP评估范式，利用度量3D场景图和Oracle干预协议，通过一致性（隐式感知与显式推理的对齐）来解耦潜在推理能力与感知瓶颈，实现结构化诊断。

Result: 1) 闭源模型拥有强大的潜在推理引擎，但存在度量估计不准确和未能利用隐式结构表征的关键缺陷；2) 开源模型则根本受限于缺乏多跳组合推理能力。

Conclusion: CRISP将评估重点从通过语言先验"猜对"转向真正的"感知-验证-推理"，为超越端到端后训练的多模态对齐提供了严谨的路线图。

Original Abstract: Current VLM evaluations often conflate language priors with genuine spatial reasoning. To address this, we introduce CRISP, a novel structural-diagnostic evaluation paradigm that assesses visual spatial intelligence through consistency, the alignment between implicit perception and explicit reasoning. Unlike traditional black-box QA, CRISP utilizes metric 3D Scene Graphs and an oracle intervention protocol to decouple latent reasoning capabilities from perceptual bottlenecks. This granular diagnosis uncovers a systematic perception-reasoning disconnect. Crucially, we reveal that while proprietary models possess robust latent reasoning engines, they suffer from inaccurate metric estimation and a critical failure to leverage their implicit structural representations. Conversely, open-source models remain fundamentally bottlenecked by their lack of multi-hop compositional reasoning. By shifting the focus from merely ``guessing correctly'' via language priors to genuinely ``perceiving, verifying, and reasoning,'' CRISP offers a rigorous roadmap for multimodal alignment beyond end-to-end post-training. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/iiyamayuki/CRISP-Bench.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的视觉语言模型评估常常将语言先验与真正的空间推理混为一谈。为解决这一问题，我们提出了CRISP，一种新颖的结构化诊断评估范式，通过一致性（即隐式感知与显式推理之间的对齐）来评估视觉空间智能。与传统的黑盒问答不同，CRISP利用度量3D场景图和Oracle干预协议，将潜在推理能力与感知瓶颈解耦。这种细粒度诊断揭示了系统性的感知-推理脱节。关键在于，我们发现虽然闭源模型拥有强大的潜在推理引擎，但它们存在度量估计不准确以及未能利用其隐式结构表征的关键缺陷。相反，开源模型则根本受限于缺乏多跳组合推理能力。通过将焦点从通过语言先验"猜对"转向真正的"感知、验证与推理"，CRISP为超越端到端后训练的多模态对齐提供了严谨的路线图。代码和数据集可在 https://github.com/iiyamayuki/CRISP-Bench 获取。

</details>


### [22] [PhyEditBench: A Real-World Multi-Stage Benchmark for Physics-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26551)
*Shengbin Guo,Shaokang He,Chaoyue Meng,Shengpeng Xiao,Xunzhi Xiang,Shaofeng Zhang,Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyEditBench基准，用于评估图像编辑模型的物理理解能力，并提出了训练无关的基线方法PhyWorld，利用视频生成过程作为推理机制来提升物理感知编辑效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准缺乏对基于物理推理能力的全面评估，而该能力对于处理真实世界场景至关重要。

Method: 构建了包含4个主类、12个子类的分层分类体系，从视频中提取238个高质量真实世界实例和35个合成反物理实例。提出训练无关的基线PhyWorld，采用测试时缩放和潜在降维策略，利用视频生成过程作为图像编辑的推理机制。

Result: 对当前SOTA编辑方法的实证分析揭示了它们在物理推理方面的显著局限性；PhyWorld优于可比模型。

Conclusion: 视频生成过程可以有效地作为图像编辑的推理机制，且所提出的基准和基线方法为提升物理感知编辑能力提供了有效途径。

Original Abstract: While instruction-based image editing, enabled by multi-modal generative models, has advanced significantly, existing benchmarks lack a comprehensive evaluation of physics-based reasoning, a critical capability for handling real-world scenarios. To address this, we introduce PhyEditBench, a benchmark designed to assess the physical understanding of editing models. Guided by a hierarchical taxonomy, we establish 4 primary classes and 12 subclasses. It comprises 238 high-quality, high-resolution, real-world instances meticulously extracted from videos to capture authentic physical dynamics, alongside 35 synthetic Anti-Physics instances. Our empirical analysis of current SOTA editing methods exposes substantial limitations in their physics-based reasoning. We further propose a training-free baseline named PhyWorld that uses test-time scaling and a latent reduction strategy. PhyWorld outperforms comparable models and suggests that the video generation process can effectively serve as a reasoning mechanism for image editing. The project page is available at https://github.com/Previsior/PhyEditBench.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然基于多模态生成模型的指令式图像编辑已取得显著进展，但现有基准缺乏对基于物理推理能力的全面评估，而这一能力对于处理真实世界场景至关重要。为此，我们引入了PhyEditBench——一个旨在评估编辑模型物理理解能力的基准。在分层分类体系的指导下，我们建立了4个主类和12个子类。该基准包含从视频中精心提取的238个高质量、高分辨率真实世界实例，以捕捉真实的物理动态，以及35个合成反物理实例。我们对当前最先进编辑方法的实证分析揭示了它们在物理推理方面的显著局限性。我们进一步提出了一个训练无关的基线方法PhyWorld，该方法采用测试时缩放和潜在降维策略。PhyWorld优于可比模型，并表明视频生成过程可以有效地作为图像编辑的推理机制。项目页面见https://github.com/Previsior/PhyEditBench。

</details>


### [23] [Perception, Verdict, and Evolution: Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining Forensics Agent for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26552)
*Yangjun Wu,Keyu Yan,Yu Liu,Jingren Zhou,Fei Huang,Rong Zhang,Zhou Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeAgent，一种面向AI生成图像检测的智能体取证框架，通过感知-判决架构聚合多视图线索，并采用事后驱动自我精进策略实现持续自我进化，在多个基准上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的深度伪造检测方法存在两个关键局限：对细粒度取证伪影的敏感性不足，以及依赖前沿模型的静态合成监督，导致灵活性和成本受限。

Method: ForeAgent采用两种核心机制：1）感知-判决（Perception-Verdict）架构：聚合语义、空间和频域特征的多视图线索，利用MLLM作为判决模块进行逻辑融合；2）事后驱动自我精进（Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining）策略：遵循采样-反思-进化（Sampling-Reflection-Evolution）范式，通过真实标签作为事后指导，对失败案例和低质量推理轨迹进行反思并重构高质量推理样本，经双专家质量门控模块严格过滤后，通过微调实现持续进化。

Result: 1）在Chameleon基准上达到82.18%准确率（比AIDE提升16.41%）；2）在AIGCDetect-Benchmark上跨16个生成器平均准确率达93.3%；3）外部评估表明，ForeAgent的推理比GPT-5和GPT-5-mini更一致且因果更合理。

Conclusion: ForeAgent通过智能体化的迭代自我进化框架，有效解决了现有MLLM方法对细粒度取证伪影不敏感和依赖静态监督的局限，在AI生成图像检测任务上实现了最先进性能。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models presents a significant challenge to existing deepfake detection methods, particularly given the widespread dissemination of highly realistic AI-generated images. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong potential for this task, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: insufficient sensitivity to fine-grained forensic artifacts and reliance on static synthetic supervision from frontier models, leading to limited flexibility and high-cost. To address these issues, we propose ForeAgent, an agentic forensics framework for AI-generated image detection with iterative self-evolution. First, ForeAgent adopts a Perception-Verdict architecture that aggregates multi-view cues spanning semantic, spatial, and frequency-domain features, and leverages an MLLM as a verdict module to fuse these signals for a logical-grounded verdict. Second, to enable continual self-improvement, we introduce a Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining strategy following a Sampling-Reflection-Evolution paradigm. The agent performs inference rollouts on training instances. Guided by ground-truth labels as hindsight, it reflects on failure cases and low-quality reasoning trajectories to regenerate higher-quality reasoning traces. These synthesized samples are then strictly filtered through a dual-expert quality gating module. ForeAgent continuously evolves via fine-tuning on self-curated high-quality samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ForeAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Chameleon benchmark, reaching 82.18% accuracy (+16.41% over AIDE), and achieves 93.3% mean accuracy on AIGCDetect-Benchmark across 16 generators. In addition, external evaluation shows that ForeAgent produces more consistent and causally grounded reasoning compared to GPT-5 and GPT-5-mini.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型的快速发展对现有深度伪造检测方法构成了重大挑战，尤其是在高度逼真的AI生成图像广泛传播的背景下。尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在此任务上展现出强大潜力，现有方法仍存在两个关键局限性：对细粒度取证伪影的敏感性不足，以及依赖前沿模型的静态合成监督，导致灵活性有限且成本高昂。为解决这些问题，本文提出ForeAgent——一种面向AI生成图像检测的、具备迭代自我进化能力的智能体取证框架。首先，ForeAgent采用感知-判决（Perception-Verdict）架构，聚合涵盖语义、空间和频域特征的多视图线索，并利用MLLM作为判决模块融合这些信号以形成逻辑可支撑的判决。其次，为实现持续的自我改进，我们引入了一种事后驱动自我精进（Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining）策略，遵循采样-反思-进化（Sampling-Reflection-Evolution）范式。该智能体在训练实例上执行推理展开，以真实标签作为事后指导，对失败案例和低质量推理轨迹进行反思，从而重构更高质量的推理样本。这些合成样本随后通过双专家质量门控模块进行严格筛选。ForeAgent通过在自我精选的高质量样本上进行微调来持续进化。大量实验表明，ForeAgent在Chameleon基准上取得了最先进的性能，准确率达到82.18%（较AIDE提升16.41%），并在AIGCDetect-Benchmark上跨16个生成器实现了93.3%的平均准确率。此外，外部评估显示，与GPT-5和GPT-5-mini相比，ForeAgent产生的推理更加一致且更具因果可解释性。

</details>


### [24] [Coarse-to-Fine: A Hybrid Self-Supervised Method for Non-rigid 3D Shape Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26557)
*Feifan Luo,Ting Li,Zhao Li,Hongyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于粗到细策略的混合自监督非刚性3D形状匹配方法，采用双分支功能图学习架构（拉普拉斯基与弹性基），在保持计算效率的同时，在非等距变形和拓扑噪声等挑战场景中达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非刚性3D形状匹配是计算机视觉与图形学的基础任务，现有方法在非等距变形和拓扑噪声等复杂场景下性能有限，且缺乏对粗匹配与精对应之间一致性的有效保障。

Method: 提出一种基于粗到细策略的混合自监督方法，包含双分支对称功能图学习流（拉普拉斯基分支与弹性基分支），通过精化模块确保粗映射与精对应之间的一致性，并利用对比能量增强特征判别性。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持计算效率的同时，在非等距变形和拓扑噪声等多种挑战场景下达到最先进性能；对比能量能有效提升特征判别性，且与现有方法集成可带来一致改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的粗到细混合自监督框架有效解决了非刚性3D形状匹配中的一致性问题，双分支功能图学习与对比能量的结合为形状匹配提供了新的高性能范式。

Original Abstract: Non-rigid 3D shape matching is a fundamental task in computer vision and graphics. In this paper, we propose a hybrid self-supervised method based on a coarse-to-fine strategy, which ensures consistency between the coarse mapping and the refined correspondence produced by our refinement module. The architecture features a dual-branch design, consisting of two symmetric functional map learning streams: one based on the Laplacian basis and the other utilizing the elastic basis. Extensive experiments show that our approach not only maintains computational efficiency, but also achieves state-of-the-art performance across a variety of challenging scenarios, including non-isometric deformations and topological noise. Finally, we rigorously demonstrate that contrastive energies promote feature discrimination. Furthermore, integrating these energies with existing methods yields consistent improvements, validating the overall efficacy of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/LuoFeifan77/Coarse-to-Fine-Hybrid-Self-Supervised-Matching.

中文摘要翻译: 非刚性三维形状匹配是计算机视觉与图形学中的一项基础任务。本文提出了一种基于粗到细策略的混合自监督方法，该方法确保粗映射与由精化模块产生的精对应之间的一致性。架构采用双分支设计，包含两个对称的功能图学习流：一个基于拉普拉斯基，另一个利用弹性基。大量实验表明，我们的方法不仅保持了计算效率，而且在包括非等距变形和拓扑噪声在内的多种挑战场景中均达到了最先进的性能。最后，我们严格论证了对比能量能够促进特征判别性。此外，将这些能量与现有方法集成可带来一致的改进，验证了我们方法的整体有效性。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/LuoFeifan77/Coarse-to-Fine-Hybrid-Self-Supervised-Matching 获取。

</details>


### [25] [SpaceRipple: Lightweight Semantic Delivery for Mission-Oriented LEO Earth Observation Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26559)
*Ziyi Yang,Hao Yuan,Yunxiang Yi,Wenbo Wang,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceRipple是一种轻量级框架，用于地球观测卫星网络中面向任务的语义传输与星上处理，通过协同压缩、转发、恢复与语义推理，实现语义级而非像素级图像传输，显著节省带宽并提升语义检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地球观测卫星网络生成海量高分辨率影像，但星间链和下行链路资源十分有限；在许多时间敏感任务中，地面用户真正需要的是与任务相关的语义信息，而非完整的原始图像数据。

Method: 提出SpaceRipple框架，包含：(1) 感知卫星执行自适应压缩与元数据生成以减少星间流量；(2) 边缘计算卫星恢复接收到的表示并提取任务相关语义信息；(3) 引入压缩感知的MoE（混合专家）增强模块，提升在退化视觉输入下的鲁棒性；(4) 构建压缩、转发、恢复与语义推理的协同流水线，实现语义导向传输。

Result: 实验结果表明，SpaceRipple在有限的卫星网络资源下，实现了良好的重建质量、更优的语义检测性能以及显著的带宽节省。

Conclusion: SpaceRipple证明了在资源受限的卫星网络中进行高效、可靠的地球观测是可行的，能够在带宽受限条件下兼顾重建质量与语义任务性能。

Original Abstract: Earth observation satellite networks generate massive volumes of high-resolution imagery, whereas inter-satellite and downlink resources remain limited. In many time-sensitive missions, ground users require mission-relevant semantic information rather than a full raw-image downlink. This paper proposes SpaceRipple, a lightweight framework for mission-oriented semantic delivery and on-board processing in Earth observation satellite networks. A sensing satellite performs adaptive compression and metadata generation to reduce inter-satellite traffic, while an edge computing satellite restores the received representation and extracts task-relevant semantic information. Unlike fidelity-driven image transmission, SpaceRipple coordinates compression, forwarding, restoration, and semantic inference within a collaborative pipeline, enabling semantic-oriented delivery instead of pixel-level image delivery. A compression-aware MoE enhancement module is further introduced to improve robustness under degraded visual inputs. Experimental results show that SpaceRipple achieves favorable reconstruction quality, improved semantic detection performance, and substantial bandwidth savings, demonstrating its potential for efficient and reliable Earth observation under constrained satellite-network resources.

中文摘要翻译: 地球观测卫星网络产生海量高分辨率影像，然而星间链路和下行链路资源仍然有限。在许多时间敏感任务中，地面用户需要的是与任务相关的语义信息，而非完整的原始图像下行传输。本文提出SpaceRipple，一种用于地球观测卫星网络中面向任务的语义传输与星上处理的轻量级框架。感知卫星执行自适应压缩与元数据生成以减少星间流量，同时边缘计算卫星恢复接收到的表示并提取任务相关的语义信息。与基于保真度的图像传输不同，SpaceRipple在协同流水线中协调压缩、转发、恢复和语义推理，实现语义导向传输而非像素级图像传输。进一步引入一种压缩感知的MoE增强模块，以提升在退化视觉输入下的鲁棒性。实验结果表明，SpaceRipple实现了良好的重建质量、更优的语义检测性能以及显著的带宽节省，展示了其在卫星网络资源受限条件下实现高效、可靠地球观测的潜力。

</details>


### [26] [DiCoBench: Benchmarking Multi-Image Fine-Grained Perception via Differential and Commonality Visual Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26602)
*Geng Li,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DiCoBench，一个面向跨图像细粒度感知的高分辨率多图像基准，揭示了现有MLLMs在高分辨率微观细节感知上的显著不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs基准多依赖显式文本线索或低分辨率输入，无法评估模型自主感知高分辨率图像中隐式视觉线索的能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含765个精心筛选样本的多图像高分辨率基准DiCoBench，分为差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索两个渐进轨道，涵盖8种感知任务，采用接近2K分辨率的高清图像，并设计为多项选择题形式以消除评估指标偏差。

Result: 在18个MLLMs上的广泛评估显示，与人类准确率（98.3%）相比存在显著差距，顶级模型在微观尺度细节捕获上表现困难。

Conclusion: DiCoBench可作为具有挑战性的测试平台，推动自主高分辨率多图像感知领域的未来研究。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive fine-grained perception capabilities. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on explicit textual cues or low-resolution inputs, failing to evaluate a model's ability to autonomously perceive implicit visual cues in high-resolution. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiCoBench, a comprehensive, multi-image high-resolution benchmark designed for cross-image fine-grained perception. DiCoBench consists of 765 meticulously curated samples categorized into two progressive tracks: Differential Visual Cues and Commonality Visual Cues, covering 8 distinct perception tasks. By formulating the benchmark as a multiple-choice question task and utilizing high-resolution imagery (approaching 2K), we eliminate evaluation metric bias and pose a substantial challenge to current state-of-the-art MLLMs. Our extensive evaluation of 18 diverse MLLMs reveals a striking performance gap compared to human accuracy (98.3\%), with top-performing models struggling significantly with micro-scale detail capture. We believe DiCoBench will serve as a challenging testbed to drive future research in autonomous, high-resolution multi-image perception.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型的最新进展展现了令人印象深刻的细粒度感知能力。然而，现有基准主要依赖显式文本线索或低分辨率输入，未能评估模型自主感知高分辨率图像中隐式视觉线索的能力。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了DiCoBench——一个面向跨图像细粒度感知的综合性多图像高分辨率基准。DiCoBench包含765个精心筛选的样本，分为两个渐进轨道：差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索，涵盖8种不同的感知任务。通过将基准设计为多项选择题形式并采用接近2K的高分辨率图像，我们消除了评估指标偏差，并对当前最先进的多模态大语言模型构成了实质性挑战。我们对18种不同多模态大语言模型的广泛评估显示，与人类准确率（98.3%）相比存在显著性能差距，顶级模型在微观尺度细节捕获上表现尤为困难。我们相信DiCoBench将作为一个具有挑战性的测试平台，推动自主高分辨率多图像感知领域的未来研究。

</details>


### [27] [LogicIR: Logic Gate Networks for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26609)
*Hongjae Lee,Myungjun Son,Jaeseong Yu,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LogicIR 是首个将逻辑门网络（LGN）应用于图像复原任务的模型，采用完全由逻辑门构成的 UNet 架构，通过可微比特解码层和索引洗牌机制提升信息传播效率，在显著降低计算成本的同时实现了有竞争力的复原性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像复原模型计算需求不断增长，而逻辑门网络（LGN）在高效计算方面展现出巨大潜力，但其在图像复原领域尚未被充分探索。因此，作者希望将 LGN 引入图像复原任务，以在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Method: 提出 LogicIR，一种完全由逻辑门（如 NAND、XOR）构成的 UNet 式架构；设计了可微比特解码层（differentiable bit decoding layer）和索引洗牌机制（index shuffling mechanism），以改善逻辑门之间的信息传播。

Result: 在多个图像复原基准上，LogicIR 以显著降低的计算成本取得了强竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: LogicIR 验证了逻辑门网络在图像复原任务中的可行性，为高效图像复原提供了一种有前景的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Image restoration aims to reconstruct high-quality images from degraded low-quality inputs. As the computational demands of image restoration models continue to rise, there is growing interest in lightweight architectures optimized for fast and efficient inference. Logic gate networks (LGNs), which operate using fundamental logic operations such as NAND and XOR, have recently emerged as a promising direction for achieving highly efficient computation. However, their potential remains largely untapped in the domain of image restoration. In this work, we introduce LogicIR, the first LGN specifically designed for image restoration tasks. LogicIR incorporates a UNet-inspired architecture composed entirely of logic gates. In addition, we propose a differentiable bit decoding layer and an index shuffling mechanism that improves information propagation across logic gates. Experimental results across multiple image restoration benchmarks demonstrate that LogicIR achieves strong performance with significantly reduced computational cost, establishing LogicIR as a viable and efficient alternative for image restoration. The source code is available at https://github.com/jimmy9704/LogicIR

中文摘要翻译: 图像复原旨在从退化的低质量输入中重建出高质量图像。随着图像复原模型的计算需求不断增长，针对快速高效推理优化的轻量级架构日益受到关注。逻辑门网络（LGN）通过使用诸如 NAND 和 XOR 等基本逻辑运算来实现高效计算，近来已成为一个极具前景的研究方向。然而，其在图像复原领域的潜力仍未被充分挖掘。在本工作中，我们提出了 LogicIR——首个专为图像复原任务设计的逻辑门网络。LogicIR 采用受 UNet 启发的架构，该架构完全由逻辑门构成。此外，我们提出了一种可微比特解码层和一种索引洗牌机制，以改善逻辑门之间的信息传播。在多个图像复原基准上的实验结果表明，LogicIR 在显著降低计算成本的同时取得了强劲的性能，确立了 LogicIR 作为一种可行且高效的图像复原替代方案的地位。源代码已开源在 https://github.com/jimmy9704/LogicIR。

</details>


### [28] [Position Rebinding Cache Reuse: Replay-Free Visual Revisiting for Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26631)
*Mengzhao Wang,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo,Peng Ye,Chongjun Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出位置重绑定缓存复用（PRCR）方法，通过存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标，在解码时重新分配位置兼容坐标并重绑定键，实现免重放的视觉证据复用，避免因位置绑定导致的解码崩溃，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有交错多模态推理方法依赖token重放（反复前馈选中的视觉token），计算成本高；直接复用历史视觉KV缓存会导致位置绑定问题——缓存的视觉键已与原始位置上下文绑定，在后续解码上下文中会扭曲注意力，甚至引发自回归解码崩溃。

Method: 提出位置重绑定缓存复用（PRCR），包含三步：1) 存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标；2) 根据当前解码状态分配位置兼容坐标以选择缓存条目；3) 对所选条目的键进行位置重绑定，然后将重构的缓存注入当前解码器缓存中。该方法在重用历史视觉证据的同时，保持文本位置连续性和相对视觉结构。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上，PRCR达到与token重放相当或更优的性能，平均准确率提升5%，视觉重访计算量降低高达数万倍。

Conclusion: 有效的缓存复用不能直接复制位置绑定的历史缓存条目，而需要重建与当前解码状态位置兼容的视觉证据；PRCR通过位置重绑定策略实现了这一目标，兼顾性能与效率。

Original Abstract: Interleaved multimodal reasoning improves visual grounding by revisiting visual evidence during multi-step generation, yet existing methods typically rely on token replay, repeatedly forwarding selected visual tokens. A natural shortcut is to reuse the historical visual key-value (KV) cache directly. However, we identify a critical failure mode of this strategy: cached visual keys are already bound to their original positional context. Such stale positional binding distorts attention under later decoding contexts and can trigger severe autoregressive decoding collapse. This failure suggests that effective cache reuse requires reconstructing visual evidence under positions compatible with the current decoding state, rather than directly copying position-bound historical cache entries. To this end, we propose Position Rebinding Cache Reuse (PRCR), a cache-level framework for replay-free visual revisiting. PRCR stores raw visual KV cache together with their original spatial coordinates, then reassigns position-compatible coordinates to select entries and rebinds their keys before injecting the reconstructed cache into the active decoder cache. This design reuses historical visual evidence while preserving textual positional continuity and relative visual structure. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that PRCR achieves replay-level or better performance, improving average accuracy by 5 percent and reducing visual-revisiting computation by up to tens of thousands of times.

中文摘要翻译: 交错多模态推理通过在多步生成过程中重新审视视觉证据来改进视觉定位，然而现有方法通常依赖token重放，反复前馈选中的视觉token。一种自然的捷径是直接复用历史视觉键值（KV）缓存。然而，我们发现该策略存在一个关键失败模式：缓存的视觉键已与其原始位置上下文绑定。这种陈旧的位置绑定会在后续解码上下文中扭曲注意力，并可能引发严重的自回归解码崩溃。这一失效表明，有效的缓存复用需要重建与当前解码状态位置兼容的视觉证据，而非直接复制位置绑定的历史缓存条目。为此，我们提出位置重绑定缓存复用（PRCR），一种用于免重放视觉重新审视的缓存级框架。PRCR将原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标一同存储，然后重新分配位置兼容的坐标以选择条目，并在将重构的缓存注入当前解码器缓存之前对其键进行位置重绑定。该设计在重用历史视觉证据的同时，保持了文本位置连续性和相对视觉结构。跨多个多模态推理基准的实验表明，PRCR达到了与token重放相当或更优的性能，平均准确率提升5%，并将视觉重访计算量降低高达数万倍。

</details>


### [29] [Temporally Consistent Label Interpolation for Robust Surgical Multi-Task Learning under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26634)
*Garam Kim,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对外科手术场景理解中多任务学习因标注粒度不匹配（时间任务需密集帧级标注，空间任务仅稀疏标注）而受限的问题，本文提出FAROS框架，利用光流引导的标签插值生成密集伪标签，并整合到统一的Transformer多任务框架中，显著提升跨任务表示学习和手术场景理解性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 外科手术场景理解中的多任务学习面临标注粒度不匹配的根本障碍：时间维度任务（如阶段识别、步骤识别与预测）需要密集的帧级监督，而像素级空间任务（如器械分割与动作识别）因标注成本过高仅能在少数关键帧上进行稀疏标注。这种监督不平衡损害了共享表示学习，限制了异构手术任务间的联合优化。

Method: 提出FAROS（Flow-guided Annotation for Robust Operating Scenes），一种光流引导的标签插值框架。该方法结合零样本分割的掩码传播与光流估计，克服在遮挡、烟雾、运动模糊等复杂手术条件下基于外观传播的局限性，从稀疏关键帧标注生成时序一致的密集伪标签。然后将生成的密集器械掩码和动作标签整合到统一的基于Transformer的多任务框架中，联合学习手术阶段识别、步骤识别、预测、器械分割和动作识别，实现密集时间监督与稀疏空间监督之间的平衡优化。

Result: 首先在DAVIS 2017基准的稀疏真值协议下验证了FAROS的标签插值质量，证实了其在外科领域之外的鲁棒传播能力。在GraSP、MISAW和AutoLaparo三个基准上的大量实验表明，FAROS显著提升了跨任务表示学习，并在时空任务上增强了整体手术场景理解性能。

Conclusion: FAROS通过光流引导的标签插值有效解决了多任务手术场景理解中的标注粒度不匹配问题，生成的密集伪标签能够平衡时间与空间任务的监督信号，从而提升跨任务表示学习与整体场景理解性能。

Original Abstract: Effective multi-task learning for surgical scene understanding is fundamentally hindered by annotation granularity mismatch; temporal workflow tasks such as phase recognition, step recognition and anticipation benefit from dense frame-level supervision, whereas pixel-level spatial tasks including instrument segmentation and action recognition are only sparsely annotated on selected keyframes due to prohibitive labeling costs. This supervision imbalance undermines shared representation learning and limits joint optimization across heterogeneous surgical tasks. To address this, we propose Flow-guided Annotation for Robust Operating Scenes (FAROS), a flow-guided label interpolation framework, that combines zero-shot segmentation-based mask propagation with optical flow estimation to overcome the limitations of appearance-based propagation under challenging surgical conditions such as occlusion, smoke, and motion blur, generating temporally consistent dense pseudo labels from sparse keyframe annotations. The densified instrument masks and action labels are integrated into a unified Transformer-based multi-task framework that jointly learns surgical phase recognition, step recognition, anticipation, instrument segmentation, and action recognition, enabling balanced optimization between dense temporal supervision and sparse spatial supervision. The label interpolation quality of FAROS is first validated on the DAVIS 2017 benchmark under a sparse ground-truth protocol, confirming robust propagation beyond the surgical domain. Extensive experiments on GraSP, MISAW, and AutoLaparo benchmarks further demonstrate that FAROS significantly improves cross-task representation learning and enhances holistic surgical scene understanding performance across spatio-temporal tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 有效的外科手术场景理解多任务学习从根本上受到标注粒度不匹配的阻碍；时间维度的工作流任务（如阶段识别、步骤识别和预测）受益于密集的帧级监督，而像素级空间任务（包括器械分割和动作识别）由于标注成本过高，仅在选定的关键帧上进行稀疏标注。这种监督不平衡损害了共享表示学习，并限制了异构手术任务间的联合优化。为解决这一问题，我们提出了FAROS（Flow-guided Annotation for Robust Operating Scenes），一种光流引导的标签插值框架，结合零样本分割的掩码传播与光流估计，克服了在遮挡、烟雾和运动模糊等具有挑战性的手术条件下基于外观传播的局限性，从稀疏关键帧标注生成时序一致的密集伪标签。生成的密集器械掩码和动作标签被整合到一个统一的基于Transformer的多任务框架中，该框架联合学习手术阶段识别、步骤识别、预测、器械分割和动作识别，实现了密集时间监督与稀疏空间监督之间的平衡优化。FAROS的标签插值质量首先在DAVIS 2017基准上采用稀疏真值协议进行验证，证实了其在外科领域之外的鲁棒传播能力。在GraSP、MISAW和AutoLaparo基准上的大量实验进一步表明，FAROS显著提升了跨任务表示学习，并增强了时空任务上的整体手术场景理解性能。

</details>


### [30] [LayersReg: A Layer-by-Layer Progressive Regressor for Reliable Intraoperative 3D/2D Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26647)
*Xiyuan Wang,Zhenchao Wang,Xinran Chen,Junkai Liu,Chuan Chen,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LayersReg是一种新颖的3D/2D配准回归范式，通过逐层迭代搜索特征空间中的像素流趋势，模拟经典配准的优化过程，使模型具备3D解剖结构感知能力，在大偏移和多模态条件下实现了高精度（X射线/CT配准0.68°, 1.41 mm；切片定位0.73°, 1.55 mm），优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统迭代优化算法在术中配准中效率低、失败率高；现有深度学习方法将配准重构为回归问题，但本质上只是记忆驱动的特定姿态特征检索，而非真正理解图像对齐任务本身，导致在复杂场景中泛化能力受限。因此，需要一种能够理解图像对齐本质、具备3D解剖感知能力的配准方法。

Method: 提出LayersReg，一种逐层渐进式回归范式。方法核心包括：(1) 在特征空间中搜索移动图像与固定图像之间的相关性，捕捉像素流趋势，从而迭代收敛到正确的空间姿态变换；(2) 设计节点级回归与渐进式配准框架的耦合，增强模型对空间姿态变化的感知能力。该方法模拟经典配准的迭代姿态搜索优化准则。

Result: 在大偏移和多模态条件下，LayersReg在X射线/CT配准任务上达到0.68°、1.41 mm的精度，在切片定位任务上达到0.73°、1.55 mm的精度，均优于现有最先进方法，同时满足术中精准性和实时性要求。

Conclusion: LayersReg通过逐层渐进式回归的范式，成功将经典配准的迭代优化思想融入深度回归框架，使模型具备3D解剖结构感知能力，在复杂场景下兼顾了高精度与实时性，为术中3D/2D配准提供了一种有效的新方案。

Original Abstract: 3D/2D registration serves as a cornerstone technique in surgical navigation. Traditional iterative optimization algorithms suffer from low efficiency and high failure rates in intraoperative settings. Deep learning-based methods reformulate registration from iterative optimization to a regression problem that maps image appearance features to spatial pose, typically achieving improved real-time performance and accuracy.
