However, such learnable methods are confined to memory-driven retrieval of specific pose features rather than understanding the task of image alignment itself, which limits their generalization in complex scenarios. We propose LayersReg, a pioneering regression paradigm that endows the model with 3D anatomical awareness and searches for the correct pose in a progressive, layer-by-layer manner. Inspired by the iterative pose-searching optimization criterion of classical registration, LayersReg searches for correlations between the moving and fixed images in feature space, capturing the trend of pixel flow and thereby converging iteratively toward the correct spatial pose transformation. We further design a coupling of node-wise regression with the progressive registration framework to enhance the model's perception of spatial pose changes. Experimental results demonstrate that under large offsets and multimodality conditions, LayersReg achieves high accuracy on both X-ray/CT registration (0.68°, 1.41 mm) and slice localization (0.73°, 1.55 mm) tasks, outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods while meeting the intraoperative demands for precision and real-time capability.

中文摘要翻译: 3D/2D配准是手术导航中的基石技术。传统的迭代优化算法在术中场景下存在效率低、失败率高的问题。基于深度学习的方法将配准从迭代优化重构为回归问题，将图像外观特征映射到空间姿态，通常能实现更好的实时性和精度。然而，这类可学习方法局限于通过记忆驱动检索特定姿态特征，而非理解图像对齐任务本身，从而限制了其在复杂场景中的泛化能力。我们提出LayersReg，一种开创性的回归范式，它赋予模型3D解剖结构感知能力，并以逐层渐进的方式搜索正确姿态。受经典配准中迭代姿态搜索优化准则的启发，LayersReg在特征空间中搜索移动图像与固定图像之间的相关性，捕捉像素流趋势，从而逐步迭代收敛到正确的空间姿态变换。我们进一步设计了节点级回归与渐进式配准框架的耦合，以增强模型对空间姿态变化的感知能力。实验结果表明，在大偏移和多模态条件下，LayersReg在X射线/CT配准（0.68°, 1.41 mm）和切片定位（0.73°, 1.55 mm）任务上均实现了高精度，优于现有最先进方法，同时满足术中对精准性和实时性的需求。

</details>


### [31] [Disco-LoRA: Disentangled Composition of Content, Style, and Motion for Multi-concept Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26668)
*Xuancheng Xu,Gengyun Jia,Bing-Kun Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Disco-LoRA框架，通过两阶段解耦与融合方法，实现文本到视频生成中内容、风格和运动的多概念联合控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有T2V定制方法在图像风格化和视频运动定制上取得进展，但同时对内容、风格和运动等多个概念进行联合控制仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 提出Disco-LoRA框架，分两阶段：(1)将目标分解为内容-风格和内容-运动两个子任务，每个子任务使用迭代双LoRA解耦框架；(2)识别层间权重趋势对LoRA身份至关重要，提出基于Z分数的统计正则化来对齐权重分布，保留层间趋势同时最小化不同LoRA间的干扰。

Result: 大量实验表明Disco-LoRA在多概念视频定制中表现出色，能够有效保留外观、风格和运动，实现可控的文本到视频生成。

Conclusion: Disco-LoRA通过解耦与重组合框架及Z分数正则化，有效解决了多概念视频定制中内容、风格和运动的联合控制难题。

Original Abstract: Video customization based on Text-to-Video (T2V) models aims to learn specific features from reference data to generate controllable videos. While significant strides have been made in image stylization and video motion customization, simultaneously controlling multiple concepts, such as content, style, and motion, remains a major challenge. In this work, we systematically define the task of multi-concept video customization, which requires the joint control of content, style, and motion. To facilitate research in this area, we construct a comprehensive benchmark and propose Disco-LoRA, a unified framework designed to tackle this problem by disentangling and flexibly recombining different concepts in two stages: (1) We decompose the objective into two sub-tasks: Content-Style and Content-Motion. Each sub-task is addressed using our Iterative Dual-LoRA Disentanglement Framework, which effectively disentangles distinct concepts within the data. (2) We identify layer-wise weight trends as crucial for LoRA identity, while weight magnitudes dictate composability. To harmonize these scales, we propose a Z-score-based statistical regularization that aligns weight distributions, preserving layer-wise trends while minimizing interference between different LoRAs. Extensive experiments show that Disco-LoRA excels in multi-concept video customization, effectively preserving appearance, style, and motion for controllable text-to-video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于文本到视频（T2V）模型的视频定制旨在从参考数据中学习特定特征，以生成可控视频。尽管在图像风格化和视频运动定制方面已取得显著进展，同时控制多个概念（如内容、风格和运动）仍然是一个重大挑战。本文系统性地定义了多概念视频定制任务，该任务要求联合控制内容、风格和运动。为促进该领域的研究，我们构建了一个全面的基准，并提出了Disco-LoRA，这是一个统一的框架，通过两个阶段解耦并灵活重组不同概念来解决该问题：(1) 我们将目标分解为两个子任务：内容-风格和内容-运动。每个子任务均采用我们提出的迭代双LoRA解耦框架，该框架能有效解耦数据中的不同概念。(2) 我们识别出层间权重趋势对LoRA身份至关重要，而权重幅度则决定可组合性。为协调这些尺度，我们提出了一种基于Z分数的统计正则化方法，用于对齐权重分布，在保留层间趋势的同时，最小化不同LoRA之间的相互干扰。大量实验表明，Disco-LoRA在多概念视频定制中表现出色，能够有效保留外观、风格和运动，实现可控的文本到视频生成。

</details>


### [32] [DeCoFlow: Structural Decomposition of Normalizing Flows for Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26687)
*Hun Im,Jungi Lee,Subeen Cha,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCoFlow，一种基于归一化流的持续异常检测方法，通过解耦子网络为冻结通用基座与任务特定低秩适配器，在保持严格可逆性的前提下实现参数隔离，克服灾难性遗忘，在MVTec-AD和VisA数据集上达到SOTA性能并实现零遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业环境中新产品类别顺序到达，需要在不访问历史数据的情况下进行持续异常检测。归一化流虽能精确估计密度，但参数更新会导致灾难性遗忘，且参数隔离方法必须保持NF的严格可逆性与雅可比行列式有效性，现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出DeCoFlow，其核心思想是利用仿射耦合层的不变性（子网络参数化不影响变换有效性），将子网络分解为冻结的通用基座和任务特定的低秩适配器，实现参数隔离。此外引入任务特定对齐、辅助耦合层和尾感知损失来补偿冻结基座的刚性。

Result: 在MVTec-AD上达到98.40%图像级AUROC，在VisA上达到93.00%图像级AUROC；在正确路由下实现0.00%遗忘度量（零遗忘），每任务仅需2.27M参数。

Conclusion: DeCoFlow通过巧妙利用仿射耦合层的固有属性，实现了持续异常检测中参数级零遗忘与高检测性能的兼得，为工业场景下高效、无遗忘的异常检测提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: In industrial environments, new product categories arrive sequentially, requiring continual anomaly detection without access to past data. Normalizing Flows (NFs) provide exact density estimation but suffer from catastrophic forgetting as parameter updates across tasks distort the density manifold. While parameter isolation can prevent interference, it must preserve the strict invertibility and Jacobian validity of NFs. To satisfy these requirements, we exploit the inherent property that affine coupling layers maintain transformation validity regardless of subnet parameterization. Based on this, we propose DeCoFlow, which decomposes subnets into a frozen universal base and task-specific low-rank adapters to isolate updates. We further introduce Task-Specific Alignment, Auxiliary Coupling Layers, and Tail-Aware Loss to compensate for frozen-base rigidity. DeCoFlow achieves state-of-the-art image-level AUROCs of 98.40% on MVTec-AD and 93.00% on VisA, while maintaining parameter-level zero forgetting (0.00% FM under correct routing) with only 2.27M parameters per task.

中文摘要翻译: 在工业环境中，新产品类别顺序到达，要求在不访问历史数据的情况下进行持续异常检测。归一化流（NF）能够提供精确的密度估计，但跨任务的参数更新会扭曲密度流形，导致灾难性遗忘。虽然参数隔离可以防止干扰，但必须保持NF的严格可逆性和雅可比有效性。为满足这些要求，我们利用仿射耦合层的一个固有特性——无论子网络如何参数化，其变换有效性保持不变。基于此，我们提出DeCoFlow，将子网络分解为冻结的通用基座与任务特定的低秩适配器，从而实现参数隔离。我们进一步引入任务特定对齐、辅助耦合层和尾感知损失来补偿冻结基座的刚性。DeCoFlow在MVTec-AD上达到98.40%的图像级AUROC，在VisA上达到93.00%，同时在正确路由下实现参数级零遗忘（遗忘度量0.00%），每任务仅需2.27M参数。

</details>


### [33] [PhysEditWorld: A Large-Scale Dataset Toward Physics-Editable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26694)
*Bin Hu,Yanwen Ma,Jiehui Huang,Ziliang Zhang,Haoning Wu,Ruicheng Zhang,Yaokun Li,Zijun Wang,Yuechen Zhang,Chun-Mei Tseng,Hanhui Li,Shengju Qian,Jun Zhou,Kaipeng Zhang,Xiaodan Liang,Jiaya Jia,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysEditWorld是一个以重力参数为核心的多模态游戏世界数据集，通过UE5重放-渲染管线生成60M+帧数据，支持可控物理变量建模，提升生成式视频模型和世界理解模型在物理一致性方面的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型生成的轨迹多为探索性或漫游式，物理动力学仅作为隐式相关性从数据中学习，而非可控变量，限制了其在物理规则需显式操控的编创游戏环境中的应用。

Method: 基于UE5重放-渲染管线构建数据集：录制归一化动作轨迹，在不同重力配置下重放相同初始状态、角色控制器、动作序列和相机策略，同步采集RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和显式重力标签等多模态信号。

Result: 包含12个电影级UE5场景、100+小时游戏交互、60M+帧；在生成式视频模型和世界理解模型上的初步实验表明，PhysEditWorld能够提升重力保真的动力学建模能力，增强物理编辑下的一致性。

Conclusion: PhysEditWorld为可控世界建模研究提供了可扩展的基础，证明了显式物理参数标注数据对提升模型物理一致性的有效性。

Original Abstract: Recent game world models can synthesize visually plausible, action-conditioned rollouts. However, their interaction behaviors often remain limited to exploratory or wandering trajectories, and physical dynamics are typically learned as implicit correlations from data rather than as controllable variables. This limitation hinders their applicability to authored game environments, where physical rules are deliberately designed and require explicit manipulation. We introduce PhysEditWorld, a multimodal dataset with physical parameters, with a primary focus on gravity in this initial version. At its core, PhysEditWorld is built upon a replay paradigm implemented with a UE5 replay-and-rendering pipeline. Each scenario records a normalized action trace and replays the same initial state, character controller, action sequence, and camera policy under multiple gravity configurations, enabling controlled and attributable physical variation. PhysEditWorld contains 12 cinematic UE5 scenes, over 100 hours of gameplay interactions, and more than 60 million rendered rollout frames. Each sample provides synchronized multimodal signals, including RGB, depth, normals, audio, action traces, camera trajectory, engine states, semantic annotations, and explicit gravity labels. We further conduct initial utility studies on both generative video models and world understanding models, demonstrating that PhysEditWorld enables improved gravity-faithful dynamics modeling, enhances consistency under physical edits, and provides a scalable foundation for controllable world modeling research.

中文摘要翻译: 近期游戏世界模型能够合成视觉上合理的、以动作为条件的 rollout 结果。然而，它们的交互行为往往局限于探索性或漫游式轨迹，且物理动力学通常作为数据中的隐式相关性进行学习，而非作为可控变量。这一局限性阻碍了它们应用于编创游戏环境，因为在这些环境中物理规则是精心设计的，并要求显式操控。我们提出了 PhysEditWorld——一个包含物理参数的多模态数据集，本初始版本主要关注重力参数。PhysEditWorld 的核心建立在基于 UE5 重放-渲染管线的重放范式之上。每个场景录制一条归一化的动作轨迹，并在多种重力配置下重放相同的初始状态、角色控制器、动作序列和相机策略，从而实现可控且可归因的物理变化。PhysEditWorld 包含 12 个电影级 UE5 场景、超过 100 小时的游戏交互数据以及超过 6000 万帧渲染 rollout。每个样本提供同步的多模态信号，包括 RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和显式重力标签。我们进一步在生成式视频模型和世界理解模型上开展了初步效用研究，结果表明 PhysEditWorld 能够提升重力保真的动力学建模能力，增强物理编辑下的一致性，并为可控世界建模研究提供了可扩展的基础。

</details>


### [34] [Intracranial Aneurysm Classification and Segmentation via Tri-Axial ROI and Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26706)
*Pengcheng Shi,Kaiyuan Yang,Houjing Huang,Jiawei Chen,Yan Lu,Jiaqi Liu,Murong Xu,Bjoern Menze,Xinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多任务框架，同时进行颅内动脉瘤的多标签分类、多类别分割和血管分割，覆盖13个解剖位置和4种成像模态，在RSNA 2025挑战赛中取得第二名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动化方法仅局限于二元检测，缺乏细粒度的解剖分类和多类别分割能力，而动脉瘤的破裂风险评估和治疗规划同时依赖于形态学和解剖位置，因此需要更全面的自动化分析框架。

Method: 两阶段方法：首先使用快速2D三轴感兴趣区域（ROI）提取方法，然后采用基于3D多任务nnU-Net骨干网络的双解码器架构。双解码器设计缓解动脉瘤与血管类别间的极端体积不平衡，跨注意力池化和模态特定辅助头改善了异构输入下的特征学习。

Result: 两折集成方法在RSNA 2025颅内动脉瘤检测挑战赛中取得第二名。

Conclusion: 该多任务框架能有效实现多标签分类、多类别分割和血管分割的统一处理，开源了代码、模型权重和3D Slicer插件以促进临床应用。

Original Abstract: Intracranial aneurysms are often asymptomatic until rupture, which carries high mortality. Rupture risk assessment and treatment planning depend on both aneurysm morphology and anatomical location, yet existing automated methods remain limited to binary detection without fine-grained anatomical classification or multi-class segmentation. We present a multi-task framework that simultaneously performs multi-label classification, multi-class aneurysm segmentation, and multi-class vessel segmentation across 13 anatomical locations and four imaging modalities (CTA, MRA, T2, T1-post). Our two-stage approach combines a fast 2D tri-axial Region of Interest (ROI) extraction method with a 3D multi-task nnU-Net backbone. A dual-decoder design mitigates the extreme volume imbalance between aneurysm and vessel classes, while cross-attention pooling and modality-specific auxiliary heads improve feature learning across heterogeneous inputs. Our two-fold ensemble achieved 2nd place in the RSNA 2025 Intracranial Aneurysm Detection challenge. Code, model weights, and a 3D Slicer plugin are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 颅内动脉瘤在破裂前通常无症状，而破裂后死亡率极高。破裂风险评估和治疗规划同时依赖于动脉瘤形态学和解剖位置，然而现有自动化方法仍局限于二元检测，缺乏细粒度的解剖分类或多类别分割。我们提出了一个多任务框架，可同时在13个解剖位置和四种成像模态（CTA、MRA、T2、T1-post）上执行多标签分类、多类别动脉瘤分割和多类别血管分割。我们的两阶段方法结合了快速2D三轴感兴趣区域（ROI）提取方法与3D多任务nnU-Net骨干网络。双解码器设计缓解了动脉瘤与血管类别之间的极端体积不平衡，而跨注意力池化和模态特定辅助头改善了跨异构输入的特征学习。我们的两折集成方法在RSNA 2025颅内动脉瘤检测挑战赛中取得了第二名。代码、模型权重和3D Slicer插件均已公开发布。

</details>


### [35] [Mask to Concept: Auto-Promptable SAM3 via Efficient Test-Time Concept Embedding Search for Few-Shot Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26711)
*Quan Zhou,Shaoqing Zhai,Qiang Hu Jia Chen,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M2C框架，无需外部模块或重新训练，利用可学习概念嵌入和混合不确定性估计，将SAM3从人类提示工具转化为自动提示标注器，实现高效医学图像少样本标注。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖外部特征匹配器或辅助网络来实现自动几何提示，引入了架构开销且限制了性能扩展；SAM3虽原生支持文本提示的概念分割，但缺乏细粒度临床知识且人工描述存在歧义，难以直接用于医学影像。

Method: 提出M2C框架：1) 初始化可学习概念嵌入，引导SAM3分割并利用梯度更新嵌入以最小化概念分割误差；2) 引入混合不确定性估计(HUE)模块，计算预测熵并将概念预测映射回框提示，度量概念-几何提示不一致性；3) 高不确定性样本主动标记供人工修正，修正掩码反馈回M2C持续搜索更精确概念嵌入，形成自增强标注循环。

Result: 在医学分割基准上实现最先进的少样本分割性能和出色的标注效率。

Conclusion: M2C提供了一种无需外部模块、无需参数重训练、无需手动文本工程的实用高效路径，可扩展用于大规模医学图像标注。

Original Abstract: Transforming foundation segmentation models from human-prompted tools into auto-promptable annotators is critical for scalable medical data annotation. Current methods commonly depend on external feature matchers or auxiliary networks to automate geometric prompting, but introducing architectural overhead and limiting performance scalability. Although SAM3 natively supports concept segmentation via reusable text prompts, its direct use in medical imaging is hindered by a lack of fine-grained clinical knowledge and the ambiguity of human-written descriptions. In this work, we propose Mask to Concept (M2C), an efficient framework that adapts SAM3 for medical few-shot annotation without external modules, parameter retraining, or manual text engineering. Using only a few labeled images, M2C enables SAM3 to automatically search for transferable visual concepts entirely within its frozen architecture: it initializes a learnable concept embedding, uses it to prompt segmentation, and updates the embedding by gradients of minimizing the concept segmentation error. We further introduce a Hybrid Uncertainty Estimation (HUE) module that calculates the prediction entropy and maps concept predictions back to the box prompts, measuring concept-geometry prompting inconsistency. Highly uncertain samples are flagged actively for human correction, and the corrected masks are then fed back to M2C to continuously search for more precise concept embeddings, forming a self-enhancing annotation loop with minimal expert effort. Experiments on medical segmentation benchmarks show that our method achieves SOTA few-shot segmentation performance and outstanding annotation efficiency, offering a practical and efficient pathway toward scalable medical image labeling. Codes are at https://github.com/Huster-Hq/M2C.

中文摘要翻译: 将基础分割模型从人工提示工具转化为自动可提示标注器，对于可扩展的医学数据标注至关重要。当前方法通常依赖外部特征匹配器或辅助网络来自动化几何提示，但这引入了架构开销并限制了性能的可扩展性。尽管SAM3通过可重用文本提示原生支持概念分割，但其在医学影像中的直接使用受到缺乏细粒度临床知识和人工描述的歧义性的阻碍。在这项工作中，我们提出了Mask to Concept (M2C)，一个高效的框架，在无需外部模块、参数重训练或手动文本工程的情况下，将SAM3适配到医学少样本标注。仅使用少量标注图像，M2C就能使SAM3在其冻结架构内自动搜索可迁移的视觉概念：它初始化一个可学习的概念嵌入，使用该嵌入提示分割，并通过最小化概念分割误差的梯度来更新嵌入。我们进一步引入了混合不确定性估计（HUE）模块，该模块计算预测熵并将概念预测映射回框提示，从而度量概念-几何提示的不一致性。高不确定性的样本被主动标记以供人工修正，修正后的掩码被反馈回M2C以持续搜索更精确的概念嵌入，形成一个仅需最少专家努力的自增强标注循环。在医学分割基准上的实验表明，我们的方法实现了最先进的少样本分割性能和出色的标注效率，为可扩展的医学图像标注提供了一条实用且高效的途径。代码见https://github.com/Huster-Hq/M2C。

</details>


### [36] [Extracting Neural Materials from Multi-view Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26715)
*Kim Youwang,Jon Hasselgren,Peter Kocsis,Andrea Weidlich,Tae-Hyun Oh,Jacob Munkberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuMatEx提出了一种基于可微逆渲染的神经材质提取方法，利用大型材质重建模型（LMRM）提供初始预测和不确定性引导，解决了神经材质隐空间非线性结构导致的优化困难，在合成和真实资产上实现了优于PBR方法的材质分解效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经材质能以紧凑的通用基表示复杂的镜面反射和散射效应，但其获取和编辑仍具挑战性，且神经材质隐空间的非线性结构使得朴素逆渲染优化不可行。

Method: 训练一个大型材质重建模型（LMRM），直接从图像预测初始基础颜色、神经材质隐变量和偶然不确定性指导；该材质先验提供良好初始化并约束后续的逆路径追踪优化，其中预测的不确定性用于将高置信区域更紧密锚定到LMRM预测，防止光照和复杂镜面效应被烘焙到材质中。

Result: 在合成和真实资产上的实验表明，NeuMatEx提取的复杂材质在视觉质量上优于基于PBR的方法，且材质分解效果更好。

Conclusion: NeuMatEx通过结合学习的材质先验与可微逆渲染，有效地从图像中提取高质量的空间变化神经材质，克服了传统方法的局限性。

Original Abstract: Neural materials can represent complex specular reflections and scattering effects in a compact, universal basis. However, acquiring and authoring such materials remains challenging. We present NeuMatEx, a differentiable inverse rendering method for extracting spatially varying neural materials from images. The nonlinear structure of neural material latent spaces makes optimization with naive inverse rendering infeasible. To address this, we train a Large Material Reconstruction Model (LMRM) that directly predicts initialbase color, neural material latents, and aleatoric uncertainty guides from images. This material prior provides a good initialization and better constrains our subsequent optimization using inverse path tracing. The predicted uncertainty further helps by anchoring high-confidence regions more tightly to the LMRM prediction, preventing lighting and complex specular effects from being baked into materials. Experiments on synthetic and real assets show that NeuMatEx extracts complex materials with better visual quality and material decomposition than PBR-based methods.

中文摘要翻译: 神经材质可以用紧凑且通用的基来表示复杂的镜面反射和散射效果。然而，获取和编辑此类材质仍然具有挑战性。我们提出NeuMatEx，一种可微逆渲染方法，用于从图像中提取空间变化的神经材质。神经材质隐空间的非线性结构使得朴素的逆渲染优化不可行。为解决这一问题，我们训练了一个大型材质重建模型（LMRM），该模型直接从图像预测初始基础颜色、神经材质隐变量以及偶然不确定性指导。这一材质先验提供了良好的初始化，并更好地约束了后续基于逆路径追踪的优化。预测的不确定性进一步帮助将高置信区域更紧密地锚定到LMRM的预测上，防止光照和复杂的镜面效果被烘焙到材质中。在合成和真实资产上的实验表明，与基于PBR的方法相比，NeuMatEx能够提取出具有更好视觉质量和材质分解效果的复杂材质。

</details>


### [37] [Full spectrum Unlearnable Examples via Spectral Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26719)
*Jiale Cai,Gezheng Xu,Zhihao Li,Ruiyi Fang,Ruizhi Pu,Di Wu,Qicheng Lao,Charles Ling,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有的不可学习示例（UEs）在低频滤波下会失效，因为扰动信号主要集中在高频；为此提出FUSE方法，通过频谱均衡生成全频段有效的扰动，确保模型在不同频谱下均无法学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有UEs存在关键缺陷：一旦应用低通滤波，扰动信号失效，因为有效扰动高度集中于高频，缺乏对低频段的鲁棒性。因此，需要设计能在全频谱范围内保持有效的可靠UEs。

Method: 提出FUSE（Full-spectrum Unlearnable examples via Spectral Equalization）：(1) 随机频谱掩码（RSM）策略，在生成器训练时随机移除连续频带，迫使剩余频带维持不可学习性；(2) 跨频带引导（CBG），强制高/低频分量相互一致，增强低频不可学习性并约束高频扰动以保留图像语义保真度。

Result: 在多个数据集、多种网络架构和不同频谱滤波条件下，FUSE均实现了强效的保护效果，验证了其全频段鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 可靠的不可学习示例应当在全频谱范围内均有效。FUSE通过频谱均衡生成与频谱无关的扰动，弥补了现有方法对低频滤波脆弱的缺陷。

Original Abstract: Unlearnable examples (UEs) protect training data by injecting imperceptible perturbations so that models fail to extract exploitable representations. In this paper, we reveal that existing UEs exhibit a critical failure once low-pass filtering is applied, indicating that the effective perturbation signals for unlearnability concentrate predominantly in high frequencies. Hence, we argue that reliable UEs should remain effective across the full spectrum. To this end, we propose Full-spectrum Unlearnable examples via Spectral Equalization (FUSE), which aims to generate spectrum-agnostic perturbations by equalizing the contributions from different bands and enforcing cross-band consistency. Specifically, FUSE adopts a Random Spectral Masking (RSM) strategy during generator training, which randomly removes a contiguous frequency band, forcing the remaining bands to maintain unlearnability. In addition, FUSE further integrates Cross-Band Guidance (CBG), which enforces mutual consistency between high- and low-frequency components, thereby further enhancing low-frequency unlearnability and regulating high-frequency perturbations to preserve the semantic fidelity of images. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and spectral filtering demonstrate the strong protection achieved by FUSE.

中文摘要翻译: 不可学习示例（UEs）通过注入不可察觉的扰动来保护训练数据，使得模型无法提取可利用的表征。在本文中，我们揭示了现有UEs在应用低通滤波后会出现关键性失效，这表明实现不可学习性的有效扰动信号主要集中分布在高频区域。因此，我们认为可靠的UEs应该在整个频谱范围内保持有效。为此，我们提出了基于频谱均衡的全频谱不可学习示例（FUSE），旨在通过均衡不同频带的贡献并强制跨频带一致性来生成与频谱无关的扰动。具体来说，FUSE在生成器训练过程中采用随机频谱掩码（RSM）策略，随机移除一个连续频带，迫使剩余频带维持不可学习性。此外，FUSE进一步集成了跨频带引导（CBG），强制高低频分量之间的相互一致性，从而增强低频不可学习性并调节高频扰动以保持图像的语义保真度。在多个数据集、多种网络架构和不同频谱滤波条件下的广泛实验证明了FUSE所实现的强效保护能力。

</details>


### [38] [Robust Onion: Peeling Open Vocab Object Detectors Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26734)
*Priyank Pathak,Mukilan Karuppasamy,Aaditya Baranwal,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过可控合成退化实验系统分析了真实世界噪声对开放词汇目标检测器鲁棒性的影响，揭示了鲁棒性主要由视觉骨干网络和图像域决定，并提出了一种轻量级即插即用方法NN&TK0，仅需端到端训练0.1%的参数即可提升鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开放词汇目标检测器（OV-ODs）架构复杂，真实世界噪声对其鲁棒性的影响尚不明确，现有研究缺乏系统性的逐层分析。

Method: 采用"Robust Onion"方法，通过可控合成视觉退化逐层"剥离"OV-ODs，分析特征坍缩（feature collapse）机制；并提出轻量级即插即用方法NN&TK0，仅用96倍更少的可训练参数提升鲁棒性。

Result: 1) 视觉骨干网络相似的模型表现出类似的鲁棒性，特征坍缩发生在相似层；2) 预训练策略、架构细节和文本监督对鲁棒性贡献甚微；3) 鲁棒性主要由图像域而非标注决定，解释了COCO与LVIS上的相似表现以及ODinW-13上鲁棒性被高估的现象；4) NN&TK0在BDD100K等真实场景中有效提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 开放词汇目标检测器的鲁棒性主要受视觉骨干网络和图像域驱动，可通过轻量级方法有效改善，并解释了先前工作中的鲁棒性观察结果。

Original Abstract: The impact of real-world noise on Open Vocabulary Object Detectors (OV-ODs) remains poorly understood due to their architectural complexity. We present our comprehensive analysis Robust Onion, an empirical study that uses controlled synthetic visual degradations to peel OV-ODs layer-by-layer, revealing how, why, and where robustness degrades, systematically analyzing feature collapse. Our findings reveal that models with similar vision backbones exhibit comparable robustness, driven by similar feature collapse at similar layers, while factors such as pretraining strategy, architectural nuances, and caption supervision contribute little. Robustness is primarily governed by the image domain rather than annotations, explaining the similar robustness impact on COCO and LVIS, and why datasets like ODinW-13 can give an impression of inflated robustness due to large, isolated objects. Finally, we validate our insights by improving robustness on real-world BDD100K, WiderFace, and VisDRONE via our lightweight plug-and-play NN & TK0 approach, using 96x fewer trainable parameters than end-to-end training. We also explain the prior works' robustness observations.

中文摘要翻译: 由于开放词汇目标检测器（OV-ODs）架构复杂，真实世界噪声对其影响仍知之甚少。我们提出了综合性的分析框架Robust Onion，通过可控合成视觉退化逐层"剥离"OV-ODs，揭示了鲁棒性如何、为何以及何处下降，并系统性分析了特征坍缩。我们的发现表明，视觉骨干网络相似的模型表现出相似的鲁棒性，这是由相似层上的相似特征坍缩所驱动的，而预训练策略、架构细节和文本监督等因素贡献甚微。鲁棒性主要由图像域而非标注决定，这解释了COCO和LVIS上相似的鲁棒性影响，以及ODinW-13等数据集因包含大尺寸孤立目标而给人以鲁棒性被高估的印象。最后，我们通过轻量级即插即用方法NN&TK0，在真实场景BDD100K、WiderFace和VisDRONE上验证了我们的见解，该方法使用的可训练参数比端到端训练少96倍。我们还对先前工作中的鲁棒性观察结果进行了解释。

</details>


### [39] [Do Image Editing Models Understand Lighting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26738)
*Tim Küchler,Johann-Friedrich Feiden,Matthias Nießner,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DLP基准，通过采集真实世界灯光变化的高动态范围图像数据集，评估了多种图像编辑模型在物理光照理解方面的能力，发现最佳模型已较为符合物理规律但仍存在提升空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准要么依赖人工判断或视觉语言模型评估感知合理性，要么使用合成数据集，缺乏对生成图像编辑模型是否真正理解真实世界光照的量化评估。

Method: 提出3D锚定光照探针(3DLP)基准，采集了1000对真实室内场景的高动态范围图像（灯光物理开关），标注了特定区域如投影阴影和金属表面，并设计了两个新评分来补偿AI生成的摄影效果（如白平衡调整）。

Result: 不同模型性能差异显著，镜面高光区域差异较小；最佳模型与真实物理一致性较好，但仍有改进空间；所有模型在光照探针照射较弱的区域更易出错；视觉语言模型不适用于像素级光照传输分析。

Conclusion: 现有图像编辑模型对真实物理光照的理解已有不错表现但不够完美，光照较弱区域仍是难点，视觉语言模型无法胜任像素级光照分析任务。

Original Abstract: While recent advancements in generative image editing models have achieved stunning visual fidelity, it remains an open question whether these systems possess an intrinsic knowledge of real-world lighting. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate high-level plausibility of perceptual light transport on curated internet imagery, using VLMs or human judgement, or they rely on synthetically generated datasets. In this work, we introduce the 3D-anchored Light Probe (3DLP) benchmark, for which we have captured a new high-fidelity HDR dataset of real-world lighting changes. The dataset consists of 1K image pairs of diverse indoor scenery in which light probes are physically turned on and off. To allow for a granular performance analysis, we annotated specific image regions such as cast shadows or metallic surfaces. With this data, we evaluate a range of state-of-the-art image editing models by measuring how well their light probe edits align with reality. The evaluation uses two new scores to compensate for AI-generated photographic effects, such as adjusted white balance. Our results show that the overall performance of models differs considerably, with differences slightly less pronounced for specular highlights. The best image editing models are remarkably consistent with real-world physics, however, they still leave room for improvement. We observe that image regions that receive less light from the light probe are more prone to errors for all models. Furthermore, building on their success in evaluating macroscopic lighting plausibility, we test VLMs on our task but find that they are unsuitable for pixel-level light transport analysis. We will make the benchmark, together with the real-world dataset, publicly available to encourage future research on this topic.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管生成式图像编辑模型的最新进展已实现惊人的视觉保真度，但这些系统是否具备真实世界光照的内在知识仍是一个悬而未决的问题。现有基准通常利用视觉语言模型或人工判断，评估从互联网收集图像的感知光照传输合理性的宏观层面，或者依赖合成生成的数据集。在这项工作中，我们引入了3D锚定光照探针（3DLP）基准，为此我们采集了一个新的高保真度高动态范围真实世界光照变化数据集。该数据集包含1000对多样室内场景的图像对，其中光照探针被物理地打开和关闭。为了实现细粒度的性能分析，我们标注了特定图像区域，如投影阴影和金属表面。利用这些数据，我们通过评估光照探针编辑结果与真实物理的一致程度，来评测一系列最先进的图像编辑模型。该评估使用了两个新评分来补偿由AI生成的摄影效果，如调整后的白平衡。我们的结果表明，模型间的整体性能存在显著差异，镜面高光区域的差异略小。最佳图像编辑模型与真实物理具有显著的一致性，然而，它们仍有改进空间。我们观察到，对于所有模型，从光照探针接收较少光照的图像区域更容易出错。此外，基于视觉语言模型在宏观光照合理性评估中的成功，我们测试了它们在我们任务上的表现，但发现它们不适合进行像素级光照传输分析。我们将公开该基准及其实世界数据集，以鼓励未来在该方向上的研究。

</details>


### [40] [LiveEdit: Towards Real-Time Diffusion-Based Streaming Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26740)
*Xinyu Wang,Chongbo Zhao,Fangneng Zhan,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种流式视频编辑框架，通过三阶段蒸馏将双向模型能力迁移至单向流式编辑器，并引入面向AR的掩码缓存加速推理，实现了12.66 FPS的实时编辑性能，同时保持背景稳定和内容保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流式视频编辑方法难以同时满足长时间背景稳定、非编辑区域保持以及实时低延迟的要求；而流式视频生成方法因缺乏严格的内容保真和区域控制，无法直接用于编辑任务。

Method: 设计了一个三阶段蒸馏流水线，逐步将强大的双向基础模型的编辑能力迁移到高效的单向流式编辑器中；同时引入面向AR的掩码缓存机制，跨帧复用与区域相关的计算，减少冗余处理以加速推理。

Result: 在流式基线方法中达到了最先进的视觉质量，推理速度大幅提升至12.66 FPS，适用于交互式和增强现实应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在保持高视觉保真度的同时实现了实时流式视频编辑，为交互式AR等场景提供了可行的部署方案。

Original Abstract: Streaming video editing has made rapid progress, yet practical deployment is still limited by two core issues: maintaining stable backgrounds and non-edited regions over time, and achieving the low latency required for real-time interactive scenarios. Meanwhile, recent streaming video generation methods are mostly developed for synthesis and cannot be directly applied to editing due to the strict preservation requirement and region-specific control. In this work, we present a novel streaming video editing framework that performs causal, frame-by-frame editing with strong content preservation and real-time responsiveness. Our key design is a three-stage distillation pipeline that progressively transfers editing capability from a powerful bidirectional foundation model to an efficient unidirectional streaming editor, enabling stable long-horizon edits without sacrificing visual fidelity. To further support real-time deployment, we introduce an AR-oriented mask cache that reuses region-related computation across frames, substantially reducing redundant processing and accelerating inference. Finally, we establish a dedicated benchmark for streaming video editing. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art visual quality among streaming baselines while drastically boosting inference speed to 12.66 FPS, making it suitable for interactive and augmented reality applications.

中文摘要翻译: 流式视频编辑已取得快速进展，但实际部署仍受限于两个核心问题：长时间维持背景及非编辑区域的稳定性，以及实现实时交互场景所需的低延迟。同时，现有的流式视频生成方法大多为合成任务开发，由于严格的内容保真要求和区域特异性控制，无法直接应用于编辑。本文提出一种新颖的流式视频编辑框架，以因果方式逐帧执行编辑，兼具强内容保持能力和实时响应性。我们的关键设计是一个三阶段蒸馏流水线，逐步将强大的双向基础模型的编辑能力迁移到高效的单向流式编辑器中，从而在不牺牲视觉保真度的前提下实现稳定的长时域编辑。为进一步支持实时部署，我们引入一种面向增强现实的掩码缓存机制，跨帧复用与区域相关的计算，大幅减少冗余处理并加速推理。最后，我们建立了一个专用的流式视频编辑基准。大量实验表明，我们的方法在流式基线中达到了最先进的视觉质量，同时将推理速度大幅提升至12.66 FPS，使其适用于交互式和增强现实应用。

</details>


### [41] [Depth-Semantic Alignment and Affinity-Guided Fusion for Structured Radar Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26743)
*Amjad Hussain,Xin Qiu,Fuyuan Ai,Yuchen Tan,Zecheng Li,Chunyi Song,Wenjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉-雷达融合的多模态点云生成方法，利用图像语义信息对雷达点云进行结构约束和空间对齐，并采用稀疏补全策略提升点密度和恢复缺失结构，从而改善毫米波雷达点云稀疏、噪声大、结构不完整的问题，实验证明该方法能有效提升点云质量及下游感知任务的检测精度和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 毫米波雷达点云通常稀疏、噪声大且结构不完整，严重影响了基于点云的目标检测与跟踪等下游感知任务的性能。

Method: 提出一种基于视觉-雷达融合的多模态点云生成方法，利用图像语义信息对雷达点云施加结构约束并进行空间对齐，同时采用稀疏补全策略增强点云密度并恢复缺失结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法有效提升了点云质量，并提高了感知模型在复杂环境下的检测精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法为多传感器点云生成和智能感知系统提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Point clouds are an important carrier of three-dimensional spatial information, and their quality directly affects the performance of downstream perception tasks such as object detection and tracking. However, millimeter-wave radar point clouds are typically sparse, noisy, and structurally incomplete. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a multimodal point cloud generation method based on vision-radar fusion. The proposed method leverages image semantic information to impose structural constraints and achieve spatial alignment for radar point clouds, while incorporating a sparse completion strategy to enhance point density and recover missing structures. The generated point clouds are further evaluated in object detection and tracking tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves point cloud quality and enhances the detection accuracy and robustness of perception models in complex environments, providing a practical solution for multisensor point cloud generation and intelligent perception systems.

中文摘要翻译: 点云是三维空间信息的重要载体，其质量直接影响目标检测与跟踪等下游感知任务的性能。然而，毫米波雷达点云通常稀疏、噪声大且结构不完整。针对这些局限性，本文提出了一种基于视觉-雷达融合的多模态点云生成方法。该方法利用图像语义信息对雷达点云施加结构约束并进行空间对齐，同时采用稀疏补全策略增强点云密度并恢复缺失结构。生成的点云进一步在目标检测与跟踪任务中进行了评估。实验结果表明，所提方法能够有效提升点云质量，增强感知模型在复杂环境下的检测精度和鲁棒性，为多传感器点云生成及智能感知系统提供了一种实用的解决方案。

</details>


### [42] [Capacity-Controlled Multi-View Stylization of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26754)
*Zhihao Wen,Yixin Yang,Bojian Wu,Yang Zhou,Dani Lischinski,Daniel Cohen-Or,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输的容量可控框架，通过半平衡最优传输和列容量约束解决3D高斯泼溅中多视角风格迁移的不一致和特征重用问题，实现跨视角风格连贯的3D场景风格化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D风格化方法通常对每个渲染视图独立应用2D特征匹配损失，导致风格分配不稳定、多对一特征重用以及跨视角一致性不足的问题。

Method: (1) 将局部风格匹配重构为半平衡最优传输问题，引入可调强度的列容量约束来缓解多对一匹配并实现可控风格特征分配；(2) 提出跨视角匹配引导机制增强跨视角一致性；(3) 引入多种几何正则化增强3DGS，使优化后的高斯基元能表示更精细的纹理。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著提升了多视角风格一致性，生成了稳定且富有表现力的3D风格化结果，同时保留了场景的核心语义结构。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的容量控制框架能有效解决多视角风格迁移中的分配不稳定和一致性不足问题，为3DGS风格化提供了一种原则性的特征覆盖与风格多样性平衡机制。

Original Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an efficient and explicit representation for novel view synthesis, enforcing stylistic coherence across viewpoints remains challenging. Existing 3D stylization methods typically apply 2D feature-matching losses independently per rendered view, which leads to unstable style allocation, many-to-one feature reuse, and limited cross-view consistency. We propose a capacity-controlled framework for multi-view stylization of 3DGS, grounded in optimal transport. Specifically, we reformulate local style matching as a semi-balanced optimal transport problem. By introducing explicit column-capacity constraints with tunable strength, our formulation mitigates many-to-one matching and enables controllable allocation of style features. This transport-based objective provides a principled mechanism for balancing feature coverage and stylistic diversity while maintaining stable correspondences across viewpoints. To further enhance cross-view coherence, we incorporate a novel cross-view matching guidance to constrain correspondences between scene content and style patterns. In addition, we introduce several geometric regularizations to enhance the vanilla 3DGS, thereby enabling optimized Gaussian primitives to represent finer-grained textures during stylization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves multi-view stylistic consistency and produces stable, expressive 3D stylizations while preserving the core semantic structure of the scene.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）为新视角合成提供了高效且显式的表示方法，但跨视角强制执行风格一致性仍然具有挑战性。现有的3D风格化方法通常对每个渲染视图独立应用2D特征匹配损失，这导致了不稳定的风格分配、多对一特征重用以及有限的跨视角一致性。我们提出了一种基于最优传输的容量可控框架，用于3DGS的多视角风格化。具体而言，我们将局部风格匹配重构为半平衡最优传输问题。通过引入具有可调强度的显式列容量约束，我们的公式缓解了多对一匹配问题，并实现了风格特征的可控分配。这种基于传输的目标提供了一种原则性机制，用于平衡特征覆盖与风格多样性，同时保持跨视角的稳定对应关系。为了进一步增强跨视角一致性，我们引入了一种新颖的跨视角匹配引导机制，以约束场景内容与风格模式之间的对应关系。此外，我们引入了多种几何正则化来增强原始3DGS，从而使优化后的高斯基元在风格化过程中能够表示更细粒度的纹理。大量实验表明，我们的方法显著提高了多视角风格一致性，生成了稳定且富有表现力的3D风格化结果，同时保留了场景的核心语义结构。

</details>


### [43] [ProtoKV: Streaming Video Understanding under Delayed Query with Summary-State Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26762)
*Le Tu Ngoc Minh,Jinyeong Lim,Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoKV 提出了一种恒定显存占用的流视频理解记忆方法，通过原型KV缓存将远距离历史压缩为固定容量的语义-空间原型状态，在长延迟查询场景下比token保留基线方法准确率提升高达12.5个点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 流视频理解中，异步到达的查询可能错过早期出现的决定性视觉线索，而有限GPU内存下后续大量token更新会导致这些关键线索被驱逐或稀释，形成"延迟查询"问题。

Method: ProtoKV 采用双区设计：保留精确的近窗口KV缓存，同时将更早的历史内容聚合成固定容量的语义-空间原型库（含残差统计信息）。查询时通过有界伪token接口暴露每个原型，可无缝替换标准注意力机制。

Result: 在同等预算和查询时延下，ProtoKV 在长延迟场景的SVU基准测试中比token保留基线方法准确率提升最高12.5个百分点，且增益随查询延迟增加而扩大。

Conclusion: ProtoKV 通过原型化远距离记忆，在严格内存和延迟约束下有效解决了流视频理解中的延迟查询问题，实现了精度与效率的帕累托改进。

Original Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) must answer queries that arrive asynchronously while visual tokens stream continuously under strict GPU-memory and query-time latency budgets. A key challenge is delayed query: decisive cues may appear briefly, yet many subsequent updates occur before the query arrives, increasing the risk that those cues are evicted or diluted under bounded memory. We propose ProtoKV, a constant-footprint SVU memory that represents far history as a fixed-capacity summary state rather than retaining token instances. ProtoKV keeps an exact near-window KV cache and aggregates older content into a semantic-spatial prototype bank with residual statistics. At query time, each prototype is exposed through a bounded pseudo-token interface that is drop-in compatible with standard attention. Under matched budgets and comparable query-time cost, ProtoKV improves accuracy by up to 12.5 points over token-retention baselines on SVU benchmarks in the long-delay regime, with gains that grow as query delay increases.

中文摘要翻译: 流视频理解（SVU）必须在视觉token持续流入的同时，在严格的GPU内存和查询时延预算下，回答异步到达的查询。其中一个关键挑战是延迟查询：决定性线索可能短暂出现，但在查询到达之前会发生大量后续更新，这增加了在有限内存下这些线索被驱逐或稀释的风险。我们提出 ProtoKV，一种恒定显存占用的SVU记忆方法，它将远距离历史表示为固定容量的摘要状态而非保留token实例。ProtoKV 保留精确的近窗口KV缓存，并将更早的内容聚合成一个带有残差统计信息的语义-空间原型库。在查询时，每个原型通过一个有界伪token接口暴露，该接口可无缝替换标准注意力机制。在同等预算和可比的查询时延成本下，ProtoKV 在长延迟场景的SVU基准测试中比token保留基线方法的准确率提升高达12.5个百分点，且增益随查询延迟增加而扩大。

</details>


### [44] [Calibrated Harmonic Overlaid Implicit Neural Representations for Multi-Dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26763)
*Honghang Chen,Xiujun Zhang,Xiaoli Sun,Mingqing Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CHOIR，一种基于校准谐波叠加的隐式神经表示方法，通过Coordinated Harmonic Superposition替代传统函数组合以保证深层网络优化稳定性，并引入Perceptual Spectrum Calibration嵌入自然图像幂律谱先验以缓解频谱偏差，在多维数据恢复任务上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于周期激活函数（如Sine）的隐式神经表示方法主要依赖函数组合，随着网络深度增加会导致优化不稳定，从而限制性能；同时，这些方法缺乏适当的物理先验来有效缓解频谱偏差问题。

Method: 提出CHOIR框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Coordinated Harmonic Superposition (CHS)：用协调谐波叠加替代传统函数组合，保证网络深度扩展时的优化稳定性；2) Perceptual Spectrum Calibration (PSC)：嵌入自然图像的幂律谱先验，将全局固定的频谱校准为物理上合理的对数均匀分布，以缓解频谱偏差。

Result: 在多维数据恢复任务上的大量实验表明，该方法在性能上优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 通过理论启发于深度周期网络与广义傅里叶级数的共性，所提出的CHOIR有效解决了深层周期网络的优化不稳定性和频谱偏差问题，为多维数据表示提供了一种更优的隐式神经表示方法。

Original Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) has emerged as a powerful prior for multi-dimensional data (e.g., multispectral images and videos). However, most INR methods employing periodic activation functions (e.g., Sine) predominantly rely on function composition. This mechanism introduces optimization instability as network depth increases, thereby limiting their performance. Meanwhile, these methods fail to incorporate proper physical priors to effectively alleviate spectrum bias. To address these issues, inspired by the commonalities between deep periodic networks and generalized Fourier series, we propose a novel Calibrated Harmonic Overlaid Implicit Neural Representation (CHOIR). Specifically, we utilize Coordinated Harmonic Superposition (CHS) to replace the conventional function composition used in most INRs, thereby ensuring optimization stability when scaling network depth. Furthermore, we introduce a Perceptual Spectrum Calibration (PSC) to mitigate spectrum bias. This calibration embeds the ubiquitous power-law spectrum prior of natural images and adjusts the globally fixed spectrum towards a physically plausible log-uniform distribution. Extensive experiments on various multidimensional data recovery problems demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/chorl0229/CHOIR.

中文摘要翻译: 隐式神经表示（INR）已成为多维数据（如多光谱图像和视频）的一种强大先验。然而，大多数采用周期激活函数（如Sine）的INR方法主要依赖函数组合。这种机制随着网络深度增加会引入优化不稳定性，从而限制其性能。同时，这些方法未能融入适当的物理先验来有效缓解频谱偏差。为解决这些问题，受深度周期网络与广义傅里叶级数之间共性的启发，我们提出了一种新颖的校准谐波叠加隐式神经表示（CHOIR）。具体而言，我们利用协调谐波叠加（CHS）替代大多数INR中使用的传统函数组合，从而在扩展网络深度时保证优化稳定性。此外，我们引入了感知频谱校准（PSC）来缓解频谱偏差。这种校准嵌入了自然图像普遍存在的幂律谱先验，并将全局固定的频谱校准为物理上合理的对数均匀分布。在各种多维数据恢复问题上的大量实验表明，我们的方法实现了优于最先进方法的性能。代码可在 https://github.com/chorl0229/CHOIR 获取。

</details>


### [45] [Anatomy-Guided Residual Motion Diffusion for Controllable 4D Cardiac MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26764)
*Yiheng Cao,Gustavo Andrade-Miranda,Jiatian Zhang,Lingxiao Zhao,Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于4D医学影像语义一致数据增强的可控生成框架，通过半监督VAE和级联隐式扩散模型解耦解剖结构与时间动态，在心电影像上验证了高可控性和时序一致性，并能显著提升跨厂商下游分割任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 4D医学影像（3D+时间）建模面临标注数据稀缺、跨设备域偏移和隐私限制三大挑战，现有数据增强方法难以生成解剖结构一致且时序连贯的合成数据。

Method: 先利用半监督VAE联合学习解剖体积的紧凑隐表示和对齐分割掩膜；再通过级联隐式扩散模型（LDM）解耦解剖结构与时间动态：静态LDM基于临床先验（诊断、体积测量）生成个体解剖结构，运动LDM估计残差隐式运动，确保4D序列的严格时序一致性。

Result: 在心电影像上，静态解剖控制相关性Pearson r>0.8，时序一致性FVD=288.08；跨厂商泛化实验中，使用合成数据增强训练集后nnU-Net分割的平均Dice提升1.4%，Hausdorff距离降低3.0mm，左心室Dice提升2.8%，边界误差降低5.4mm。

Conclusion: 该框架为4D医学图像合成提供了可扩展且可控的解决方案，能够在标注有限和跨厂商变异性下支持更鲁棒模型的开发。

Original Abstract: Developing robust artificial intelligence models for 4D (3D + time) medical imaging is constrained by limited annotated data, inter-device domain shifts, and privacy restrictions. To address this, we propose a 4D controllable generative framework for anatomically consistent data augmentation. A semi-supervised variational autoencoder learns a compact latent representation of anatomical volumes while jointly predicting aligned segmentation masks in a unified framework. Anatomical structure is then disentangled from temporal dynamics through a cascaded latent diffusion model (LDM). A static LDM generates subject-specific anatomy conditioned on clinical priors (diagnosis and volumes measures) and a subsequent motion LDM estimates residual latent motions, ensuring strict temporal coherence across the 4D sequence. The proposed approach was evaluated on cine cardiac MRI as a representative 4D imaging application. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate high controllability of static anatomy (Pearson r > 0.8) and strong temporal coherence (FVD = 288.08). In cross-vendor generalization experiments, augmenting training sets with synthetic 4D sequences significantly improves downstream segmentation performance. Using nnU-Net, the proposed augmentation strategy improves the average Dice score by 1.4% and reduces the Hausdorff Distance by 3.0mm compared to training on real data alone, for the left ventricle, Dice improves by 2.8% with a 5.4mm reduction in boundary error. Overall, this framework provides a scalable and controllable solution for 4D medical image synthesis, supporting the development of more robust models with limited annotations and cross-vendor variability. Code available on https://github.com/cyiheng/4DCardiacMRISynthesis.

中文摘要翻译: 开发用于4D（3D+时间）医学影像的鲁棒人工智能模型受到标注数据有限、跨设备域偏移和隐私限制的制约。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种用于解剖一致数据增强的4D可控生成框架。一个半监督变分自编码器学习解剖体积的紧凑隐表示，同时在统一框架中联合预测对齐的分割掩膜。随后通过级联隐式扩散模型（LDM）将解剖结构从时间动态中解耦。静态LDM基于临床先验（诊断和体积测量）生成个体特异性解剖结构，后续的运动LDM估计残差隐式运动，确保4D序列的严格时序一致性。所提方法以电影心脏MRI作为代表性4D成像应用进行评估。跨多个数据集的实验表明该方法具有高静态解剖可控性（Pearson r>0.8）和强时序一致性（FVD=288.08）。在跨厂商泛化实验中，使用合成4D序列增强训练集显著提升了下游分割性能。使用nnU-Net时，与仅使用真实数据训练相比，所提增强策略将平均Dice分数提升了1.4%，将Hausdorff距离降低了3.0mm；对于左心室，Dice提升了2.8%，边界误差降低了5.4mm。总体而言，该框架为4D医学图像合成提供了可扩展且可控的解决方案，支持在标注有限和跨厂商变异性下开发更鲁棒的模型。代码地址：https://github.com/cyiheng/4DCardiacMRISynthesis。

</details>


### [46] [LearniBridge: Learnable Calibration of Feature Caching for Diffusion Models Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26778)
*Xuyue Huang,Zhe Chen,Wang Shen,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LearniBridge 提出了一种可学习的校准机制，通过轻量级 LoRA 更新桥接多个时间步，在扩散 Transformer 的特征缓存中实现高效校准，仅需 3-5 个训练样本即可在高加速比下显著减少误差累积，在 FLUX、HunyuanVideo 和 WAN2.1 上分别实现了高达 5.87×、5.75× 和 4.10× 的加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有特征缓存方法依赖历史特征进行加速，实现简单但在高加速比下存在严重的误差累积问题。本文旨在探索最优校正更新的本质，解决这一局限性。

Method: 首先理论分析证明最优校准更新具有跨不同提示共享的低秩子空间结构，进而提出 LearniBridge——一种可学习的校准机制，通过轻量级 LoRA 更新桥接多个时间步，仅需 3-5 个训练样本即可实现有效校准。

Result: 在图像和视频生成任务上，LearniBridge 在 FLUX、HunyuanVideo 和 WAN2.1 上分别实现高达 5.87×、5.75× 和 4.10× 的加速；在 WAN2.1 上，以 4.10× 加速时 VBench 相比先前 SOTA 提升 1.28%。

Conclusion: LearniBridge 通过可学习的低秩校准机制有效解决了特征缓存在高加速比下的误差累积问题，以极少训练样本实现了显著的加速效果，并在多个主流扩散 Transformer 模型上验证了其有效性和泛化性。

Original Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have driven substantial progress in image and video generation but suffer from prohibitive computational costs. Feature caching accelerates inference by reusing intermediate representations. Existing methods rely on historical features for implementation simplicity, yet suffer from severe error accumulation at high acceleration ratios. To address this limitation, we investigate the nature of the requisite feature correction. We demonstrate that the optimal calibration update is characterized by a shared low-rank subspace across diverse prompts. Guided by this structural insight, we propose LearniBridge, a learnable calibration mechanism for feature caching that bridges multiple timesteps through lightweight LoRA updates. This mechanism enables effective calibration requiring only 3-5 training samples. Extensive experiments on image and video generation show that LearniBridge achieves up to $5.87\times$, $5.75\times$, and $4.10\times$ acceleration on FLUX, HunyuanVideo, and WAN2.1, respectively. On WAN2.1, it improves VBench by 1.28% over the previous SOTA at $4.10\times$ acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/Iiiiiiirene/LearniBridge.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散 Transformer（DiTs）在图像和视频生成领域取得了显著进展，但面临着高昂的计算成本。特征缓存通过重用中间表示来加速推理。现有方法依赖历史特征，实现简单，但在高加速比下存在严重的误差累积问题。为解决这一局限，我们研究了所需特征校正的本质。我们证明，最优校准更新具有跨不同提示共享的低秩子空间结构。基于这一结构洞见，我们提出了 LearniBridge——一种用于特征缓存的可学习校准机制，通过轻量级 LoRA 更新桥接多个时间步。该机制仅需 3-5 个训练样本即可实现有效校准。在图像和视频生成上的大量实验表明，LearniBridge 在 FLUX、HunyuanVideo 和 WAN2.1 上分别实现了高达 5.87×、5.75× 和 4.10× 的加速。在 WAN2.1 上，以 4.10× 加速时，其 VBench 相比先前最先进方法提升了 1.28%。我们的代码开源在 https://github.com/Iiiiiiirene/LearniBridge。

</details>


### [47] [ReasonCLIP-58M: Visually Grounded Commonsense Reasoning Supervision for CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26794)
*Sicheng Zhang,Muzammal Naseer,Binzhu Xie,Naufal Suryanto,Shi Qiu,Jamal Bentahar,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReasonCLIP-58M 提出了一种持续预训练框架，通过两阶段策略将大规模推理监督融入CLIP风格模型，在不改变架构的情况下提升视觉基础常识推理和组合推理能力，同时增强零样本检索性能，并在多模态大语言模型中作为即插即用视觉编码器带来一致增益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管CLIP及其变体被广泛用作多模态系统的视觉骨干，但其预训练主要依赖描述性图像-文本对齐。下游应用日益需要视觉基础的常识推理和组合推理能力，但尚不清楚CLIP风格编码器能否在不改变架构的情况下支持此类推理。

Method: 提出ReasonCLIP-58M持续预训练框架，包含两阶段策略：第一阶段渐进整合推理信号同时保持描述性对齐，第二阶段引入类别结构化的推理监督。为此构建了两个互补数据集（ReasonLite-42M：开放形式、视觉可验证的推理描述；ReasonPro-16M：类别特定推理监督）及诊断基准RCLIP-Bench。

Result: ReasonCLIP提升了视觉基础常识推理和组合推理能力，同时增强了零样本检索性能；作为LLaVA-NeXT等多模态大语言模型的即插即用视觉编码器，在不增加推理成本的情况下带来一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的推理监督能够增强CLIP风格视觉表示的表达能力，表明无需改变架构即可通过持续的推理预训练使CLIP模型支持更复杂的视觉推理任务。

Original Abstract: CLIP and its variants are widely adopted visual backbones in multimodal systems, but their pretraining remains dominated by descriptive image-text alignment. As downstream applications increasingly demand visually grounded commonsense inference and compositional reasoning, it remains unclear whether CLIP-style encoders can support such reasoning without architectural changes. To address this, we present ReasonCLIP-58M, a continual pretraining framework that integrates large-scale reasoning supervision into CLIP-style models through our two-stage strategy, which progressively integrates reasoning signals while preserving descriptive alignment, followed by category-structured reasoning supervision. To support this framework, we construct two complementary datasets and a benchmark: ReasonLite-42M, with open-form, visually verifiable reasoning captions; ReasonPro-16M, with category-specific reasoning supervision; and RCLIP-Bench for diagnostic evaluation of visually grounded reasoning. We train a family of ReasonCLIP that improves visually grounded commonsense and compositional reasoning while also enhancing zero-shot retrieval performance. As a drop-in visual encoder for multimodal large language models such as LLaVA-NeXT, ReasonCLIP delivers consistent gains without additional inference cost, demonstrating that structured reasoning supervision enhances the expressive capacity of CLIP-style visual representations. All datasets, models, and training code are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/ReasonCLIP.

中文摘要翻译: CLIP及其变体被广泛用作多模态系统中的视觉骨干，但其预训练仍主要由描述性图像-文本对齐主导。随着下游应用日益需求视觉基础的常识推理和组合推理，目前尚不清楚CLIP风格的编码器能否在不进行架构更改的情况下支持此类推理。为解决这一问题，我们提出了ReasonCLIP-58M，这是一个持续预训练框架，通过我们的两阶段策略将大规模推理监督整合到CLIP风格模型中——该策略逐步整合推理信号同时保持描述性对齐，随后引入类别结构化的推理监督。为支撑该框架，我们构建了两个互补数据集和一个基准：ReasonLite-42M（包含开放形式、视觉可验证的推理描述）、ReasonPro-16M（包含类别特定的推理监督）以及用于视觉基础推理诊断评估的RCLIP-Bench。我们训练了ReasonCLIP系列模型，该模型不仅提升了视觉基础常识推理和组合推理能力，还增强了零样本检索性能。作为多模态大语言模型（如LLaVA-NeXT）的即插即用视觉编码器，ReasonCLIP在不增加推理成本的情况下带来一致性能提升，这表明结构化推理监督增强了CLIP风格视觉表示的表达能力。所有数据集、模型和训练代码均可从https://github.com/RISys-Lab/ReasonCLIP获取。

</details>


### [48] [NaviCache: Test-Time Self-Calibration Caching for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26795)
*Zheqi Lv,Zhibo Zhu,Jinke Wang,Qi Tian,Shengyu Zhang,Zhengyu Chen,Chengxi Zang,Zhou Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NaviCache，一种即插即用的测试时自校准方法，将扩散模型中的特征演化重构思为惯性导航系统（INS）问题，通过双状态估计架构实现自适应计算跳过，在多个视频扩散模型上取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型计算成本高昂，离线校准方法依赖校准数据、耗时长且对分布偏移敏感；离线免校准方法依赖瞬时零阶近似，对观测噪声敏感且忽略了扩散轨迹中的内在动量。

Method: 将特征演化重构思为惯性导航系统（INS）问题，建模输入输出变化之间的相对耦合关系；提出双状态估计架构，自适应跟踪特征变化比及其潜在漂移；通过初始对齐阶段初始化，结合时间相关噪声调度和不确定性感知的测量更新机制，实现有理论保证的误差有界计算跳过。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo、Wan和Open-Sora系列模型上的大量实验表明，NaviCache在计算跳过时具有更准确的误差判断，并取得了优异的综合性能。

Conclusion: NaviCache作为一种即插即用的测试时自校准方法，通过惯性导航系统视角有效解决了视频扩散模型加速中的分布偏移和噪声敏感问题，为计算跳过提供了理论保证。

Original Abstract: Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) is constrained by immense computational costs. While offline calibration-based acceleration suffers from calibration data dependency, prohibitive calibration duration, and susceptibility to distribution shifts, offline calibration-free methods eliminate these hurdles. However, since they rely on instantaneous zero-order approximations where the mapping between input and output differences varies in real-time, they are susceptible to observational noise and ignore the intrinsic momentum within the diffusion trajectory. In this paper, we propose NaviCache, a plug-and-play test-time self-calibration method re-conceptualizing feature evolution as an Inertial Navigation System (INS) problem. NaviCache bridges the fundamental domain gap and the non-stationary nature of diffusion by modeling the relative coupling between input and output variations. We introduce a dual-state estimation architecture that adaptively tracks the feature change ratio and its latent drift, initialized via a specialized Initial Alignment phase. By integrating a time-dependent noise schedule with an uncertainty-aware Measurement Update mechanism, NaviCache provides a theoretically grounded mechanism for error-bounded computation skipping. Extensive experiments on the HunyuanVideo, Wan, and Open-Sora series demonstrate that NaviCache exhibits more accurate error judgment for computation skipping and achieves outstanding comprehensive performance.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型（VDMs）受到巨大计算成本的制约。虽然基于离线校准的加速方法存在校准数据依赖、校准时间过长以及对分布偏移敏感的问题，离线免校准方法消除了这些障碍。然而，由于这些方法依赖于输入输出差异之间映射关系实时变化的瞬时零阶近似，它们容易受到观测噪声的影响，并且忽略了扩散轨迹中的内在动量。在本文中，我们提出了NaviCache，一种即插即用的测试时自校准方法，将特征演化重构思为惯性导航系统（INS）问题。NaviCache通过对输入输出变化之间的相对耦合进行建模，弥合了基础领域差距和扩散的非平稳特性。我们引入了一种双状态估计架构，该架构通过专门的初始对齐阶段进行初始化，自适应地跟踪特征变化比及其潜在漂移。通过将时间相关噪声调度与不确定性感知的测量更新机制相结合，NaviCache为误差有界计算跳过提供了有理论依据的机制。在HunyuanVideo、Wan和Open-Sora系列上的大量实验表明，NaviCache在计算跳过时展现出更准确的误差判断，并取得了出色的综合性能。

</details>


### [49] [Multi-modality Image Fusion under Adverse Weather: Mask-Guided Feature Restoration and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26812)
*Xilai Li,Xiaosong Li,Haishu Tan,Tao Ye,Huafeng Li,Hongbin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种掩码引导的多模态图像融合方法（AMG-Fuse），通过引入伪地面真值、掩码生成机制和掩码引导的交叉注意力，在恶劣天气下同时实现特征恢复与跨模态互补，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MMIF方法在恶劣天气条件下难以同时进行特征恢复和跨模态互补表示学习，导致实际性能受限。

Method: (1) 引入"伪地面真值"简化训练，加速特征学习；(2) 设计掩码生成机制，基于融合结果与源图像的映射关系量化各模态的相对贡献；(3) 提出掩码引导的跨模态交叉注意力机制，选择性地关注信息量大的特征；(4) 提出掩码引导学习策略和任务耦合的退化感知学习策略，平衡特征恢复与交互。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法在视觉质量、定量指标和下游任务上均超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的掩码引导MMIF框架能有效应对恶劣天气对多模态图像融合的挑战，为实际应用提供了更强的鲁棒性和性能。

Original Abstract: Multi-modality image fusion (MMIF) enhances scene representation by exploiting complementary cues from different modalities. Adverse weather, however, causes significant image degradation, disrupting feature representation and requiring simultaneous feature restoration and cross-modal complementarity. Existing methods often struggle with effective representation learning under such conditions, limiting their practical performance. To address these challenges, we propose a mask-guided MMIF method that integrates feature restoration and interaction. We first introduce "Pseudo Ground Truth" to simplify training, promoting faster and more effective feature learning. Then, we design a mask generation mechanism based on the mapping relationship between the fused result and the source images, quantifying the relative contribution of each modality during the fusion process. By incorporating the proposed mask-guided cross-modal cross-attention mechanism, the network is encouraged to selectively attend to informative features during modality interaction, mitigating the risk of overfitting to the static distribution of the "Pseudo Ground Truth". Additionally, we propose a mask-guided learning strategy and a task-coupled degradation-aware learning strategy to balance feature restoration and interaction. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method surpasses state-of-the-art approaches in visual quality, quantitative metrics, and downstream tasks. The source code is available at https://github.com/ixilai/AMG-Fuse.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态图像融合（MMIF）通过利用不同模态的互补线索来增强场景表示。然而，恶劣天气会导致图像严重退化，破坏特征表示，并需要同时进行特征恢复和跨模态互补。现有方法在这种条件下往往难以进行有效的表示学习，限制了其实用性能。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种掩码引导的MMIF方法，融合了特征恢复与交互。我们首先引入"伪地面真值"以简化训练，促进更快、更有效的特征学习。然后，我们设计了一种基于融合结果与源图像之间映射关系的掩码生成机制，量化融合过程中每种模态的相对贡献。通过引入所提出的掩码引导的跨模态交叉注意力机制，鼓励网络在模态交互过程中选择性地关注信息丰富的特征，降低过拟合到"伪地面真值"静态分布的风险。此外，我们提出了一种掩码引导的学习策略和一种任务耦合的退化感知学习策略，以平衡特征恢复与交互。在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在视觉质量、定量指标和下游任务上均超越了当前最先进的方法。源代码可在 https://github.com/ixilai/AMG-Fuse 获取。

</details>


### [50] [Learning Adversarial Augmentation Policies for Robust Garlic Seedling Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26828)
*Soeun Lee,Chanho Kim,Yeji Kang,YoungKi Hong,Byeongkeun Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对地面户外监测中光照不均导致幼苗检测困难的问题，构建了真实田间大蒜幼苗数据集，并提出基于对抗增强策略学习的鲁棒检测框架，在不增加推理计算开销的前提下，实现了更优的检测与缺苗定位性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有幼苗检测研究多在相对稳定的成像条件（如无人机影像或温室环境）下评估，对地面户外监测中严重且空间异质的光照变化的鲁棒检测探索不足；同时，许多光照鲁棒检测方法依赖额外的增强或特征提取模块，增加了推理时间开销，且未针对幼苗检测及下游缺苗定位任务进行专门设计。

Method: 提出一种基于对抗增强策略学习的光照鲁棒幼苗检测框架。该方法联合优化一个随机增强策略智能体和一个目标检测器，使检测器能在挑战性视觉条件下学习鲁棒表示；引入结构惩罚项，在训练中防止不真实的图像失真，同时鼓励生成有挑战性的增强样本。

Result: 该方法在AP₅₀上达到91.6%，比基线提升0.9个百分点，比此前最佳方法提升0.2个百分点；在下游缺苗定位任务中达到75.0%的精确率和67.0%的F1分数，分别比基线提升4.8和2.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架在复杂户外光照条件下的实际地面农业监测中具有有效性，且不增加额外的推理计算开销。

Original Abstract: Accurate seedling detection during early growth stages is essential for timely replanting and effective crop management in precision agriculture. However, existing studies are mostly evaluated under relatively stable imaging conditions, such as UAV imagery or greenhouse environments, leaving robust detection under severe and spatially heterogeneous illumination in ground-based outdoor monitoring insufficiently explored. In addition, many illumination-robust detection methods rely on additional enhancement or feature-extraction modules, which increase inference-time overhead and are not tailored to seedling detection and downstream missing seedling localization. To address these gaps, we construct a new garlic seedling dataset captured using a ground-based monitoring platform under real outdoor field conditions with highly variable illumination. We further propose an illumination-robust seedling detection framework based on adversarial augmentation policy learning. The proposed method jointly optimizes a stochastic augmentation policy agent and an object detector, enabling the detector to learn robust representations under challenging visual conditions. A structural penalty is introduced to prevent unrealistic distortions while encouraging challenging augmentations during training. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach achieves an AP$_{50}$ of 91.6%, improving the baseline by 0.9 percentage points and outperforming the previous best-performing method by 0.2 percentage points. For downstream missing seedling localization, it achieves 75.0% precision and a 67.0% F1-score, improving the baseline by 4.8 and 2.0 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for practical ground-based agricultural monitoring under complex outdoor lighting conditions without additional inference-time computational overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 在精准农业中，幼苗生长早期的准确检测对于及时补种和有效作物管理至关重要。然而，现有研究大多在相对稳定的成像条件下进行评估（如无人机影像或温室环境），地面户外监测中严重且空间异质光照条件下的鲁棒检测尚未得到充分探索。此外，许多光照鲁棒检测方法依赖于额外的增强或特征提取模块，增加了推理时间开销，且未针对幼苗检测及下游缺苗定位进行专门设计。为解决上述问题，我们构建了一个新的利用地面监测平台在真实户外田间高度可变光照条件下采集的大蒜幼苗数据集。我们进一步提出了一种基于对抗增强策略学习的光照鲁棒幼苗检测框架。该方法联合优化一个随机增强策略智能体和一个目标检测器，使检测器能够在挑战性视觉条件下学习鲁棒表示。引入结构惩罚项，在训练过程中防止不真实的失真，同时鼓励具有挑战性的增强。大量实验表明，所提方法达到91.6%的AP₅₀，比基线提升0.9个百分点，并超越此前最佳方法0.2个百分点。在下游缺苗定位任务中，该方法达到75.0%的精确率和67.0%的F1分数，分别比基线提升4.8和2.0个百分点。这些结果表明，所提框架在复杂户外光照条件下的实际地面农业监测中是有效的，且不增加额外的推理计算开销。

</details>


### [51] [Identifying the Unknown: Prompt-Free Open Vocabulary Anomaly Recognition for Robot-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26829)
*Philipp Allgeuer,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnomNOVIC 是一个两阶段框架，结合异常检测MAE和开放词汇图像分类器NOVIC，实现无预定义候选类别的实时开放词汇目标检测，在机器人环境中显著优于YOLO-World-v2、OWLv2等基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器人在开放世界中需要识别未见过的物体，但现有的开放词汇目标检测器要么需要预定义类别提示，要么效率不足以连续部署，缺乏无需提示且高效实用的解决方案。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用基于掩码自编码器（MAE）的异常检测模型生成通用、类别无关的边界框；第二阶段使用NOVIC实时无提示开放词汇图像分类器对边界框内区域进行分类。

Result: 在NICOL机器人桌面环境中，无提示识别达47.1% AP/57.5% AP50；提供类别候选时达59.0% AP/72.5% AP50；在含48个独特物体的野外测试集上达82.6%无提示检测分类准确率，全面超越YOLO-World-v2、OWLv2和YOLOE。

Conclusion: AnomNOVIC在开放词汇目标检测任务上显著超越多种强基线，验证了结合异常检测MAE与无提示开放词汇分类器在机器人场景中的有效性，为连续部署提供了高效、无需提示的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Robots operating in real-world environments must in general be able to recognize previously unseen objects. As robotic systems move toward open-world autonomy, there is a growing, yet largely unmet, need for open vocabulary object detectors that are prompt-free and efficient enough for continuous deployment. We present AnomNOVIC, a two-stage known-workspace framework that combines a masked autoencoder (MAE) trained for anomaly detection, with NOVIC, a powerful real-time prompt-free open vocabulary image classifier. The MAE produces generic object-agnostic bounding boxes, allowing NOVIC to classify salient image regions without requiring a predefined candidate class list. We evaluate AnomNOVIC against strong open vocabulary baselines in a tabletop robot-object environment featuring the NICOL humanoid robot, reaching 47.1% AP / 57.5% AP50 for prompt-free recognition, and 59.0% AP / 72.5% AP50 if class candidates are provided. Across additional datasets, including an in-the-wild test set with 48 unique objects, AnomNOVIC reaches up to 82.6% prompt-free detection and classification accuracy. These results significantly surpass all tested open vocabulary baselines, including YOLO-World-v2, OWLv2, and YOLOE.

中文摘要翻译: 在真实环境中运行的机器人通常需要能够识别未曾见过的物体。随着机器人系统向开放世界自主性发展，对无需提示且足够高效以支持连续部署的开放词汇目标检测器的需求日益增长，但目前仍远未得到满足。我们提出AnomNOVIC，一个两阶段的已知工作空间框架，结合了为异常检测训练的掩码自编码器（MAE）与NOVIC——一个强大的实时无提示开放词汇图像分类器。MAE生成通用的、与类别无关的边界框，使NOVIC能够在无需预定义候选类别列表的情况下对显著图像区域进行分类。我们在以NICOL仿人机器人为特征的桌面机器人物体环境中，将AnomNOVIC与强基线开放词汇模型进行对比评估，在无提示识别任务上达到47.1% AP/57.5% AP50，若提供类别候选则达到59.0% AP/72.5% AP50。在额外数据集（包括包含48个独特物体的野外测试集）上，AnomNOVIC的无提示检测和分类准确率最高可达82.6%。这些结果显著超越了所有测试的开放词汇基线方法，包括YOLO-World-v2、OWLv2和YOLOE。

</details>


### [52] [Liquid Fusion of Heterogeneous Representations Towards General Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26849)
*Ke Chen,Ling Zhou,Guangqi Jiang,Gengshen Wu,Yi Liu,Shoukun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有SSM和CNN模型忽略各自频谱偏置的问题，提出LFNet，通过液态融合网络动态集成VMamba（SSM）和ConvNeXt（CNN）的互补特征，并设计频谱-空间协同的显著性引导上采样算子，在五个通用SOD任务上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SSM和CNN模型在通用显著性目标检测中忽略了各自固有的频谱偏置（spectral biases），即CNN偏向低频而SSM偏向高频，二者的语义表征天然互补但未被充分利用。

Method: 提出液态融合网络（LFNet），采用双骨干架构（VMamba+ConvNeXt）。核心是受液态神经网络（LNN）启发的动态门控融合机制：将连续VMamba特征视为演化状态（evolving states），ConvNeXt特征视为外源刺激（exogenous stimulus），通过状态-刺激范式实现内容感知的特征聚合。同时设计显著性引导上采样（SGU）算子，通过频谱-空间协同设计抑制上采样伪影并保留语义。

Result: 在RGB、RGB-D、RGB-T、VSOD和VDT五个不同任务上达到SOTA性能，实现检测精度与模型效率的优越权衡。

Conclusion: 通过液态融合桥接SSM和CNN的频谱偏置，LFNet有效融合二者的互补表征，在通用SOD中实现灵活的多模态扩展与性能提升。

Original Abstract: General Salient Object Detection (SOD) aims to identify and segment visually interesting objects from uni-modality or multi-modality scenes, recently advanced by cutting-edge State Space Models (SSMs). However, a critical limitation of current approaches is their neglect of the inherent spectral biases exhibited by different neural network paradigms. By digging to the dataset-level spectral analysis of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and SSMs, their semantic representations are inherently complementary based on their complementary frequency preferences. Inspired by this, we harmonize heterogeneous representations from SSMs and CNNs to bridge their spectral biases for general salient object detection. To this end, inspired by the dynamic information propagation of Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs), we introduce a liquid fusion to dynamically integrates features from two backbones, including VMamba and ConvNeXt, referred to Liquid Fusion Network (LFNet). Concretely, by treating the continuous VMamba features and ConvNeXt features as evolving states and exogenous stimulus, respectively, LFNet employs a dynamic gating mechanism for content-aware feature aggregation. Crucially, this state-stimulus paradigm enables to scale to multi-modal cues, resulting in flexibility in general SOD. Besides, a Saliency-Guided Upsampling (SGU) operator to propagate the features to the shallow layer, which leverages a spectral-spatial co-design to suppress upsampling artifacts while preserving semantics. Extensive experiments across five diverse tasks (RGB, RGB-D, RGB-T, VSOD, and VDT) demonstrate that LFNet achieves state-of-the-art performance, offering a superior trade-off between detection accuracy and model efficiency. Code has been released at https://github.com/cke520/LFNet.

中文摘要翻译: 通用显著性目标检测（SOD）旨在从单模态或多模态场景中识别并分割视觉上感兴趣的目标，近期得益于先进的状态空间模型（SSMs）而取得进展。然而，当前方法的一个关键局限性在于忽视了不同神经网络范式所固有的频谱偏置。通过对卷积神经网络（CNNs）和状态空间模型进行数据集层面的频谱分析挖掘，我们发现两者的语义表征基于其互补的频率偏好而天然互补。受此启发，我们协调来自SSMs和CNNs的异质表征，以桥接其频谱偏置，从而实现通用显著性目标检测。为此，受液态神经网络（LNNs）动态信息传播的启发，我们引入一种液态融合机制，用于动态集成两个骨干网络（即VMamba和ConvNeXt）的特征，称为液态融合网络（LFNet）。具体而言，通过将连续的VMamba特征和ConvNeXt特征分别视为演化状态和外源刺激，LFNet采用动态门控机制进行内容感知的特征聚合。关键的是，这种状态-刺激范式能够扩展到多模态线索，从而在通用SOD中实现灵活应用。此外，我们设计了显著性引导上采样（SGU）算子，将特征传播到浅层，该算子利用频谱-空间协同设计来抑制上采样伪影同时保留语义。在五个不同任务（RGB、RGB-D、RGB-T、VSOD和VDT）上的大量实验表明，LFNet实现了最先进的性能，在检测精度与模型效率之间取得了优越的权衡。代码已开源在https://github.com/cke520/LFNet。

</details>


### [53] [Rolling Shutter Relative Pose Estimation Made Practical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26863)
*Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用仿射对应（ACs）解决卷帘快门（RS）相机的相对位姿估计问题，仅需7个对应点即可在1.2毫秒内完成求解，显著优于传统需要20个点的RS求解器，并在RS和全局快门数据集上均取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RS相对位姿估计器至少需要20个点对应，导致RANSAC迭代次数因样本量呈指数增长而计算成本极高，无法投入实际应用。

Method: 将仿射对应引入RS双视图几何，推导了RS校正的仿射约束（每个对应提供两个额外方程）；利用RS参数物理上的小量特性进行线性化，通过零空间投影消除12个RS未知数，剩余20次系统采用作用矩阵法在1.2ms内求解。

Result: 在TUM RS基准上取得最优的位姿和RS参数精度；唯一能提供准确平移速度估计的RS求解器（因点对应存在v-t耦合问题）；在全局快门EuRoC MAV数据集上与标准5点算法精度相当。

Conclusion: 证明了将仿射对应引入RS几何可显著降低所需对应点数量，使RS相对位姿估计变得实用，且方法能很好地泛化到全局快门设置。

Original Abstract: Rolling shutter (RS) cameras equip virtually all consumer devices, yet RS-aware relative pose estimation has remained impractical: the state-of-the-art solver requires a minimum of 20 point correspondences, making RANSAC-based robust estimation prohibitively expensive due to the exponential dependence of the iteration count on the sample size. We make RS relative pose estimation practical by introducing affine correspondences (ACs) into the RS two-view geometry. We derive novel \emph{RS-corrected affine constraints} that account for the coupling between point perturbations and the row-dependent essential matrix, providing two equations per correspondence beyond the standard epipolar constraint. Building on these constraints, we develop a linearized algebraic solver that estimates pose and RS motion from only 7 ACs. The solver exploits the physical smallness of RS parameters to linearize the constraints, eliminates the 12 RS unknowns via null-space projection, and solves the remaining degree-20 system via action matrices in 1.2\,ms. On the TUM RS benchmark, our method achieves the best pose and RS parameter accuracy among all tested methods and, uniquely among RS solvers, provides accurate translational velocity estimates -- which are poorly conditioned from point correspondences alone due to a $\vec{v}$-$\vec{t}$ coupling. On the global-shutter EuRoC MAV dataset, the solver achieves comparable accuracy to the standard 5-point algorithm, demonstrating that it generalizes well to the GS setting. Code is at https://github.com/danini/rolling_shutter_made_practical.

中文摘要翻译: 卷帘快门（RS）相机几乎配备在所有消费设备上，然而基于RS的相对位姿估计一直难以实用：最先进的求解器需要至少20个点对应，这使得基于RANSAC的鲁棒估计因迭代次数对样本量的指数依赖而成本过高。我们通过将仿射对应（ACs）引入RS双视图几何，使RS相对位姿估计变得实用。我们推导了新颖的“RS校正仿射约束”，该约束考虑了点扰动与行相关的本质矩阵之间的耦合，在标准对极约束之外每个对应提供两个方程。基于这些约束，我们开发了一个线性化代数求解器，仅需7个AC即可估计位姿和RS运动。该求解器利用RS参数物理上的小量特性对约束进行线性化，通过零空间投影消除12个RS未知数，并采用作用矩阵在1.2毫秒内求解剩余20次系统。在TUM RS基准上，我们的方法在所有测试方法中取得了最佳的位姿和RS参数精度，并且是唯一能提供准确平移速度估计的RS求解器——而对于单独的点对应，由于v-t耦合问题，平移速度估计的条件数很差。在全局快门EuRoC MAV数据集上，该求解器达到了与标准5点算法相当的精度，证明了它能很好地泛化到全局快门场景。代码参见https://github.com/danini/rolling_shutter_made_practical。

</details>


### [54] [SpatialFlow-GRPO: Where Spatial Credit Drives Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26872)
*Yankai Yang,Yancheng Long,Wei Chen,Xingyu Lu,Hongyang Wei,Bin Wen,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialFlow-GRPO，通过空间细粒度奖励反馈解决图像编辑中全局奖励无法区分不同区域贡献的问题，在多个基准上优于Flow-GRPO。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Flow-GRPO类方法依赖单一全局图像奖励，导致细粒度编辑优化困难；核心障碍在于空间均匀性假设——全局奖励无法区分不同空间区域对图像质量的贡献。

Method: 提出SpatialFlow-GRPO框架：1) 将区域感知奖励转化为语义区域级别的优化信号；2) 在策略更新时将区域优势与对应潜在位置对齐；3) 训练区域感知奖励模型SFReward；4) 构建区域标注编辑样本数据集SFReward-14K；5) 提出多区域编辑评估基准MultiEditBench。

Result: 在OmniGen2和FLUX.2-klein-4B上，SpatialFlow-GRPO在GEdit-Bench、ImgEdit-Bench和MultiEditBench上均优于Flow-GRPO。

Conclusion: SpatialFlow-GRPO能够将局部反馈转化为空间对齐的更新信号，从而有效提升图像编辑质量。

Original Abstract: Recent online reinforcement learning has substantially improved image editing quality. However, existing Flow-GRPO-style methods usually rely on a single whole-image reward, which makes fine-grained editing optimization difficult. We observe that a key obstacle in image editing is this spatial uniformity assumption: a whole-image reward cannot distinguish how different spatial regions contribute to image quality. To address this issue, we propose SpatialFlow-GRPO, a training framework that introduces spatially fine-grained reward feedback. The framework converts region-aware rewards into semantic-region-level optimization signals and aligns region advantages with the corresponding latent positions during policy updates. We also train a region-aware reward model, SFReward, construct SFReward-14K with region-annotated editing samples, and introduce MultiEditBench to evaluate multi-region editing ability. On OmniGen2 and FLUX.2-klein-4B, SpatialFlow-GRPO outperforms Flow-GRPO on GEdit-Bench, ImgEdit-Bench, and MultiEditBench. The results show that SpatialFlow-GRPO converts local feedback into spatially aligned update signals and improves editing quality.

中文摘要翻译: 近期的在线强化学习显著提升了图像编辑质量。然而，现有的Flow-GRPO类方法通常依赖单一的整图奖励，这使得细粒度编辑优化变得困难。我们观察到，图像编辑中的一个关键障碍在于这种空间均匀性假设：整图奖励无法区分不同空间区域对图像质量的贡献。为解决这一问题，我们提出SpatialFlow-GRPO，一个引入空间细粒度奖励反馈的训练框架。该框架将区域感知奖励转化为语义区域级别的优化信号，并在策略更新时将区域优势与对应的潜在位置对齐。我们还训练了一个区域感知奖励模型SFReward，构建了带有区域标注的编辑样本数据集SFReward-14K，并引入MultiEditBench以评估多区域编辑能力。在OmniGen2和FLUX.2-klein-4B上，SpatialFlow-GRPO在GEdit-Bench、ImgEdit-Bench和MultiEditBench上均优于Flow-GRPO。结果表明，SpatialFlow-GRPO能将局部反馈转化为空间对齐的更新信号，从而提升编辑质量。

</details>


### [55] [RIS-Assisted Proactive Handover for Reliable mmWave Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26885)
*Alaa Adnan,Mohammad Al-Quraan,Ahmed Zoha,M. Majid Butt,Sami Muhaidat,Muhammad Ali Imran,Marco Di Renzo,Lina Mohjazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种RIS辅助的毫米波网络主动切换方法，通过优化RIS元素分配数量，在切换时间约束下平衡信号处理复杂度与链路质量，提升能效。实验表明，减少12%的RIS元素可降低10%能耗且不影响信噪比，在阻塞区域信噪比提升15-30dB，同时保持精准的切换时序。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 毫米波网络易受视距阻塞影响，视觉辅助无线通信可通过主动切换缓解阻塞，但当附近无基站可用时主动切换变得困难。RIS可恢复连接，但其大量元素配置时间较长，限制了时间敏感场景下的响应能力。因此需要优化RIS元素分配，兼顾切换时序约束与链路质量。

Method: 提出一种RIS辅助的主动切换方法，构建基于粒子群优化（PSO）的优化问题，在切换时序约束下优化分配的RIS元素数量，平衡信号处理复杂度与链路质量，离线求解最优端到端RIS链路配置以绕过延迟约束。

Result: 减少12%的RIS元素可降低10%能耗且不牺牲信噪比；RIS辅助链路在阻塞区域信噪比提升15-30dB，同时保持准确的主动切换时序。

Conclusion: 所提出的RIS辅助主动切换方法能够在切换时间约束下，通过优化RIS元素分配实现能效与链路质量的有效平衡，为毫米波网络在阻塞场景下的可靠通信提供可行方案。

Original Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) networks are highly susceptible to line-of-sight (LoS) blockages. Vision-aided wireless communications (VAWC) enable proactive handovers (PHO) to mitigate such blockages; however, PHO becomes challenging when no nearby base station (BS) is available. In such cases, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) can be used to restore connectivity. To ensure timely PHO, the RIS configuration time must be taken into account, as the large number of RIS elements can limit responsiveness in time-sensitive scenarios. This work proposes a novel RIS-assisted PHO approach that optimizes the number of allocated RIS elements to balance signal processing complexity and link quality under handover timing constraints, making the RIS-assisted link more energy-efficient. An optimization problem based on particle swarm optimization (PSO) is formulated to determine the optimal end-to-end RIS link setup that runs offline to bypass latency constraints. Results show that reducing the number of RIS elements by 12\% leads to a 10\% decrease in dissipated energy without compromising the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Moreover, the RIS-assisted link achieves a 15--30 dB improvement in blocked regions while maintaining accurate PHO timing.

中文摘要翻译: 毫米波网络极易受视距阻塞的影响。视觉辅助无线通信能够通过主动切换来缓解此类阻塞；然而，当附近没有可用基站时，主动切换变得具有挑战性。在这种情况下，可重构智能表面可用于恢复连接。为确保及时的主动切换，必须考虑RIS的配置时间，因为大量的RIS元素会限制时间敏感场景下的响应能力。本文提出一种新颖的RIS辅助主动切换方法，通过优化分配的RIS元素数量，在切换时序约束下平衡信号处理复杂度与链路质量，从而使RIS辅助链路更具能效。本文构建了基于粒子群优化的优化问题，用于确定最优的端到端RIS链路配置，该配置离线运行以绕过延迟约束。结果表明，减少12%的RIS元素可使能耗降低10%，且不牺牲信噪比。此外，RIS辅助链路在阻塞区域实现了15-30dB的信噪比提升，同时保持精准的主动切换时序。

</details>


### [56] [Bridging Vision and Language Concepts through Optimal Transport Semantic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26891)
*Chenyang Zhang,Anqi Dong,Guangming Zhu,Nuoye Xiong,Siyuan Wang,Lin Mei,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OTF-CBM，将概念对齐从静态投影重新思考为动态最优传输流匹配过程，提升了分类准确率和概念忠实性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言概念瓶颈模型（CBM）依赖预对齐编码器或全局余弦相似度，无法捕捉细粒度概念定位，且不能反映真实的语义几何结构。

Method: 首先通过逆向最优传输学习数据驱动的语义代价来衡量跨模态距离；然后基于非平衡最优传输进行流匹配，建模视觉块与文本概念之间的语义转换；最后利用基于速度的概念激活捕获可解释的几何关系，无需ODE积分。

Result: 实验表明OTF-CBM在分类准确率和概念忠实性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: OTF-CBM为可解释的跨模态推理提供了新的几何与动力学视角。

Original Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise transparent reasoning by predicting through human-interpretable concepts, yet their effectiveness fundamentally depends on how well visual and textual representations are aligned or matched. Existing vision-language CBMs often rely on pre-aligned encoders or global cosine similarity, which obscures fine-grained concept localization and fails to reflect true semantic geometry. In this work, we rethink concept alignment as a dynamic cross-modal transport process instead of static projection and propose the Optimal Transport Flow Concept Bottleneck Model (OTF-CBM). It first learns a data-driven semantic cost via Inverse Optimal Transport to measure cross-modal distances, and then performs unbalanced optimal-transport-based flow matching to model semantic transitions between visual patches and textual concepts. With velocity-based concept activation, OTF-CBM captures interpretable geometric relations without ODE integration. Experiments further show that OTF-CBM achieves superior classification accuracy and concept faithfulness, offering a new geometric and dynamical perspective for interpretable cross-modal reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）通过可解释的中间概念进行推理，承诺透明的决策过程，但其有效性根本上取决于视觉表示与文本表示之间的对齐程度。现有的视觉-语言CBM通常依赖预对齐的编码器或全局余弦相似度，这种方式模糊了细粒度的概念定位，也无法反映真实的语义几何结构。在本工作中，我们将概念对齐重新思考为一个动态的跨模态传输过程，而非静态投影，并提出了最优传输流概念瓶颈模型（OTF-CBM）。该方法首先通过逆向最优传输学习数据驱动的语义代价以衡量跨模态距离，然后执行基于非平衡最优传输的流匹配，建模视觉块与文本概念之间的语义转换。通过基于速度的概念激活，OTF-CBM能够在不进行ODE积分的情况下捕获可解释的几何关系。实验进一步表明，OTF-CBM在分类准确率和概念忠实性上均取得了更优性能，为可解释的跨模态推理提供了新的几何与动力学视角。

</details>


### [57] [Modeling Local, Global, and Cross-Modal Context in Multimodal 3D MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26894)
*Minh Duc Do,Tillmann Rheude,Noel Kronenberg,Roland Eils,Benjamin Wild*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MICViT，一种3D视觉Transformer，通过显式建模模态内（局部和全局）和跨模态（局部和全局）的注意力机制，有效融合多模态脑MRI数据，在脑龄预测任务上超越现有SOTA方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态脑MRI能提供互补信息，但现有方法难以有效整合来自不同模态的异构信号，尤其是在高维3D数据和小样本场景下。

Method: MICViT包含四种注意力机制：模态特异性局部注意力、模态特异性全局注意力（用于模态内特征学习），以及跨模态局部注意力、跨模态全局注意力（用于捕获模态间交互）。

Result: 在三个数据集（UK Biobank, n=41,404; SOOP, n=1,062; Cam-CAN, n=613）上使用T1、FLAIR、DWI、SWI等多模态MRI进行脑龄预测，MICViT一致优于3D CNN和Transformer基线，且随模态数增加性能增益更大。

Conclusion: 显式建模模态内和跨模态交互是释放多模态脑MRI潜力的关键，为神经影像表示学习提供了有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Brain MRI poses a fundamental challenge for machine learning: models must learn from high-dimensional 3D data spanning multiple co-registered modalities, despite the limited sample sizes typical of neuroimaging studies relative to the diversity in anatomy, pathology, and acquisition conditions. While multimodal imaging provides complementary information critical for clinical interpretation, effectively integrating these signals remains difficult. We propose Multimodal Intra- and Cross-Context Vision Transformer (MICViT), a 3D vision transformer that explicitly models both modality-specific representations and cross-modal interactions across local and global contexts. Concretely, MICViT combines four attention mechanisms: modality-specific local and global attention for intra-modal feature learning, and cross-modal local and global attention to capture interactions between modalities. We evaluate MICViT on brain age prediction across three heterogeneous datasets (UK Biobank, n=41,404; SOOP, n=1,062; Cam-CAN, n=613) using multiple MRI modalities (e.g. T1, FLAIR, DWI, SWI). MICViT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art CNN and transformer baselines in 3D settings. Notably, it benefits more strongly from multimodal inputs, yielding larger performance gains as additional modalities are incorporated. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling intra- and cross-modal interactions is key to unlocking the full potential of multimodal brain MRI, highlighting a promising direction for representation learning in neuroimaging.

中文摘要翻译: 脑部MRI对机器学习构成了根本性挑战：模型必须从跨越多个共配准模态的高维3D数据中学习，而神经影像学研究通常面临样本量有限与解剖、病理和采集条件多样性之间的矛盾。尽管多模态成像提供了对临床解释至关重要的互补信息，有效整合这些信号仍然困难。我们提出了多模态内-跨上下文视觉Transformer（MICViT），这是一种3D视觉Transformer，能够显式地建模模态特异性表示以及局部和全局上下文中的跨模态交互。具体而言，MICViT结合了四种注意力机制：模态特异性局部和全局注意力用于模态内特征学习，以及跨模态局部和全局注意力用于捕获模态间的交互。我们在三个异构数据集（UK Biobank，n=41,404；SOOP，n=1,062；Cam-CAN，n=613）上，使用多种MRI模态（如T1、FLAIR、DWI、SWI）对MICViT进行了脑龄预测评估。在3D设置下，MICViT一致优于最先进的CNN和Transformer基线。值得注意的是，它从多模态输入中获益更多，随着更多模态的加入产生了更大的性能提升。这些结果表明，显式建模模态内和跨模态交互是释放多模态脑MRI全部潜力的关键，为神经影像中的表示学习指明了有前景的方向。

</details>


### [58] [Tractography-Driven Synthetic Data Generation for Fiber Bundle Segmentation in Tracer Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26898)
*Kyriaki-Margarita Bintsi,Sparsh Makharia,Yaël Balbastre,Joselyn Romero Avila,Julia F. Lehman,Suzanne N. Haber,Anastasia Yendiki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于合成数据增强的框架，用于自动分割猕猴示踪组织学中的纤维束，在减少3倍人工标注数据的同时达到与现有方法相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弥散MRI纤维束成像的精度受限于间接、低分辨率的轴突组织测量，而示踪剂注射研究虽提供金标准但需要耗时的手动标注，因此亟需自动化纤维束分割方法以减少人工标注负担。

Method: 利用离体dMRI纤维束成像作为生成先验合成2D图像块，与块面照片背景组合并通过域随机化增加多样性；在混合真实与合成图像块上训练2D U-Net进行分割。

Result: 在留出脑数据上，该方法在跨脑泛化能力和纤维束密度泛化方面均优于仅用真实数据训练；仅用合成数据训练性能较差，表明真实监督的必要性；整体性能与现有方法相当，但所需人工标注数据减少3倍。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强结合少量真实标注可以有效提升纤维束分割的泛化能力并大幅降低人工标注成本。

Original Abstract: Diffusion MRI (dMRI) tractography enables non-invasive reconstruction of white-matter pathways, but its accuracy is fundamentally limited by indirect, low-resolution measurements of axonal organization. Tracer injection studies in non-human primates provide a gold standard for validating dMRI tractography. This, however, requires time-consuming manual annotation of fiber bundles in histology sections. We propose a synthetic-data augmented framework for automated fiber bundle segmentation in macaque tracer histology. Our approach uses ex vivo dMRI tractography as a generative prior to synthesize 2D image patches for training. This provides us with sufficiently realistic foreground texture, which we compose with backgrounds from blockface photos and diversify via domain randomization. A 2D U-Net is trained on mixed real and synthetic patches. Experiments on held-out brains demonstrate improved generalization across brains and fiber bundle densities compared to training with real data only. Training with synthetic data only leads to poor performance, underscoring the need for real supervision. Overall, our approach achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art while requiring 3x less manually annotated data.

中文摘要翻译: 弥散MRI（dMRI）纤维束成像能够无创重建白质通路，但其准确性从根本上受到轴突组织间接、低分辨率测量的限制。非人灵长类动物的示踪剂注射研究为验证dMRI纤维束成像提供了金标准，但这需要对组织学切片中的纤维束进行耗时的手动标注。我们提出了一种基于合成数据增强的框架，用于猕猴示踪组织学中纤维束的自动分割。该方法利用离体dMRI纤维束成像作为生成先验，合成用于训练的2D图像块。这为我们提供了足够逼真的前景纹理，我们将其与块面照片的背景组合，并通过域随机化增加多样性。在混合真实与合成图像块上训练一个2D U-Net。在留出脑数据上的实验表明，与仅使用真实数据训练相比，该方法在跨脑泛化能力和纤维束密度泛化方面均有提升。仅使用合成数据训练会导致性能较差，凸显了真实监督的必要性。总体而言，我们的方法在性能上与现有方法相当，而所需人工标注数据减少了3倍。

</details>


### [59] [Confidence-Aware Tool Orchestration for Robust Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26904)
*Yangfan He,Yujin Choi,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust-TO，一个将每帧可信度显式集成到推理各阶段的智能视频理解框架，通过统一证据接口、可靠性-相关性分数筛选帧、三级证据综合及置信-成本GRPO奖励，在多个视频推理基准上显著提升了鲁棒性和准确性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频推理语言模型隐含假设所有输入帧同样可靠，在运动模糊、眩光、遮挡等真实扰动下，前沿模型准确率下降15-30个百分点，且无法感知视觉证据已被降质。这被称为"盲目信任问题"。

Method: Robust-TO框架包含：(1) 统一证据接口，组织异构视觉感知工具；(2) 可靠性-相关性分数(RRS)筛选可信帧；(3) 三级证据综合（高/中/低）加权合成；(4) 置信-成本GRPO奖励函数，联合优化正确性、证据可靠性和效率。

Result: 在涵盖八项任务的两个视频推理基准上，清洁输入下平均准确率56.4%，超越最强开源基线10.6个百分点，超过Gemini-2.5-Pro（46.2%）；在五种真实扰动下保持54.3%平均准确率，比最强开源基线高5.8个百分点，且清洁到扰动的准确率下降幅度最小。

Conclusion: 显式建模每帧可信度并将其集成到推理全流程中，能有效提升视频推理模型在真实世界扰动下的鲁棒性和准确性，为解决视频推理中的盲目信任问题提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning language models implicitly assume that every input frame is equally reliable. This leads to what we term the Blind Trust Problem: under realistic perturbations such as motion blur, glare, or occlusion, frontier video reasoning models can suffer 15-30%p accuracy drops on real-world embodied benchmarks, while remaining unaware that their visual evidence has been degraded. To address this challenge, we propose Robust-TO, an agentic video understanding framework that explicitly integrates per-frame trustworthiness into every stage of reasoning. Robust-TO organizes heterogeneous visual perception tools under a unified evidence interface. Each tool receives a sub-query derived from the original question and a set of trustworthy frames selected by the reliability-relevance score. It returns evidence in a shared format: a concrete prediction (e.g., a bounding box, motion trajectory, recognized text, or action label), temporal grounding, and a calibrated reliability score. During reasoning, these calibrated scores guide evidence weighting in a three-tier synthesis process (high/medium/low) and define a confidence-cost GRPO reward that jointly optimizes correctness, evidence reliability, and efficiency. On two video reasoning benchmarks spanning eight tasks, Robust-TO achieves 56.4% average accuracy on clean inputs, surpassing the strongest open-source baseline by 10.6%p and outperforming Gemini-2.5-Pro (46.2%). Under five realistic corruption types, Robust-TO maintains 54.3% average accuracy, 5.8%p above the strongest open-source baseline, while exhibiting the smallest clean-to-corrupted accuracy drop among all compared methods.

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理语言模型隐式地假设每一帧输入都具有相同的可靠性。这导致我们称之为"盲目信任问题"的现象：在运动模糊、眩光或遮挡等现实扰动下，前沿视频推理模型在真实世界具身基准上的准确率下降15-30个百分点，同时模型自身并未意识到其视觉证据已被降质。为解决这一挑战，我们提出Robust-TO，一个将每帧可信度显式集成到推理每个阶段的智能视频理解框架。Robust-TO在统一的证据接口下组织异构的视觉感知工具。每个工具接收由原始问题派生出的子查询以及一组由可靠性-相关性分数筛选出的可信帧，并以共享格式返回证据：具体预测（如边界框、运动轨迹、识别文本或动作标签）、时间定位以及校准后的可靠性分数。在推理过程中，这些校准后的分数在三层综合过程（高/中/低）中指导证据加权，并定义了一个置信-成本GRPO奖励函数，该函数联合优化正确性、证据可靠性和效率。在涵盖八项任务的两个视频推理基准上，Robust-TO在清洁输入下实现了56.4%的平均准确率，超越最强开源基线10.6个百分点，并优于Gemini-2.5-Pro（46.2%）。在五种现实类型扰动下，Robust-TO保持了54.3%的平均准确率，比最强开源基线高5.8个百分点，同时在所有对比方法中展现出最小的清洁到扰动准确率下降幅度。

</details>


### [60] [Qwen-Image-Agent: Bridging the Context Gap in Real-World Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26907)
*Zekai Zhang,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Kun Yan,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Shengming Yin,Tianhe Wu,Xiaoyue Chen,Xiao Xu,Yan Shu,Yanran Zhang,Yixian Xu,Yuxiang Chen,Zhendong Wang,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen-Image-Agent是一个统一的智能体框架，通过上下文感知规划和上下文接地逐步构建生成上下文，弥合文本到图像模型中用户输入与充分生成上下文之间的差距，在IA-Bench等基准上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在处理真实世界中欠指定、隐式或依赖最新知识的需求时表现不佳，存在用户上下文与模型充分生成上下文之间的不匹配（上下文差距）。

Method: 提出Qwen-Image-Agent框架，以上下文为中心集成规划、推理、搜索、记忆和反馈。包含两个核心模块：上下文感知规划（识别缺失上下文并规划获取方式）和上下文接地（通过推理、搜索、记忆和反馈收集所需上下文）。

Result: 在IA-Bench、Mindbench和WISE-Verified基准上，Qwen-Image-Agent超越了强基线方法，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体框架有效弥合了上下文差距，验证了以上下文为中心的智能体图像生成范式的有效性。

Original Abstract: While text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable progress, they struggle with real-world requests that are often underspecified, implicit, or dependent on up-to-date knowledge. We identify this challenge as the Context Gap: the mismatch between the user context and the sufficient generation context for T2I models. To bridge this gap, we propose Qwen-Image-Agent, a unified agentic framework that integrates plan, reason, search, memory and feedback in a context-centric manner.
