Qwen-Image-Agent treats user input as partial context and progressively constructs the generation context through Context-Aware Planning and Context Grounding. Specifically, Context-Aware Planning identifies missing context and plans how it should be acquired and used, while Context Grounding gathers this context from reason, search, memory, and feedback. To evaluate agentic image generation, we further introduce Image Agent Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark covering four core image agent capabilities: Plan, Reason, Search, and Memory. Experiments on IA-Bench, Mindbench and WISE-Verified show that Qwen-Image-Agent outperforms strong baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管文本到图像模型取得了显著进展，但在处理真实世界中往往欠指定、隐式或依赖最新知识的需求时仍面临挑战。我们将这一挑战识别为上下文差距：用户上下文与文本到图像模型充分生成上下文之间的不匹配。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了Qwen-Image-Agent，一个统一的智能体框架，以上下文为中心集成了规划、推理、搜索、记忆和反馈。Qwen-Image-Agent将用户输入视为部分上下文，通过上下文感知规划和上下文接地逐步构建生成上下文。具体而言，上下文感知规划识别缺失的上下文并规划如何获取和使用它，而上下文接地则通过推理、搜索、记忆和反馈收集这些上下文。为评估智能体图像生成，我们进一步引入了Image Agent Bench（IA-Bench），一个涵盖智能体图像生成四大核心能力（规划、推理、搜索和记忆）的基准。在IA-Bench、Mindbench和WISE-Verified上的实验表明，Qwen-Image-Agent超越了强基线方法，取得了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [61] [PhysRAG: Enhancing Physics-Awareness in Video Generation via Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26916)
*Kexu Cheng,Zicheng Liu,Mingju Gao,Chunhe Song,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysRAG 提出通过检索增强生成（RAG）将物理知识注入视频扩散模型，并构建高质量数据集，在物理感知视频生成任务上达到 SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型缺乏物理感知能力，难以捕捉热动力学、力学、光学等多样物理现象，且高质量训练数据有限。

Method: 1）基于 WISA-80K 数据集设计两阶段数据过滤流程，筛选出 7K 高质量视频；2）构建物理视频数据库，利用可学习查询将物理知识注入视频扩散模型。

Result: 在 PhyGenBench 和 VBench 基准上，视觉质量和物理规则遵从性均超越现有模型，消融实验验证了各关键组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 RAG 机制可有效增强视频生成模型的物理感知能力，并为后续研究提供了开源数据、代码和模型。

Original Abstract: Developing physically aware video generation models remains a significant challenge due to the difficulty in capturing diverse physical phenomena, such as thermal dynamics, mechanics, and optics. In this work, we introduce PhysRAG, a novel pipeline that enhances physical awareness in video generation through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). To address the issue of limited high-quality data, we design a two-stage data filtering pipeline based on the WISA-80K dataset, resulting in a curated set of 7K high-quality videos for training. Furthermore, we construct a physical video database and develop a mechanism to inject physical knowledge into a video diffusion model using learnable queries. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both visual quality and physical rule compliance, surpassing existing models in benchmarks such as PhyGenBench and VBench. We conduct extensive ablation studies to validate the effectiveness of our key components, including the data filtering pipeline, RAG mechanism, and method for physical information extraction. To facilitate future research, our code, data, and models are prepared for release at https://github.com/sediment1024/PhysRAG.

中文摘要翻译: 由于难以捕捉诸如热动力学、力学和光学等多样物理现象，开发具有物理感知能力的视频生成模型仍然是一项重大挑战。在本工作中，我们提出了 PhysRAG，一种通过检索增强生成（RAG）提升视频生成中物理感知能力的新型流水线。为解决高质量数据有限的问题，我们基于 WISA-80K 数据集设计了一个两阶段数据过滤流水线，最终筛选出 7K 高质量视频用于训练。此外，我们构建了一个物理视频数据库，并开发了一种利用可学习查询将物理知识注入视频扩散模型的机制。我们的方法在视觉质量和物理规则遵从性方面均达到了最先进水平，在 PhyGenBench 和 VBench 等基准上超越了现有模型。我们开展了广泛的消融研究，以验证各关键组件（包括数据过滤流水线、RAG 机制和物理信息提取方法）的有效性。为促进未来研究，我们的代码、数据和模型已准备在 https://github.com/sediment1024/PhysRAG 发布。

</details>


### [62] [PortraitGen: Exemplar-Driven GRPO with Dual-Reward Guidance for Photorealistic Portrait Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26930)
*Xiaomin Li,Qian Liang,Yinan Li,Ying Zhang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Huchuan Lu,Xu Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对文本到图像后训练中现有方法偏好表面美学而忽略AI伪影和生物不合理性的问题，论文提出PortraitGen框架，通过将真实图像引入GRPO采样组并设计双奖励机制，显著提升了肖像生成的光真实感。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有强化学习方法（如GRPO）在文本到图像后训练中偏好过度饱和色彩等表面美学，无法解决AI伪影和生物不合理性等关键缺陷。原因有二：（1）后训练中缺乏真实图像，GRPO采样局限于原始分布，无法突破固有生成边界；（2）优化过程缺乏针对细粒度伪影（如过度油腻皮肤）的特定奖励。

Method: 提出PortraitGen框架：（1）将真实图像直接引入GRPO采样组，通过图像反演获取其转移概率和潜变量，从而突破固有生成边界；（2）引入互补双奖励机制——OmniReward负责通用质量，AI-Portrait负责人类中心保真度；（3）构建PortraitBench肖像基准数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，PortraitGen显著优于现有基线方法，有效抑制了AI伪影，实现了前所未有的光真实感水平。

Conclusion: PortraitGen通过引入真实图像到GRPO采样组和双奖励机制，有效解决了文本到图像后训练中表面美学偏好和细粒度伪影问题，在肖像生成领域实现了卓越的光真实感。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has significantly advanced text-to-image post-training. However, current methods often favor superficial aesthetics, such as over-saturated colors, leaving critical flaws like AI artifacts and biological implausibilities unresolved. We attribute these limitations to two primary factors: (1) The absence of real images during post-training confines GRPO sampling to the original distribution, failing to break inherent generative boundaries; (2) the optimization process lacks specific rewards targeting fine-grained artifacts like overly oily skin and other AI artifacts. To address this, we propose PortraitGen, a novel framework tailored for photorealistic portrait generation. First, we break inherent generative boundaries by directly introducing real images into the GRPO sampling groups, where image inversion is employed to obtain their transition probabilities and latents. Second, to explicitly steer the model toward photorealism, we introduce a complementary dual-reward mechanism: OmniReward for general quality and AI-Portrait for human-centric fidelity. Furthermore, we curate PortraitBench, a comprehensive portrait-centric benchmark. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PortraitGen significantly outperforms existing baselines, effectively suppressing AI artifacts and achieving unprecedented photorealism.

中文摘要翻译: 以群体相对策略优化（GRPO）为代表的强化学习方法显著推动了文本到图像的后训练。然而，当前方法往往偏好表面美学，例如过度饱和的色彩，而未能解决AI伪影和生物不合理性等关键缺陷。我们将这些局限性归因于两个主要因素：（1）后训练过程中缺乏真实图像，使得GRPO采样局限于原始分布，无法突破固有的生成边界；（2）优化过程缺乏针对细粒度伪影（如过度油腻的皮肤和其他AI伪影）的特定奖励。为解决这一问题，我们提出了PortraitGen，一个专为逼真肖像生成而设计的新颖框架。首先，我们通过将真实图像直接引入GRPO采样组来突破固有的生成边界，其中采用图像反演来获取其转移概率和潜变量。其次，为明确引导模型朝向光真实感，我们引入了一种互补的双奖励机制：用于通用质量的OmniReward和用于以人为中心保真度的AI-Portrait。此外，我们构建了PortraitBench，一个全面的肖像中心基准数据集。大量实验表明，PortraitGen显著优于现有基线方法，有效抑制了AI伪影，实现了前所未有的光真实感。

</details>


### [63] [Focusing on What Matters: Saliency-Harnessing Accurate Routing for Diffusion MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26938)
*Haoyou Deng,Keyu Yan,Chaojie Mao,Xiang Wang,Yu Liu,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SharpMoE提出了一种基于清晰潜特征引导路由的后训练框架，解决了扩散MoE模型中路由分配不准确的问题，在视觉生成任务中实现了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散MoE框架中，路由器依赖噪声扰动后的潜特征进行路由分配，导致无法准确为显著token分配更多计算资源；噪声掩盖了关键的结构和纹理信息，阻碍路由器有效区分显著token。

Method: 提出SharpMoE后训练框架，包含：（1）基于清晰潜特征的无噪声引导信号的路由机制，绕过噪声扭曲输入提供清晰显著性引导；（2）轨迹路由损失函数，约束多步去噪轨迹上的计算分配，确保生成过程中的精确资源分配。

Result: SharpMoE作为即插即用的通用解决方案，显著增强了预训练且已收敛的MoE模型，在视觉生成任务中达到了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入清晰潜特征作为路由指导信号和轨迹路由损失，SharpMoE有效解决了扩散MoE中的路由分配问题，实现了更高效准确的计算资源分配，提升了视觉生成质量。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling diffusion models in visual generation. Recent advancements have focused on adaptively allocating computational resources across diverse tokens to improve efficiency and performance. However, we identify a routing assignment problem in existing diffusion MoE frameworks: the router fails to accurately allocate more computational resources to salient tokens. Our analysis attributes this failure to the router's reliance on noise-corrupted latent features throughout the denoising process. Such stochastic noise obscures the critical structural and textural information, thereby preventing the router from effectively distinguishing salient tokens. To address this, we propose SharpMoE, a post-training framework with a saliency-harnessing accurate routing mechanism, which utilizes clean latent features as a noise-free guidance signal for routing. By bypassing the noise-distorted inputs, SharpMoE provides the router with clear saliency guidance, enabling the identification of salient tokens even in high-noise stages. Furthermore, we introduce a trajectory routing loss to constrain the compute allocation throughout the multi-step denoising trajectory, ensuring precise resource allocation along the generation rollout. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SharpMoE serves as a versatile, plug-and-play solution that further enhances the pretrained, converged MoE models, achieving state-of-the-art performance in visual generation.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）架构已成为视觉生成领域中扩展扩散模型的有效范式。近期研究聚焦于在多样化的token间自适应分配计算资源，以提升效率和性能。然而，我们发现现有扩散MoE框架存在路由分配问题：路由器无法准确地将更多计算资源分配给显著token。我们的分析将这一失败归因于路由器在整个去噪过程中依赖于被噪声破坏的潜特征。这种随机噪声掩盖了关键的结构和纹理信息，从而阻碍路由器有效区分显著token。为解决这一问题，我们提出了SharpMoE——一种后训练框架，其核心是一种利用显著性的精确路由机制，该机制借助清晰的潜特征作为无噪声的引导信号进行路由。通过绕过受噪声扭曲的输入，SharpMoE为路由器提供了清晰的显著性引导，使其即便在高噪声阶段也能识别出显著token。此外，我们引入了轨迹路由损失，以约束多步去噪轨迹上的计算分配，确保在生成展开过程中的精确资源分配。大量实验表明，SharpMoE作为一种通用的即插即用解决方案，能够进一步增强预训练且已收敛的MoE模型，在视觉生成领域取得最先进的性能。

</details>


### [64] [TraMP-LLaMA: Generative Interpretability with Decoupled Instruction Tuning for Facial Expression Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26942)
*Shuchao Duan,Alan Whone,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu,Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TraMP-LLaMA统一多模态框架，联合预测帕金森病面部表情严重程度并生成结构化文本报告，通过解耦指令微调策略减少任务干扰，并在扩展的PFED5-plus数据集上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有面部表情质量评估方法仅输出严重程度分数，缺乏可解释性，无法明确告知模型预测所依据的面部运动证据，限制了在帕金森病评估中的应用。

Method: 提出TraMP-LLaMA统一多模态框架，融合RGB外观特征和地标轨迹线索，采用解耦指令微调策略以降低严重程度预测与语言生成之间的任务干扰，同时联合预测严重程度分数并生成结构化文本报告。

Result: 在扩展的PFED5-plus数据集上，TraMP-LLaMA在报告生成任务上优于竞争性的视频-语言基线方法；在多表情联合训练下，严重程度预测的Spearman秩相关系数相较所有竞争方法至少提升4.39%。

Conclusion: TraMP-LLaMA通过多模态融合与解耦指令微调，有效实现了帕金森病面部运动评估的预测与可解释报告生成，提升了性能与可解释性。

Original Abstract: Existing facial expression quality assessment (FEQA) methods typically produce only a severity score, without explicitly communicating the observable facial motion evidence that supports the prediction. This limits interpretability and makes it difficult to inspect the basis of model outputs in Parkinson's disease assessment. To address this gap, we propose TraMP-LLaMA, a unified multimodal framework that jointly predicts severity scores and generates structured textual reports from facial motion cues. The framework integrates RGB appearance and landmark trajectory cues, and adopts a decoupled instruction-tuning strategy to reduce task interference between severity prediction and language generation. To support this task, we further extend the PFED5 dataset with expert-guided textual motion descriptions and construct PFED5-plus. Experiments on PFED5-plus show that TraMP-LLaMA outperforms competitive video-language baselines in report generation and achieves the best severity prediction performance among the compared methods under joint multi-expression training, improving Spearman's rank correlation by at least 4.39 percent over all competing methods. The text annotations and code are available at https://github.com/shuchaoduan/TraMP-LLaMA.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的面部表情质量评估方法通常仅输出一个严重程度分数，而未能明确传达支持预测的可观测面部运动证据。这限制了模型的可解释性，使得在帕金森病评估中难以检查模型输出的依据。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了TraMP-LLaMA，一个统一的多模态框架，能够联合预测严重程度分数并从面部运动线索生成结构化文本报告。该框架融合了RGB外观特征和地标轨迹线索，并采用解耦指令微调策略以减少严重程度预测与语言生成之间的任务干扰。为支持该任务，我们进一步扩展了PFED5数据集，添加了专家引导的文本运动描述，构建了PFED5-plus数据集。在PFED5-plus上的实验表明，TraMP-LLaMA在报告生成任务上优于竞争性的视频-语言基线方法，并且在多表情联合训练下，其严重程度预测性能在所有对比方法中达到最优，Spearman秩相关系数相较所有竞争方法至少提升4.39%。文本标注和代码已开源在https://github.com/shuchaoduan/TraMP-LLaMA。

</details>


### [65] [Scaling Multi-Reference Image Generation with Dynamic Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26947)
*Wenwang Huang,Yusen Fu,Junjie Wang,Mengfei Huang,Yulin Li,Gan Liu,Jing Cai,Yancheng He,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多参考图像生成（MRIG）任务中现有基准不足的问题，提出OmniRef-Bench复杂基准，并设计DyRef两阶段训练框架（监督微调+难度感知优势重加权与判别性奖励缩放），显著提升开源模型在复杂MRIG场景下的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准无法充分评估复杂的多参考图像生成（MRIG）场景，阻碍了该领域的进一步发展。

Method: 提出DyRef两阶段训练框架：第一阶段通过监督微调使模型具备处理复杂MRIG任务的基础能力；第二阶段引入难度感知优势重加权（DAR）动态调整优化目标以应对大量混合类型参考图像，以及判别性奖励缩放（DRS）扩大组内奖励差异以实现更有效的策略优化。

Result: 主流开源模型在OmniRef-Bench上表现不佳，且性能随混合类型参考图像数量增加而显著下降。DyRef显著提升了开源模型在OmniRef-Bench和单图像编辑基准上的性能。

Conclusion: DyRef能有效提升开源模型在复杂MRIG任务上的性能，并在单图像编辑任务上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: While personalized image generation has achieved remarkable progress, multi-reference image generation (MRIG) remains a challenging task. Most existing benchmarks fail to adequately evaluate complex MRIG scenarios, hindering further progress in this area. To better assess model performance on complex MRIG tasks, we introduce OmniRef-Bench, a benchmark that covers complex combinations of reference image types and a large number of reference images. Evaluations on OmniRef-Bench show that mainstream open-source models struggle in complex MRIG scenarios, and their performance deteriorates significantly as the number of mixed-type reference images increases. To address this issue, we propose DyRef, a two-stage training framework. In the first stage, supervised fine-tuning equips the model with the basic capability to handle complex MRIG tasks. In the second stage, we introduce Difficulty-aware Advantage Reweighting (DAR) and Discriminative Reward Scaling (DRS). DAR dynamically adjusts the optimization objective to improve performance when handling a large number of mixed-type reference images. DRS enlarges intra-group reward differences for more effective policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that DyRef significantly improves the performance of open-source models on OmniRef-Bench and single-image editing benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalization capability of our approach.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管个性化图像生成取得了显著进展，多参考图像生成（MRIG）仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务。大多数现有基准未能充分评估复杂的MRIG场景，阻碍了该领域的进一步发展。为了更好地评估模型在复杂MRIG任务上的性能，我们引入了OmniRef-Bench，该基准涵盖了参考图像类型的复杂组合以及大量参考图像。在OmniRef-Bench上的评估显示，主流开源模型在复杂MRIG场景中表现不佳，且随着混合类型参考图像数量的增加，其性能显著下降。为解决这一问题，我们提出了DyRef，一种两阶段训练框架。在第一阶段，监督微调使模型具备处理复杂MRIG任务的基础能力。在第二阶段，我们引入了难度感知优势重加权（DAR）和判别性奖励缩放（DRS）。DAR动态调整优化目标，以提升处理大量混合类型参考图像时的性能；DRS则扩大组内奖励差异，实现更有效的策略优化。实验表明，DyRef显著提升了开源模型在OmniRef-Bench和单图像编辑基准上的性能，验证了该方法的效果和泛化能力。

</details>


### [66] [Computer Vision for MOBA Analytics: A Dataset and Baseline for Visibility Analysis in Dota 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26970)
*Ricardo da Rocha Carvalho,Eloísa Oliveira,Luiz Bernardo Martins Kummer,Emerson Cabrera Paraiso,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dota2-Vis，一个基于视频的Dota 2职业比赛可见性分析数据集（包含144场比赛、288个全高清视频和2,477张手动标注的小地图图像），并构建了以YOLO11l为主体的基线检测流水线，通过对手可见的玩家存在曲线揭示了传统结构化数据难以捕捉的玩家、英雄、角色和团队级别的行为模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的MOBA游戏分析研究大多依赖结构化数据（如击杀、经济等统计数据），这些数据无法直接反映比赛中各队伍实际能看到的视野信息。因此，本文旨在引入基于视频的可见性分析，弥补结构化数据在捕捉团队视野信息方面的不足。

Method: 本文构建了Dota2-Vis数据集，包含The International 2025全部144场比赛的双视角全高清视频（共288个视频），以及2,477张手动标注的小地图图像。评估了多种现代目标检测器（YOLO系列变体）用于玩家图标检测的性能，选择最优模型（YOLO11l）估计对手可见的玩家存在随时间变化的曲线。

Result: YOLO11l（大型模型）在密集且视觉杂乱的小地图场景中取得了最佳的整体性能，能够可靠地识别玩家图标。基于该模型生成的可见性曲线揭示了玩家、英雄、角色和团队层面的行为模式，这些模式是传统结构化数据分析难以获得的。

Conclusion: 基于视频的可见性分析能够为MOBA游戏分析提供补充性的行为洞察，揭示结构化数据无法直接反映的团队视野信息。数据集和代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Introduction: Most Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) analytics studies rely on structured data, which does not directly capture what each team could actually see during a match. Objective: This work introduces Dota2-Vis, a video-based dataset, and a baseline pipeline for visibility analysis in professional Dota 2 matches. Methodology: The dataset comprises all 144 matches from The International 2025, recorded from both team perspectives, totaling 288 Full HD videos, together with 2,477 manually annotated minimap images. We evaluate multiple variants of a modern object detector for player-icon detection and use the best-performing model to estimate opponent-visible player presence over time. Results: YOLO11l (large) achieved the best overall performance, reliably identifying player icons even in dense and visually cluttered minimap scenes. The resulting visibility curves reveal player, hero, role, and team-level patterns that complement conventional MOBA analytics, highlighting behavioral differences that are difficult to obtain from structured data alone. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/RicardoRCarvalho/dota2-vis/.

中文摘要翻译: 引言：大多数多人在线战术竞技（MOBA）游戏分析研究依赖于结构化数据，这类数据无法直接捕捉每支队伍在比赛中实际能够看到的视野信息。目的：本文提出了Dota2-Vis，一个基于视频的数据集，以及一个用于职业Dota 2比赛可见性分析的基线流水线。方法论：该数据集包含The International 2025全部144场比赛，从双方队伍视角录制，共计288个全高清视频，以及2,477张手动标注的小地图图像。我们评估了多种现代目标检测器变体用于玩家图标检测的性能，并使用表现最佳的模型来估计对手可见的玩家存在随时间变化的曲线。结果：YOLO11l（大型模型）取得了最佳的整体性能，即使在密集且视觉杂乱的小地图场景中也能可靠地识别玩家图标。由此生成的可见性曲线揭示了玩家、英雄、角色和团队层面的行为模式，这些模式补充了传统的MOBA分析，突显了仅从结构化数据难以获得的行为差异。数据集和代码已在https://github.com/RicardoRCarvalho/dota2-vis/公开。

</details>


### [67] [Geometric Gradient Rectification for Safe Open-Set Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26973)
*Jiahe Chen,Qian Shao,Qiyuan Chen,Jiaying He,Jintai Chen,Jian Wu,Hongxia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何梯度修正（GGR），通过梯度级控制而非样本选择来解决开放集半监督学习中利用与过滤的权衡问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放集半监督学习方法面临一个共同困境：激进过滤会丢弃困难但信息丰富的类内样本，而利用策略在伪标签错误时会引入与监督学习冲突的辅助梯度。

Method: 提出几何梯度修正（GGR），以监督梯度为锚点，将有冲突的辅助梯度投影到梯度空间的可容许区域，使修正后的辅助更新一阶非对抗，同时保留可能携带有用表征信号的正交分量。进一步引入子空间感知修正来稳定锚点以应对噪声小批量梯度。

Result: 在CIFAR和ImageNet基准测试中，GGR在大多数设置下提升了代表性OSSL基线方法，在闭集泛化和开集鲁棒性方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: 从样本选择转向梯度级控制是解决开放集半监督学习中利用与过滤权衡问题的有效途径。

Original Abstract: Open-set semi-supervised learning aims to leverage unlabeled data that may contain out-of-distribution outliers while maintaining performance on in-distribution classes. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: filtering suspicious samples or incorporating unlabeled objectives with soft weighting. We argue that both face a common trade-off: aggressive filtering can discard informative but hard ID samples, whereas utilization can introduce auxiliary gradients that conflict with supervised learning when pseudo labels are wrong. We therefore shift the focus from sample selection to gradient-level control. We propose \textit{Geometric Gradient Rectification} (GGR), a plug-in framework that uses the supervised gradient as an anchor and projects conflicting auxiliary gradients onto an admissible region in gradient space. This makes the applied auxiliary update first-order non-opposing within the rectified coordinate block while preserving orthogonal components that may still carry useful representation signals. We further extend GGR with subspace-aware rectification to stabilize the anchor under noisy mini-batch gradients. Experiments on CIFAR and ImageNet benchmarks show that GGR improves representative OSSL baselines in most settings and yields gains in both closed-set generalization and open-set robustness. Code will be available at https://github.com/JiaheChen2002/GGR.

中文摘要翻译: 开放集半监督学习旨在利用可能包含分布外异常值的未标记数据，同时保持对分布内类别的性能。现有方法主要遵循两种范式：过滤可疑样本或结合软加权的未标记目标。我们认为两者都面临一个共同的权衡：激进过滤可能丢弃信息丰富但困难的类内样本，而利用策略在伪标签错误时会引入与监督学习冲突的辅助梯度。因此，我们将关注点从样本选择转向梯度级控制。我们提出几何梯度修正（GGR），这是一种即插即用框架，以监督梯度为锚点，将有冲突的辅助梯度投影到梯度空间的可容许区域。这使得修正后的辅助更新在修正坐标块内一阶非对抗，同时保留可能携带有用表征信号的正交分量。我们进一步扩展GGR，引入子空间感知修正以在噪声小批量梯度下稳定锚点。在CIFAR和ImageNet基准上的实验表明，GGR在大多数设置下提升了代表性OSSL基线方法，并在闭集泛化和开集鲁棒性方面均取得收益。代码将在https://github.com/JiaheChen2002/GGR公开。

</details>


### [68] [Unison: Benchmarking Unified Multimodal Models via Synergistic Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26984)
*Jinyu Liu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Unison基准，用于评估统一多模态模型的联合理解与生成能力，包含2169个样本、双轨评估机制和人类对齐的评价模型，揭示了现有模型在协同任务上的局限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在评估时将理解与生成能力割裂开，忽略了两者之间的协同效应，缺乏能够同时评估理解与生成联合性能的基准。

Method: 构建了包含2169个高质量样本的Unison基准，涵盖内部一致性、理解引导生成、生成引导理解和相互增强四个维度；设计了统一和解耦双轨评估机制实现细粒度诊断；并训练了与人类判断对齐的Unison-Judge评估模型。

Result: 通过对现有最先进统一多模态模型的系统评估，揭示了这些模型在联合理解与生成任务中的关键局限性，并指出了有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 当前统一多模态模型在理解与生成的协同任务上存在显著不足，Unison为诊断这些问题和指导未来研究提供了有效的评估框架。

Original Abstract: Unified multimodal models capable of both understanding and generation have achieved remarkable strides. However, despite their unified designs, existing evaluations typically assess understanding and generation capabilities in isolation, overlooking the synergy between comprehension and generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce Unison, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,169 high-quality unified task samples, designed to evaluate joint understanding and generation in unified multimodal models. Unison offers three key strengths: 1) Comprehensive Dimensions: Unison encompasses internal consistency, understanding-guided generation, generation-guided understanding, and mutual enhancement to enable holistic evaluation. 2) Diagnostic Evaluation: it provides both unified and decoupled tracks for understanding and generation, allowing fine-grained attribution of failure modes and quantitative analysis of the gains from unified modeling. 3) Human Alignment: we also introduce Unison-Judge, an evaluation model well aligned with human judgments to ensure reliable assessment. Based on systematic evaluations of state-of-the-art models on Unison, we uncover critical limitations in current unified multimodal systems and highlight promising directions for future research. Codes, Unison and Unison-Judge are publicly available at https://github.com/FudanCVL/Unison.

中文摘要翻译: 兼具理解与生成能力的统一多模态模型已取得了显著进展。然而，尽管模型设计是统一的，现有的评估通常将理解与生成能力割裂开来分别评测，忽视了两者之间的协同效应。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Unison——一个包含2169个高质量统一任务样本的综合基准，旨在评估统一多模态模型中的联合理解与生成能力。Unison具备三大关键优势：1）全面的维度：Unison涵盖内部一致性、理解引导生成、生成引导理解以及相互增强四个方面，实现整体性评估；2）诊断性评估：它同时提供了统一和解耦两种评估轨道，能够对理解与生成进行细粒度的失败模式归因，并对统一建模带来的增益进行定量分析；3）人类对齐：我们还引入了Unison-Judge，一个与人类判断高度对齐的评估模型，以确保评估的可靠性。基于对当前最先进模型在Unison上的系统性评估，我们揭示了现有统一多模态系统在联合理解与生成任务中的关键局限，并指出了未来研究中有前景的方向。代码、Unison和Unison-Judge已在https://github.com/FudanCVL/Unison公开。

</details>


### [69] [Event-Aware Instructed Assistant for Referring Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26994)
*Jinyu Liu,Henghui Ding,Shuting He,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVIS 提出将视频分解为多个简单事件，利用可学习的事件查询（Event Query）和对象-像素混合学习，实现层级化视频理解，在指代视频分割任务上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有指代视频分割方法将视频视为单一事件，忽略了视频通常包含多个不同事件的事实，导致模型需要直接理解所有复杂内容，容易产生混淆和幻觉。

Method: 提出 EVIS（事件感知视频指令分割助手），包括：(1) 文本引导的事件查询（Event Query）将视频分解为多个简单事件，提取事件感知的视觉-文本特征实现层级化理解；(2) 对象-像素混合学习，融合细粒度像素特征与先验对象查询，使多模态大语言模型能在长视频中跟踪目标。

Result: 在5个公开基准数据集上的大量实验结果表明，EVIS 在指代视频分割任务上表现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过将视频分解为事件级单元并从易到难逐步理解，可有效缓解模型对复杂视频内容的混淆与幻觉问题，提升指代视频分割性能。

Original Abstract: Existing referring video segmentation methods often treat a video as a single event consisting of multiple images, overlooking the fact that a video typically contains multiple distinct events. Under such a mechanism, the model needs to directly understand all the complex content in the video and text, which can easily lead to confusion and hallucinations. To address this issue, we propose to decompose a video to a set of simple events by learnable Event Query, and understand complex video content in an event-by-event, easy-to-understand manner. This is based on the observation that natural language expressions often divide a video into distinct, text-related segments, each representing a separate event within a compound event. We introduce EVIS, an Event-Aware Video Instructed Segmentation Assistant, which utilizes text-guided Event Queries to partition a video into simple events, extracting event-aware visual-text features to achieve a hierarchical understanding of the video. Additionally, we propose Object-Pixel-Hybrid Learning, which enables the MLLMs to track targets in long-term videos by integrating fine-grained pixel features with prior object queries. Extensive experimental results on 5 public benchmarks demonstrate EVIS's strong performance in addressing the referring video segmentation task.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的指代视频分割方法通常将视频视为由多张图像组成的单一事件，忽视了视频通常包含多个不同事件的事实。在这种机制下，模型需要直接理解视频和文本中的所有复杂内容，这容易导致混淆和幻觉。为解决这一问题，我们提出通过可学习的事件查询（Event Query）将视频分解为一组简单事件，并以逐事件、由易到难的方式理解复杂视频内容。该方法基于一个观察：自然语言表达往往将视频划分为不同的、与文本相关的片段，每个片段代表复合事件中的一个独立事件。我们提出了 EVIS，一个事件感知的视频指令分割助手，它利用文本引导的事件查询将视频划分为简单事件，提取事件感知的视觉-文本特征，实现对视频的层级化理解。此外，我们提出了对象-像素混合学习，使多模态大语言模型能够通过融合细粒度像素特征与先验对象查询，在长视频中跟踪目标。在5个公开基准上的大量实验结果表明，EVIS 在指代视频分割任务上表现出强劲的性能。

</details>


### [70] [On-board Remote-Sensing Foundation Models for Unsupervised Change Detection of Disaster Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27018)
*S. Ramírez-Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RSFM（ResNet）+ FPN的无监督变化检测方法，利用未训练的FPN架构在潜空间中检测语义偏移，实现对多种异常的高效识别，无需标签和定制训练，在不同地形和传感器上具有良好的泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统监督模型依赖昂贵标签，且定制化模型需要大量开发和训练成本。遥感基础模型（RSFMs）虽然强大，但在无监督异常检测方面仍缺乏高效、训练免费且具有高分辨率映射能力的方法。

Method: 基于ResNet（RSFM）作为编码器，结合未训练的FPN架构，通过检测连续轨道观测之间潜空间中的细微语义偏移来识别异常。采用图像级生成方式，利用FPN的内在先验实现更高分辨率的映射，无需任何训练。

Result: 该方法在无需训练的情况下，相比以往的补丁级、需训练的方案，实现了高效的图像级生成和更高分辨率映射；通过替换定制模型为RSFM，在消除定制训练和开发需求的同时，实现了可比较的结果，并在多种地形和传感器上保持高性能泛化。

Conclusion: 基于RSFM+FPN的无监督检测方法为灾害监测等遥感应用提供了一条无需标签、无需定制训练的高效路径，能够最大化星载有限资源的利用率，同时保证高保真特征提取和跨场景泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Remote Sensing Foundation Models (RSFMs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to supervised models for Earth Observation, allowing satellites to autonomously trigger high-resolution captures or adjust tasking parameters upon detecting an anomaly, thereby maximizing the utility of the mission's limited power and computational resources. RSFMs are versatile, unified encoders that optimize onboard storage for multiple orbital applications while ensuring high-fidelity feature extraction. In particular, unsupervised change detection with RSFMs offers a well-informed and transformative path for disaster monitoring without expensive labels. In this paper, we present a novel unsupervised detection method based on ResNet (RSFM) + FPN which identifies a wide spectrum of anomalies by detecting subtle semantic shifts in the latent space between successive orbital passes. By relying on an untrained FPN architecture and its intrinsic priors, the system achieves efficient image-level generation and higher resolution mapping with minimal effort (training-free) compared to previous proposals (patch-based, trained). And by replacing tailored models with RSFMs, we can achieve comparable results through an approach that eliminates the need for bespoke training and extensive development effort and adds customization, while ensuring high-performance generalization across diverse terrains and sensors.

中文摘要翻译: 遥感基础模型（RSFMs）已成为地球观测中有监督模型的强大替代方案，使得卫星能够在检测到异常时自主触发高分辨率拍摄或调整任务参数，从而最大化任务有限电力和计算资源的效用。RSFMs是通用、统一的编码器，可优化多个轨道应用的星载存储，同时确保高保真特征提取。特别是，使用RSFMs进行无监督变化检测为灾害监测提供了一条信息充分且变革性的路径，无需昂贵的标签。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于ResNet（RSFM）+ FPN的新型无监督检测方法，该方法通过检测连续轨道观测之间潜空间中的细微语义偏移来识别广泛的异常。通过依赖未训练的FPN架构及其内在先验，该系统实现了高效的图像级生成和更高分辨率的映射，且几乎无需训练成本（免训练），相比以往基于补丁的、需训练的方案更具优势。通过用RSFMs替代定制模型，我们可以在消除定制训练和大量开发需求的同时，获得可比较的结果，并增加了定制化能力，同时确保在不同地形和传感器上的高性能泛化。

</details>


### [71] [PanoImager: Geometry-Guided Novel View Synthesis and Reconstruction from Sparse Panoramic Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27071)
*Zhisong Xu,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoImager是一种无需SfM的框架，通过前馈位姿/深度先验、扩散视图补全和深度引导的3DGS优化，从稀疏全景图像中实现鲁棒的3D重建，尤其适用于旋转主导、弱视差的困难场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在旋转主导、弱视差的运动场景下，从稀疏全景图像进行3D重建具有挑战性，传统SfM/SLAM初始化往往病态且不可靠，亟需一种无需SfM的鲁棒方法。

Method: PanoImager将少量全景图像分解为局部透视视图，利用前馈位姿/深度先验提供初始估计，通过几何条件扩散模型完成视图补全以丰富稀疏证据，最后采用深度引导的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）优化来增强跨视图一致性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，PanoImager在极端稀疏条件下展现出更高的稳定性，可作为SfM/SLAM无法初始化时的离线/后台地图精化组件。

Conclusion: PanoImager验证了前馈先验与扩散模型结合的SfM-free框架在稀疏全景3D重建中的有效性，为全景感知场景下的鲁棒三维重建提供了新的解决路径。

Original Abstract: Panoramic sensing offers wide field-of-view coverage, yet 3D reconstruction from sparse panoramas remains challenging under rotation-dominant, weak-parallax motion. In such regimes, SfM/SLAM initialization is often ill-conditioned and unreliable. We present PanoImager, an SfM-free framework that combines feed-forward pose/depth priors, geometry-conditioned diffusion view completion, and depth-guided 3DGS optimization. Given only a few panoramic images, PanoImager decomposes them into local perspective views, synthesizes auxiliary observations to enrich sparse evidence, and stabilizes Gaussian optimization for improved cross-view consistency. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show improved stability under extreme sparsity, suggesting PanoImager as an offline/background component for map refinement when SfM/SLAM fails to initialize.

中文摘要翻译: 全景感知提供了广阔的视场覆盖，然而在旋转主导、弱视差的运动条件下，从稀疏全景图像进行三维重建仍然具有挑战性。在这种场景下，SfM/SLAM的初始化往往是病态且不可靠的。我们提出了PanoImager，一个无需SfM的框架，它结合了前馈位姿/深度先验、几何条件扩散视图补全以及深度引导的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）优化。仅需少量全景图像，PanoImager将其分解为局部透视视图，合成辅助观测以丰富稀疏证据，并稳定高斯优化以增强跨视图一致性。多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在极端稀疏条件下具有更高的稳定性，表明PanoImager可作为SfM/SLAM无法初始化时的离线/后台地图精化组件。

</details>


### [72] [SubdivAR: Autoregressive Next-Scale Prediction for Neural Mesh Subdivision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27088)
*Huipeng Guo,Zikai Song,Hang Long,Jielei Zhang,Wenbing Li,Junkai Lin,Tianhao Zhao,Jinshen Zhang,Tianle Guo,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SubdivAR神经网格细分框架，基于网格自回归表示(MAR)将细分建模为自回归下一尺度预测，采用混合拓扑感知Transformer，在FII-40K数据集上训练，显著降低了Hausdorff距离和Chamfer距离。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于规则的网格细分方法依赖固定局部细化规则，易产生过平滑表面；而神经细分方法受限于局部建模，泛化能力有限。需要一种能同时保持细分拓扑结构并恢复细粒度几何细节的方法。

Method: 提出Mesh Autoregressive Representation (MAR)，将不同细分层次的网格组织为有序尺度序列，将细分重构为自回归下一尺度预测。设计混合拓扑感知Transformer，结合全局语义注意力与拓扑约束的局部特征聚合。采用下一尺度坐标预测范式，在每个细化阶段回归顶点偏移量。

Result: 在FII-40K数据集上，SubdivAR在Hausdorff距离和Chamfer距离上分别比最先进基线降低18.8%和14.2%，在复杂开放曲面几何上表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubdivAR通过自回归下一尺度预测范式有效结合了拓扑保持与细节恢复，优于现有方法，且泛化能力强。

Original Abstract: Mesh subdivision is a fundamental operation for converting coarse, editable meshes into high-resolution surfaces, with broad applications in digital asset creation. Classical rule-based schemes rely on fixed local refinement rules and often produce over-smoothed surfaces. Recent neural subdivision methods improve detail synthesis, but remain constrained by local modeling and exhibit limited generalizability. We present SubdivAR, a neural mesh subdivision framework based on our proposed Mesh Autoregressive Representation (MAR). MAR arranges meshes at different subdivision levels into an ordered scale sequence, reformulating subdivision as autoregressive next-scale prediction. To support this formulation, we introduce a Hybrid Topology-Aware Transformer that combines global semantic attention with topology-constrained local feature aggregation. SubdivAR adopts a next-scale coordinate prediction paradigm, regressing vertex offsets at each refinement stage to preserve subdivision topology while recovering fine-grained geometric details. To enable reliable learning, we construct FII-40K, a curated dataset of nearly 40,000 high-quality meshes with multi-level subdivision supervision. Experiments show that SubdivAR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing Hausdorff Distance and Chamfer Distance by 18.8% and 14.2%, respectively, and demonstrates strong robustness on complex open-surface geometries.

中文摘要翻译: 网格细分是将粗糙的可编辑网格转换为高分辨率曲面的基本操作，在数字资产创建中具有广泛应用。经典的基于规则的方法依赖固定的局部细化规则，通常会产生过平滑的表面。最近的神经细分方法改善了细节合成，但仍受限于局部建模，表现出有限的泛化能力。我们提出SubdivAR，一种基于我们提出的网格自回归表示(MAR)的神经网格细分框架。MAR将不同细分层次的网格组织成有序的尺度序列，将细分重新表述为自回归的下一尺度预测。为支持这一表述，我们引入了一种混合拓扑感知Transformer，它将全局语义注意力与拓扑约束的局部特征聚合相结合。SubdivAR采用下一尺度坐标预测范式，在每个细化阶段回归顶点偏移量，以在恢复细粒度几何细节的同时保持细分拓扑。为实现可靠学习，我们构建了FII-40K，一个包含近40,000个高质量网格及其多级细分监督的精选数据集。实验表明，SubdivAR优于最先进的基线方法，将Hausdorff距离和Chamfer距离分别降低了18.8%和14.2%，并在复杂开放曲面几何上展现出强大的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [73] [TMP: Tree-structured Mixed-policy Pruning for Large-scale Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27089)
*Peizhen Zhang,Yang Li,Xunsong Li,Songtao Liu,Zewen Liu,Qiangqiang Hu,Guotong Guo,Jupeng Ding,Yifu Sun,coopersli,Jian Zhang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TMP框架，首个树结构混合策略剪枝框架，可泛化到多种图像生成任务和架构（MoE、DiT），将HunyuanImage 3.0从80B压缩至20B（减少75%），并在单张24GB 4090上实现推理；同时将Z-Image turbo从6B压缩至4B（减少33%），质量损失极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代图像生成模型规模快速增长导致参数消耗和计算预算巨大，GPU内存占用过高，使得模型部署和推理成本难以承受。亟需一种能有效压缩模型同时保持生成质量的方法。

Method: 提出TMP（Tree-structured Mixed-policy Pruning）框架，采用树结构混合策略剪枝方法，可泛化到文本到图像（T2I）和文本引导图像到图像（TI2I）任务，以及混合专家（MoE）和扩散Transformer（DiT）架构，并可应用于步长蒸馏模型作为最终阶段。

Result: 将HunyuanImage 3.0从80B参数压缩至20B（75%缩减），生成质量损失有限；通过工程优化实现压缩后的20B版本在单张24GB 4090 GPU上推理；将Z-Image turbo从6B压缩至4B（33%缩减），退化可忽略不计。

Conclusion: TMP框架能有效压缩大规模图像生成模型，在极大减少参数量的同时保持可接受的生成质量，并能通过工程优化实现低成本GPU上的推理，具有实用价值。

Original Abstract: Modern image generation model rapidly grows their sizes to meet high-fidelity image synthesis. However, they gradually become unaffordable for their enormous parameter consumption and computation budget that lead to massive resources requirement and gpu memory footprint. In this paper, we propose TMP, the first Tree-structured Mixed-policy Pruning framework that generalizes prevalent image tasks (T2I and TI2I) and architectures (Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and Diffusion transformer (DiT)). It could be applied to the step-distilled models and contribute as the last stage. We perform experiments upon current open-sourced SOTA HunyuanImage-3.0 instruct and a popular efficient model Z-Image turbo. The proposed pruning framework manages to compress HunyuanImage 3.0 from 80B to 20B parameters at 75% reduction ratio, sacrificing limited generation quality. We also optimize to enable the inference of the pruned 20B version of HunyuanImage 3.0 on a single 24GB 4090 GPU by engineering skills. The inference script and model weight have been integrated into the existing HunyuanImage3.0 open-source github and huggingface repository. Besides, we prove the efficacy of TMP by compressing Z-Image turbo from 6B to 4B (33% reduction) with negligible degradation.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像生成模型为了满足高保真图像合成需求，其规模迅速增长。然而，这些模型因其庞大的参数消耗和计算预算，导致大量资源需求和GPU内存占用，逐渐变得难以负担。在本文中，我们提出了TMP，首个树结构混合策略剪枝框架，该框架能够泛化到主流的图像任务（文本到图像和文本引导图像到图像）和架构（混合专家和扩散Transformer）。它可应用于步长蒸馏模型，并作为最终阶段发挥作用。我们在当前开源的SOTA模型HunyuanImage-3.0 instruct和一个流行的高效模型Z-Image turbo上进行了实验。所提出的剪枝框架成功地将HunyuanImage 3.0从80B参数压缩至20B参数，缩减率高达75%，仅牺牲了有限的生成质量。我们还通过工程技巧优化，使得压缩后的20B版HunyuanImage 3.0能够在单张24GB 4090 GPU上进行推理。推理脚本和模型权重已集成到现有的HunyuanImage3.0开源GitHub和HuggingFace仓库中。此外，我们通过将Z-Image turbo从6B压缩至4B（33%缩减）且退化可忽略不计，证明了TMP的有效性。

</details>


### [74] [FlameVQA: A Physically-Grounded UAV Wildfire VQA Benchmark with Radiometric Thermal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27128)
*Mobin Habibpour,John Spodnik,Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlameVQA，一个面向无人机森林火灾监测的多选题视觉问答基准数据集，包含基于FLAME 3数据集的配对RGB与热红外图像，涵盖六大类34个问题，并评估了多个多模态大语言模型的性能，发现现有模型在烟雾遮挡检测和覆盖度估计上存在明显不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RGB-only的无人机火灾监测在烟雾、尺度变化和遮挡场景下推理能力受限，缺乏结合热红外温度信息的多模态视觉问答基准，无法系统评估模型在安全关键任务中的表现。

Method: 基于FLAME 3数据集，利用配对的RGB与辐射热TIFF图像构建多选题VQA基准；通过MLLM辅助标注、确定性热红外规则、跨问题一致性检查和人工审计确保标签可靠性；每张图像包含34个多选题，覆盖检测、定位、分布/覆盖估计、跨模态推理和飞行规划六大能力组。

Result: 在显式跨模态线索可用时模型表现较好，但在浓烟场景下的存在检测和覆盖度估计任务中表现显著失败，表明当前MLLM需要领域特定适配才能更好地支持灾害与火灾监测。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在无人机火灾监测任务中仍存在明显短板，尤其是烟雾遮挡和覆盖估计场景，需进行领域特定的适应和优化。

Original Abstract: Wildfire monitoring from UAVs requires reliable reasoning over complex aerial scenes, where smoke, scale variation, and occlusions often limit RGB-only interpretation. We introduce FlameVQA, a multiple-choice visual question answering benchmark for UAV-based wildfire intelligence built on FLAME 3, leveraging paired RGB imagery and radiometric thermal TIFFs for temperature-grounded, safety-critical reasoning. FlameVQA includes 34 multiple-choice questions per image spanning six operational capability groups, covering tasks such as detection, localization, distribution/coverage estimation, cross-modal reasoning, and flight planning. To ensure label reliability, we combine MLLM-assisted annotation with deterministic thermal rules and cross-question consistency checks, followed by human auditing. We also evaluate representative MLLMs on FlameVQA to provide baselines for future work. Results show strong performance when explicit cross-modal cues are available, but notable failures on presence detection under heavy smoke and on coverage estimation. These findings suggest that current MLLMs require domain-specific adaptation to better support disaster and wildfire monitoring. The dataset and benchmark code are open-source at github.com/mobiiin/WildFire_VQA

中文摘要翻译: 无人机森林火灾监测需要对复杂的空中场景进行可靠的推理，而烟雾、尺度变化和遮挡常常限制了仅依赖RGB图像的解读。本文提出了FlameVQA，一个面向无人机火灾情报的多选题视觉问答基准，该基准基于FLAME 3数据集，利用配对的RGB图像与辐射热TIFF图像，实现基于温度的、安全关键的推理。FlameVQA每张图像包含34个多选题，涵盖六大操作能力组，包括检测、定位、分布/覆盖估计、跨模态推理和飞行规划等任务。为确保标签可靠性，我们结合了多模态大语言模型辅助标注、确定性热红外规则以及跨问题一致性检查，并辅以人工审核。我们还在FlameVQA上评估了具有代表性的多模态大语言模型，为未来工作提供基线。结果表明，当存在显式跨模态线索时模型表现良好，但在浓烟场景下的存在检测和覆盖度估计任务中出现了显著失败。这些发现表明，当前的多模态大语言模型需要领域特定的适配，以更好地支持灾害与火灾监测。数据集和基准代码已在github.com/mobiiin/WildFire_VQA开源。

</details>


### [75] [Safe Autoregressive Image Generation with Iterative Self-Improving Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27147)
*Yunqi Xue,Zhijiang Li,Philip Torr,Jindong Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出迭代自改进的码本方法，通过统一多模态模型自身判断不安全生成，并修复码本中的有害映射，实现自回归图像生成的安全增强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归统一多模态模型在文本到图像生成中表现出色，但以离散视觉token序列方式生成的图像安全性未知，需探索其安全问题并提出改进方法。

Method: 两步骤迭代方法：1）利用统一模型自身识别不安全生成，构建有害/安全图像-文本对，构造有害空间并更新码本消除有害映射；2）在无害空间中对码本进行自适应微调，使用安全图像-文本对保证生成质量。重复迭代直至收敛。

Result: 无需外部人工反馈，通过模型自身能力迭代提升安全性，产出安全增强的模型码本。

Conclusion: 提出的迭代自改进码本方法能有效消除自回归生成中的不安全输出，且无需额外人类标注，实现模型安全性的自主迭代提升。

Original Abstract: Unlike diffusion-based models that operate in continuous latent spaces, autoregressive unified multimodal models produce images by sequentially predicting discretized visual tokens. These tokens are derived from a codebook that maps embeddings to quantized visual patterns. The language-like architecture enables unified multimodal models to effectively capture text conditional information for generation, making them promising for text-to-image tasks. This also raises an interesting question: how safe are the images generated in such an autoregressive way? In this work, we propose iterative self-improving codebooks for safe autoregressive generation. We leverage the understanding and judgment capabilities of the unified multimodal model itself to identify unsafe generated images without human annotation. Subsequently, the inherent representations in the codebook are fixed to eliminate harmful mappings. Our method comprises two steps: first, we use the unified model to identify unsafe generations and construct corresponding harmful and safe image-text pairs. These pairs are used to construct the Harmful Space and guide updates to the codebook, thereby eliminating harmful outputs. Second, we perform adaptive fine-tuning on the codebook within the harmless space using safe image-text pairs to ensure the quality of generated images. These two steps are repeated until no further improvement is observed, producing a safety-enhanced model codebook. Without additional external feedback, the safety of models is improved iteratively.

中文摘要翻译: 与在连续潜在空间中运行的扩散模型不同，自回归统一多模态模型通过顺序预测离散化的视觉token来生成图像。这些token源自一个将嵌入映射到量化视觉模式的码本。类语言架构使统一多模态模型能够有效捕获用于生成的文本条件信息，使其在文本到图像任务中具有前景。这也引出了一个有趣的问题：以这种自回归方式生成的图像有多安全？在这项工作中，我们提出了用于安全自回归生成的迭代自改进码本方法。我们利用统一多模态模型自身的理解和判断能力，在无需人工标注的情况下识别不安全的生成图像。随后，修复码本中的固有表示以消除有害映射。我们的方法包括两个步骤：首先，利用统一模型识别不安全生成，并构建相应的有害和安全的图像-文本对。这些对用于构造有害空间并指导码本的更新，从而消除有害输出。其次，我们在无害空间内使用安全的图像-文本对，对码本进行自适应微调，以确保生成图像的质量。重复这两步直至无进一步改进，即可产生一个安全性增强的模型码本。在无需额外外部反馈的情况下，模型的安全性得以迭代提升。

</details>


### [76] [HarmVideoBench: Benchmarking Harmful Video Understanding in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27187)
*Jiajun Wu,Haoyu Kang,Yining Sun,Jiacheng Hou,Heng Zhang,Danyang Zhang,Zhenjun Zhao,Haochi Zhang,Leixin Sun,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Yushan Li,Ruiyu Li,Mengkai Huang,Yan Gao,Xu Zhang,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HarmVideoBench多层级有害视频理解基准，包含1379个视频和4137道选择题，从三个层次（显性证据、片段内部含义、跨片段推理）评估LVLM，并引入BCR方法动态检索上下文，将宏观平均准确率从61.7%提升至84.4%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有有害视频基准存在两个主要缺陷：1) 忽视有害视频的多层次特性，仅作为二分类任务评估，无法捕捉隐含或深层上下文危害；2) 完全缺乏解释性理由，仅判断模型是否正确标记视频，导致评估成为黑箱，模型可通过表面捷径取得成功。

Method: 1) 构建HarmVideoBench基准，包含1379个视频和4137道多项选择题，从三个层次（显性证据、片段内部含义、跨片段推理）评估模型；2) 提出BCR方法，预测推理边界并仅在需要时动态检索上下文。

Result: 在HarmVideoBench上评估了19个主流模型；BCR方法将基础模型的宏观平均准确率从61.7%提升至84.4%，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: HarmVideoBench能够更全面地评估模型对有害视频的深度理解能力，BCR方法通过动态上下文检索显著提升了有害视频理解的性能。

Original Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently shown immense potential in automated content moderation, sparking growing interest in developing harmful-video benchmarks. However, we identify two primary limitations in existing works: 1) The multi-layered characteristics of harmful videos are overlooked. Existing benchmarks predominantly formulate evaluation as a binary classification task, failing to capture implicit or deep contextual harms. 2) Explanatory rationales are completely absent. Current frameworks measure exclusively whether a model flags a video correctly rather than explaining why, turning evaluation into a black box where models can succeed through superficial shortcuts. To address these problems, we present HarmVideoBench, a multi-layered diagnostic benchmark comprising 1,379 videos paired with 4,137 multiple-choice questions. HarmVideoBench benchmarks three hierarchical dimensions: Observable Evidence, Clip-Internal Meaning, and Beyond-Clip Reasoning, aiming to evaluate models' deep understanding beyond surface cues with carefully balanced and curated samples. We evaluate 19 leading models on HarmVideoBench to assess their multidimensional understanding of harmful videos. Moreover, we introduce BCR, a benchmark-aligned method that predicts reasoning boundaries and dynamically retrieves context only when needed. Experimental results show that BCR substantially improves the base model's performance in harmful video understanding, raising the macro average from 61.7 percent to a state-of-the-art 84.4 percent.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）近来在自动内容审核中展现出巨大潜力，激发了人们对开发有害视频基准的日益兴趣。然而，我们发现现有工作存在两个主要局限：1）有害视频的多层次特性被忽视。现有基准主要将评估构建为二分类任务，未能捕捉隐式或深层上下文危害。2）解释性理由完全缺失。当前框架仅衡量模型是否正确标记视频，而非解释其原因，使评估沦为黑箱，模型可通过表面捷径取得成功。为解决这些问题，我们提出HarmVideoBench，一个多层级诊断基准，包含1,379个视频及其配对的4,137道多项选择题。HarmVideoBench基准测试三个层次维度：显性证据、片段内部含义和跨片段推理，旨在通过精心平衡和筛选的样本评估模型超越表面线索的深层理解能力。我们在HarmVideoBench上评估了19个主流模型，以评估它们对有害视频的多维度理解。此外，我们引入了BCR方法，一种与基准对齐的方法，用于预测推理边界并仅在需要时动态检索上下文。实验结果表明，BCR显著提升了基础模型在有害视频理解中的性能，将宏观平均准确率从61.7%提升至84.4%，达到最先进水平。

</details>


### [77] [LISA: Likelihood Score Alignment for Visual-condition Controllable Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27192)
*Yanghao Wang,Hongxu Chen,Jiazhen Liu,Zhenqi He,Rui Liu,Zhen Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA方法，通过将侧网络的中间特征与近似似然分数对齐，加速视觉条件可控生成的训练收敛并提升合成质量，且不增加额外推理成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有双分支范式（侧网络+冻结主网络）在视觉条件生成中应用广泛，但侧分支的作用及其训练效率尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出LISA（Likelihood Score Alignment）正则化方法：1) 从侧网络指定层钩取特征，通过轻量解码器投影到分数隐空间；2) 构建近似似然分数目标，计算解码器输出与目标的距离作为额外正则化损失；3) 联合优化侧网络和解码器，结合标准扩散损失和正则化损失。

Result: 在多种图像/视频任务、架构及扩散/流模型上，LISA能够一致地加速训练收敛、提升最终合成结果，且使侧网络特征更解耦用于条件建模，仅需极少额外训练成本，零额外推理成本。

Conclusion: LISA是一种有效的正则化方法，能从分数生成建模的角度解释双分支范式，并在多种条件下验证了其加速收敛和提升质量的有效性。

Original Abstract: The prevalent dual-branch paradigm, i.e., training a side network to encode visual conditions and fusing its intermediate-layer features to a frozen pretrained main network, has shown remarkable success in visual-condition controllable generation. Despite its widespread adoption, the role of the side branch and its training efficiency remain underexplored. In this paper, we first revisit this mainstream paradigm through the lens of score-based generative modeling: 1) The main network preserves visual perceptual quality by providing a prior unconditional score. 2) The side network steers conditional control by implicitly contributing a likelihood score. Guided by this perspective, we propose LIkelihood Score Alignment (LISA), an effective regularization method that explicitly aligns the intermediate feature of the side network with an approximated likelihood score. Specifically, we first hook features from a designated layer of the side network and project them into the score latent space by a lightweight decoder. Then, we construct an approximated likelihood score target and calculate the distance between the decoder's output and this target as an additional regularization loss. Finally, we jointly optimize the side network and decoder with both standard diffusion loss and our regularization loss. Experiments across various image/video tasks, architectures, and diffusion/flow models demonstrated that LISA can not only consistently accelerate the training convergence and improve final synthetic results, but also encourage the side network's features to be more disentangled for conditional modeling with negligible additional training cost and zero extra inference cost.

中文摘要翻译: 主流双分支范式——即训练一个侧网络编码视觉条件，并将其中间层特征融合到冻结的预训练主网络中——已在视觉条件可控生成中取得了显著成功。尽管该范式被广泛采用，但侧分支的作用及其训练效率仍未得到充分探索。本文首先通过分数生成建模的视角重新审视了这一主流范式：1) 主网络通过提供先验无条件分数来保持视觉感知质量；2) 侧网络通过隐式贡献似然分数来引导条件控制。在该视角的指导下，我们提出了似然分数对齐（LISA），一种有效的正则化方法，明确地将侧网络的中间特征与近似的似然分数对齐。具体来说，我们首先从侧网络的指定层钩取特征，并通过轻量解码器将其投影到分数隐空间。然后，我们构建一个近似的似然分数目标，并计算解码器输出与该目标之间的距离作为额外的正则化损失。最后，我们联合优化侧网络和解码器，同时使用标准扩散损失和我们的正则化损失。在多种图像/视频任务、架构和扩散/流模型上的实验表明，LISA不仅能一致地加速训练收敛并改善最终合成结果，还能促进侧网络特征在条件建模中更加解耦，且仅需微小的额外训练成本，零额外推理成本。

</details>


### [78] [SatSplatDiff: Geometry-preserving generative refinement for high-fidelity satellite Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27223)
*Jiyong Kim,Shuang Song,Ronjgun Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatSplatDiff提出了一种阴影引导的生成式精化方法，在卫星场景的3D高斯泼溅重建中同时提升视觉保真度和几何精度，相比现有方法几何MAE降低18%，FID-CLIP提升28-45%，并支持5倍超分辨率重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卫星影像俯视视角有限，导致建筑立面监督不足，产生表面空洞和视觉退化。现有的生成式精化方法对每个视图独立精化，会引入幻觉并破坏光度一致性，造成几何退化。

Method: 基于光度测量DSM初始化和2DGS阴影投射（来自前期工作SatSplat），首先引入单目深度监督和多尺度几何精化以建立几何精确且规则化的表面表示；然后采用阴影引导的生成式精化，利用几何计算的阴影图引导高斯体保持与底层几何的一致性，从而在提升视觉保真度的同时减少几何退化。

Result: 在IARPA2016和DFC2019数据集上，几何MAE降低高达18%，视觉保真度（FID-CLIP）提升28-45%；支持5倍分辨率增强且幻觉极少，具有无缝的跨瓦片一致性和大规模重建的可扩展性。

Conclusion: SatSplatDiff通过阴影引导的生成式精化有效解决了卫星3D重建中几何退化与视觉保真度之间的矛盾，达到了最先进的性能，并展现出强大的实际应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has been recently explored for satellite 3D reconstruction, demonstrating flexibility and efficiency in representing radiometrically diverse satellite scenes. However, the limited top viewpoint of satellite imagery results in insufficient supervision on building facades, leaving surface holes and degraded visual fidelity. Generative refinement, which leverages pretrained generative priors to iteratively refine and update the rendered images used as supervision targets, has recently been investigated to improve the visual fidelity of Gaussian-rendered images. However, since these models refine each view independently, the resulting images can generate hallucinations and break photo-consistency, leading to geometric degradation. To address these limitations, we propose SatSplatDiff, which aims to minimize geometric degradation prevalent in generative refinement. Building on photogrammetric DSM initialization and 2DGS-based shadow casting established in our prior work SatSplat, we first introduce monocular depth supervision and multi-scale geometric refinement to establish a geometrically accurate and well-regularized surface representation. We then apply shadow-guided generative refinement, where geometrically calculated shadow maps guide the Gaussians to maintain consistency with the underlying geometry, improving visual fidelity while reducing geometric degradation. Extensive evaluations on the IARPA2016 and DFC2019 datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing geometric MAE by up to 18% and improving visual fidelity (FID-CLIP) by 28-45% over existing baselines. Our method delivers up to 5x resolution enhancement with minimal hallucination and sensor-consistent appearance, demonstrating seamless cross-tile consistency and strong scalability for large-scale reconstruction. Source code is available at https://github.com/GDAOSU/SatSplatDiff

中文摘要翻译: 高斯泼溅近年来被探索用于卫星三维重建，在表示辐射度量多样化的卫星场景方面展现出灵活性和高效性。然而，卫星影像有限的俯视视角导致建筑立面监督不足，产生表面空洞和视觉保真度下降。生成式精化——利用预训练的生成先验迭代式精化并更新用作监督目标的渲染图像——近期被研究用于提升高斯渲染图像的视觉保真度。然而，由于这些模型独立精化每个视图，生成的图像可能产生幻觉并破坏光度一致性，从而导致几何退化。为解决这些局限性，我们提出SatSplatDiff，旨在最小化生成式精化中普遍存在的几何退化。基于我们前期工作SatSplat中建立的光度测量DSM初始化和2DGS阴影投射，我们首先引入单目深度监督和多尺度几何精化，以建立几何精确且良好规则化的表面表示。随后我们应用阴影引导的生成式精化，其中几何计算的阴影图引导高斯体保持与底层几何的一致性，从而在提升视觉保真度的同时减少几何退化。在IARPA2016和DFC2019数据集上的大量评估表明，我们的方法达到了最先进的性能，几何MAE降低高达18%，视觉保真度（FID-CLIP）相比现有基线提升28-45%。我们的方法实现了高达5倍的分辨率增强，且幻觉极少、传感器一致性良好，展现出无缝的跨瓦片一致性和大规模重建的强大可扩展性。源代码可在https://github.com/GDAOSU/SatSplatDiff获取。

</details>


### [79] [CORTEX: A Structured Reasoning Benchmark for Trustworthy 3D Chest CT MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27264)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Anees Ur Rehman Hashmi,Numan Saeed,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan,Christoph Lippert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CORTEX，一个面向3D胸部CT的结构化推理基准数据集，通过四阶段诊断轨迹（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成）来恢复缺失的推理过程，并提供阶段级评估协议，填补了当前多模态大模型在3D医学影像推理中缺乏结构化监督和验证协议的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在医学影像中的推理通常是自由文本形式且仅通过最终答案评判，难以解释和验证；同时现有胸部CT问答数据集将专家报告简化为仅答案对，丢弃了从发现到结论的推理链条以及患者病史信息，导致缺乏训练和验证推理能力所需的结构化监督和协议，阻碍了具备推理能力的3D胸部CT MLLMs的发展。

Method: CORTEX通过四阶段诊断轨迹（任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理、答案合成）建模放射科医生的工作流程；利用具有广泛医学和通用知识的前沿大语言模型生成这些轨迹，然后结合自动评分和专家放射科医生审查的阶段级评估协议进行过滤和验证；推理结构和评估标准均与临床医生紧密合作设计。

Result: 基于CT-RATE数据集，CORTEX包含76,177个经过验证的推理轨迹，涵盖开放式VQA、封闭式VQA和报告生成三种任务，提供了结构化监督和阶段级评估协议。

Conclusion: CORTEX为构建和评估3D胸部CT的可信推理模型提供了必要的数据基础（结构化推理轨迹）和评估方法（阶段级评估协议），有望推动推理能力更强的3D胸部CT多模态大模型的发展。

Original Abstract: Reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, this reasoning is usually free-form text judged only by its final answer, making it hard to interpret and verify, especially in 3D radiology, where a diagnosis should be traceable to evidence in the scan. Existing chest CT question-answering datasets compound this by reducing expert radiology reports to answer-only pairs, dropping the reasoning that links findings to conclusions and omitting the patient history clinicians rely on. As a result, reasoning-capable 3D chest CT MLLMs remain out of reach, as neither the structured supervision needed to train them nor the protocol needed to verify their reasoning yet exists. We introduce CORTEX (Clinically Organized Reasoning and sTructured EXplanation), a structured reasoning benchmark for 3D chest CT. For each question, CORTEX restores the missing reasoning as a four-stage diagnostic trace mirroring a radiologist's workflow: task understanding, visual observation, diagnostic reasoning, and answer synthesis. We generate these traces using frontier large language models with broad medical and general-domain knowledge, then filter and verify them with a stage-level evaluation protocol combining automated rubric scoring with expert radiologist review. Crucially, both the reasoning structure and evaluation rubrics are designed in close collaboration with clinicians. Built on CT-RATE, a large, publicly available chest CT dataset without reasoning annotations, CORTEX comprises 76,177 validated reasoning traces across open-ended VQA, closed-ended VQA, and report generation, providing both the structured supervision and the stage-level evaluation protocol needed to build and evaluate trustworthy reasoning models for 3D chest CT. Our dataset and evaluation code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医学影像中的推理展现出了巨大潜力。然而，这种推理通常以自由文本形式呈现，仅通过最终答案来评判，导致其难以解释和验证，尤其是在三维放射学中，诊断结果应能追溯到扫描中的证据。现有的胸部CT问答数据集进一步加剧了这一问题：它们将专家的放射学报告简化为仅包含答案的配对，丢弃了将发现与结论联系起来的推理过程，并省略了临床医生依赖的患者病史。因此，具备推理能力的三维胸部CT多模态大语言模型仍然遥不可及——既缺乏训练所需的结构化监督，也缺少验证其推理所需的协议。我们提出了CORTEX（临床组织的推理与结构化解释），这是一个面向三维胸部CT的结构化推理基准。对于每个问题，CORTEX以模拟放射科医生工作流程的四阶段诊断轨迹来恢复缺失的推理过程：任务理解、视觉观察、诊断推理和答案合成。我们利用具有广泛医学和通用知识的前沿大语言模型生成这些轨迹，然后通过结合自动评分与专家放射科医生审查的阶段级评估协议进行过滤和验证。至关重要的是，推理结构和评估标准均与临床医生紧密合作设计。CORTEX基于CT-RATE（一个大型公开可用的胸部CT数据集，不含推理标注）构建，包含76,177个经过验证的推理轨迹，涵盖开放式视觉问答、封闭式视觉问答和报告生成三个任务，为构建和评估三维胸部CT的可信推理模型提供了所需的结构化监督和阶段级评估协议。我们的数据集和评估代码将在论文被接收后公开发布。

</details>


### [80] [Exact and Deterministic Patch Descriptor Retrieval via Hierarchical Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27280)
*Koichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种层级归一化（HN）方案，将补丁描述符检索从近似最近邻（ANN）变为确定性精确最近邻搜索，同时显著加速。通过在8维主分量上进行预筛选，仅0.4%的条目需完整128维评估，实现了13.7倍加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的近似最近邻（ANN）方法（如HNSW、IVF-PQ）为了速度牺牲了精确性，且多次运行结果不一致、非确定性。本文旨在实现一种既保持精确最近邻搜索的确定性（相同查询-数据库对始终返回相同结果），又能大幅加快速度的方法。

Method: 提出层级归一化（Hierarchical Normalization, HN）：将预处理特征向量拆分为K维主分量（范数sqrt(1-alpha)）和(128-K)维次分量（范数sqrt(alpha)）。利用Cauchy-Schwarz不等式限定次分量内积上限为alpha，因此主分量相似度加alpha可作为全向量相似度的可容许上界。搜索时先对所有条目计算K维主分量相似度进行剪枝，仅对无法被剪枝的条目进行完整128维评估——这是一种可证明精确的分支定界扫描。

Result: 在UBC补丁数据集的trevi和halfdome上进行评估。K=8, alpha=1/32时，相比暴力128维搜索获得13.7倍（trevi）/12.7倍（halfdome）加速，仅0.4%条目需完整评估。K=16, alpha=1/8时，trevi上7.2倍加速下FPR@95从0.0062略微升至0.0064，98.8%条目跳过完整评估。

Conclusion: HN方案成功实现了确定性精确最近邻搜索，在保持与穷举全向量搜索完全相同结果的前提下，通过智能剪枝大幅降低了计算开销。该方法可复现、与运行顺序/线程数/硬件无关，为需要确定性结果的场景提供了优于ANN的有效替代方案。

Original Abstract: We present a patch descriptor retrieval method that returns the exact nearest neighbour -- provably identical to exhaustive full-vector search -- while evaluating only a small fraction of the database, and does so deterministically: the same (database, query) pair always produces the same result, independent of run order, thread count, or hardware. This contrasts with approximate nearest-neighbour (ANN) approaches such as HNSW and IVF-PQ, which trade exactness for speed and may return different results across runs. The enabling mechanism is Hierarchical Normalization (HN): a normalisation scheme that splits the pre-normalisation feature vector into a K-dim major component (norm sqrt(1-alpha)) and a (128-K)-dim minor component (norm sqrt(alpha)). Since the minor inner product is bounded by alpha (Cauchy-Schwarz on the prescribed norms), the major similarity plus alpha is an admissible upper bound on the full similarity: the search scans the K-dim major component for all entries, then applies full 128-dim evaluation only to entries that cannot be pruned -- a provably exact branch-and-bound scan. We train HN-modified HardNet on the notredame split of the UBC patch dataset and evaluate on trevi and halfdome. With a cache-optimised Structure-of-Arrays layout and K=8, alpha=1/32, the search achieves 13.7x (trevi) / 12.7x (halfdome) speed-up over brute-force 128-dim search, with only 0.4% of entries requiring full evaluation. At K=16, alpha=1/8, FPR@95 rises from 0.0062 to 0.0064 on trevi at 7.2x speed-up, with 98.8% of entries bypassing full evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种补丁描述符检索方法，该方法能够返回精确的最近邻——可证明与穷举全向量搜索完全相同——同时仅评估数据库中的一小部分条目，并且是确定性的：相同（数据库，查询）对总是返回相同结果，与运行顺序、线程数或硬件无关。这与近似最近邻（ANN）方法（如HNSW和IVF-PQ）形成对比，后者为了速度而牺牲了精确性，并且可能在不同运行中返回不同结果。其使能机制是层级归一化（Hierarchical Normalization, HN）：一种归一化方案，将归一化前的特征向量拆分为一个K维主分量（范数sqrt(1-alpha)）和一个(128-K)维次分量（范数sqrt(alpha)）。由于次分量内积受alpha约束（柯西-施瓦茨不等式在指定范数下），主分量相似度加上alpha构成了全相似度的可容许上界：搜索对所有条目扫描K维主分量，然后仅对无法被剪枝的条目应用完整的128维评估——这是一种可证明精确的分支定界扫描。我们在UBC补丁数据集的notredame分割上训练了经过HN修改的HardNet，并在trevi和halfdome上进行了评估。采用缓存优化的结构数组（SoA）布局，在K=8、alpha=1/32的情况下，搜索相比暴力128维搜索实现了13.7倍（trevi）/12.7倍（halfdome）的加速，仅0.4%的条目需要完整评估。在K=16、alpha=1/8的情况下，trevi上FPR@95从0.0062略微上升至0.0064，实现了7.2倍加速，98.8%的条目绕过了完整评估。

</details>


### [81] [Sculpting NeRF Geometry: Human-Preference Fine-Tuning of a 3D-Aware Face GAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27305)
*Archer Moore,Mingming Gong,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需网格提取或多视图渲染的3D生成模型RLHF微调方法，直接在辐射场密度（σ）值上学习奖励，用小样本偏好数据优化3D人脸几何，在保持2D外观的同时显著提升几何质量（用户偏好率74.4%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于RLHF的3D生成方法大多需要将辐射场转换为显式网格，并依赖大量表面监督数据。作者希望绕过这些繁琐步骤，直接在隐式神经辐射场的密度场上进行偏好学习，实现更简洁、高效的3D几何优化。

Method: 1. 以预训练的3D感知生成模型（EG3D）为基础，直接读取NeRF的连续3D密度场（σ值）作为输入；2. 训练一个无需预训练的奖励模型，在少量偏好样本上即可学习；3. 引入密度一致性约束，在几何重塑过程中保持2D外观质量；4. 奖励模型仅提供几何学习信号，无需文本条件、网格提取或多视图渲染。

Result: 1. 微调后的生成器在74.4%的成对比较中被用户偏好；2. FID-50k从4.09上升至6.66，表明2D外观质量有可量化的有限代价；3. 仅需单一标注者的偏好数据即可作为概念验证；4. 奖励模型无需预训练，小样本即可训练。

Conclusion: 直接在NeRF密度场上进行RLHF微调是有效的，能以可接受的2D外观代价显著提升3D几何质量，且流程简洁（无需网格、多视图渲染或文本条件），为3D生成模型的偏好对齐提供了新的轻量级范式。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for 3D generation is now established across a number of works, but most existing pipelines optimise explicit surface representations, often by converting radiance fields into meshes and training heavily on surface-supervised data. We instead fine-tune a pretrained 3D-aware generative model directly from a learned reward over radiance-field density ($σ$) values, with no externally supplied mesh or shape prior. The reward model requires no pretraining, trains easily on a small set of preference samples, and yields robust improvement in 3D geometry. Working on an unconditional 3D-aware face GAN (EG3D), our reward reads the continuous 3D density field of the neural radiance field (NeRF) directly and supplies a geometry-only learning signal, requiring neither text conditioning, mesh extraction, nor multi-view rendering. A density-consistency constraint keeps the 2D appearance qualitatively similar while the geometry is reshaped, at a measurable but bounded distributional cost (FID-50k rises from 4.09 to 6.66): the fine-tuned generator, trained from the preferences of a single annotator as a proof of concept, produces face geometries preferred by users in 74.4% of pairwise comparisons.

中文摘要翻译: 基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）在3D生成领域已有诸多工作，但现有大多数流程优化的是显式表面表示，通常通过将辐射场转换为网格并在大量表面监督数据上训练来实现。本文则另辟蹊径，直接从基于辐射场密度（σ）值的学习奖励出发，对预训练的3D感知生成模型进行微调，无需外部提供的网格或形状先验。该奖励模型无需预训练，在少量偏好样本上即可轻松训练，并能带来3D几何的稳健改进。我们以无条件的3D感知人脸GAN（EG3D）作为研究对象，奖励模型直接读取神经辐射场（NeRF）的连续3D密度场，提供纯几何学习信号，既不需要文本条件，也无需网格提取或多视图渲染。密度一致性约束使得在几何重塑过程中2D外观在定性上保持相似，同时带来可量化但有限的质量代价（FID-50k从4.09上升至6.66）：作为概念验证，仅基于单名标注者的偏好数据进行微调的生成器，在74.4%的成对比较中产生更受用户偏好的面部几何。

</details>


### [82] [See & Sniff: Learning Visuo-Olfactory Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27307)
*Seongyu Kim,Seungwoo Lee,Hyeonggon Ryu,Joon Son Chung,Arda Senocak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SmellNet-V 跨模态视觉-嗅觉数据集和 See & Sniff 自监督框架，通过语义对齐的图文对实现视觉与嗅觉的联合表征学习，在气味分类、跨模态检索和气味定位任务上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态感知研究中嗅觉方向严重滞后，主要瓶颈在于缺乏配对的视觉-嗅觉数据。本文旨在解决这一数据稀缺问题，探索视觉与嗅觉的跨模态学习。

Method: 基于"气味身份在同一语义类别内对视觉变换具有不变性"这一洞察，将仅有气味标签的单模态数据与语义对齐的网络图像合成配对，构建 SmellNet-V 数据集；并提出 See & Sniff 自监督框架，通过密集局部对齐学习联合视觉-嗅觉表征，并可自然生成气味显著性图进行气味源空间定位。

Result: 仅使用气味数据在气味分类任务上比纯气味基线提升7%；在跨模态检索和气味定位任务上均表现优越，并建立了像素级气味定位任务的基准。

Conclusion: 本文验证了视觉-嗅觉跨模态学习的可行性和有效性，为多模态感知研究开辟了嗅觉这一新方向。

Original Abstract: While modern multimodal models integrate vision with language, audio, or touch, olfaction remains largely unexplored due to the lack of paired visuo-olfactory data. We introduce SmellNet-V, a scalable visuo-olfactory dataset built on the insight that odor identity is largely invariant to visual transformations within a semantic category. This allows us to synthetically pair smell-only samples with semantically aligned in-the-wild web images, converting a unimodal olfactory dataset into a cross-modal benchmark without costly co-collection. Building on this dataset, we propose See & Sniff, a self-supervised framework that learns joint visuo-olfactory representations via dense local alignment and naturally produces smell saliency maps for spatial grounding of odor sources. We further introduce pixel-level smell localization task and a benchmark for evaluation. Our method surpasses smell-only baselines by 7% in smell classification from smell alone and generalizes to cross-modal retrieval and smell localization, establishing visuo-olfactory learning as a new direction in multimodal perception.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管现代多模态模型已能将视觉与语言、音频或触觉融合，但由于缺乏配对的视觉-嗅觉数据，嗅觉模态仍未得到充分探索。我们提出 SmellNet-V，一个可扩展的视觉-嗅觉数据集，其构建基于以下洞见：气味身份在语义类别内对视觉变换基本保持不变。这使得我们能够将仅含气味标签的样本与语义对齐的网络图像进行合成配对，从而将单模态嗅觉数据集转化为跨模态基准，无需昂贵的联合数据采集。在此数据集基础上，我们提出 See & Sniff，一个自监督框架，通过密集局部对齐学习联合视觉-嗅觉表征，并自然生成气味显著性图，用于气味源的时空定位。我们进一步引入像素级气味定位任务及相应评估基准。我们的方法在仅凭气味数据的气味分类任务上超越纯气味基线7%，同时泛化到跨模态检索和气味定位任务，将视觉-嗅觉学习确立为多模态感知的一个新方向。

</details>


### [83] [ViQ: Text-Aligned Visual Quantized Representations at Any Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27313)
*Xumin Yu,Zuyan Liu,Zhenyu Yang,Yuhao Dong,Shengsheng Qian,Jiwen Lu,Han Hu,Yongming Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViQ提出了一种视觉量化表示框架，通过两阶段量化学习（文本对齐预训练+特征离散化），在离散视觉表示中平衡语义与细节，支持原生分辨率输入，在多模态任务上取得与连续高维特征相当的性能，并实现20%-70%的训练加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本-视觉统一离散表示方法难以平衡低层细节与高层语义：重建导向的表示缺乏语义信息，而语义更强的特征则严重损失细节。亟需一种既能保留丰富语义又能保持高精度重建的离散视觉表示方法。

Method: ViQ将量化学习分为两个阶段：(1) 文本对齐预训练——利用预训练语言模型增强视觉编码器的语义监督，使其能处理原生分辨率输入；(2) 特征离散化——提出近邻表示学习策略逐步压缩特征空间，并采用位置感知的逐头量化机制灵活处理任意分辨率。

Result: ViQ在多模态任务上与使用连续高维特征的SOTA视觉编码器性能相当；在低层重建上保持高精度；多模态训练效率提升显著，在不同基础LLM和训练方案下实现20%-70%的加速。

Conclusion: ViQ作为一种统一通用的离散视觉表示框架，成功平衡了离散表示中的语义与细节，支持原生分辨率输入，兼具高性能与高效率，可服务于任意视觉输入的统一表示。

Original Abstract: A unified representation for text and vision is a natural pursuit, as it enables simpler multimodal modeling and more efficient training. However, representing images as discrete signals in the same way as text inevitably introduces severe information loss. Existing work struggles to balance low-level details and high-level semantics in discrete representations: reconstruction-oriented representations often lack semantic information, whereas semantically stronger features typically suffer from severe loss of detail. We present ViQ, a Visual Quantized Representations framework, which is designed to balance semantics and details in discrete representations while supporting inputs at native resolutions, thereby enabling it to serve as a unified and general discrete representation for arbitrary visual inputs. Our approach structures quantization learning into two stages: text-aligned pre-training and feature discretization. With text-aligned pre-training, we enhance the visual encoder semantic-rich supervision from the pretrained language model and enable it to process native-resolution visual inputs. During discretization, we propose a proximal representation learning strategy to progressively compact the feature space, along with a position-aware head-wise quantization mechanism that enables flexible processing of arbitrary resolutions. Extensive experiments on multimodal tasks demonstrate that ViQ achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art multimodal vision encoders with continuous and high-dimensional visual features, while maintaining high precision in low-level reconstruction. We also show that multimodal training with visual quantized representations largely improves efficiency, yielding up to 20\%-70\% acceleration with different base LLMs and training recipes.

中文摘要翻译: 文本与视觉的统一表示是一个自然的研究追求，因为它能够实现更简单的多模态建模和更高效的训练。然而，以与文本相同的方式将图像表示为离散信号不可避免地引入了严重的信息损失。现有工作在离散表示中难以平衡低层细节与高层语义：重建导向的表示往往缺乏语义信息，而语义更强的特征则通常遭受严重的细节损失。我们提出ViQ，一个视觉量化表示框架，旨在离散表示中平衡语义与细节，同时支持原生分辨率输入，从而使其能够作为任意视觉输入的统一通用离散表示。我们的方法将量化学习构建为两个阶段：文本对齐预训练和特征离散化。通过文本对齐预训练，我们利用预训练语言模型增强视觉编码器的丰富语义监督，并使其能够处理原生分辨率视觉输入。在离散化过程中，我们提出了一种近邻表示学习策略，以逐步压缩特征空间，同时引入位置感知的逐头量化机制，实现任意分辨率的灵活处理。在多模态任务上的广泛实验表明，ViQ在使用连续高维视觉特征的最先进多模态视觉编码器面前取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时在低层重建中保持高精度。我们还表明，使用视觉量化表示进行多模态训练大幅提升了效率，在不同的基础LLM和训练方案下实现了高达20%-70%的加速。

</details>


### [84] [OctoSense: Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27317)
*Anthony Bisulco,Jeremy Wang,Kostas Daniilidis,Randall Balestriero,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctoSense 是一个开源多模态传感器平台与数据集（含59小时驾驶数据），提出了一种"晚期融合掩码自编码器"方法，能够高效融合异构传感器数据，在多项任务中超越纯图像基础模型，且在传感器退化或夜间场景下依然鲁棒。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器人感知系统大多依赖单一传感器或简单融合，缺乏能够处理不同传感器（如事件相机、热成像、LiDAR等）在表示形式、频率、延迟和噪声方面差异的统一框架，且缺乏包含传感器退化场景的大规模多模态数据集。

Method: 提出一种"晚期融合掩码自编码器"（late-fusion masked autoencoder），其核心包括：(i) 使用模态特定的分词器（tokenizer）来建模不同传感器的时空特性；(ii) 在推理时缓存模态特定的 token，按需处理新到达的传感器数据，实现异步高效推理。

Result: (i) 推理速度快（NVIDIA 5090上6.68ms，Orin NX上112ms）；(ii) 在光流估计、深度估计、语义分割、自我运动估计等任务上优于纯图像基础模型；(iii) 在夜间或传感器退化场景下依然保持鲁棒预测能力。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过设计合适的模态特定分词和晚期融合策略，能够在真实机器人多模态数据上实现高效且鲁棒的自监督学习，为异构传感器融合提供了新的范式和基准数据集。

Original Abstract: We present OctoSense, an open-source sensor platform with stereo RGB and event cameras, LiDAR, a thermal camera, an inertial measurement unit, RTK-corrected global positioning system, and proprioception (CAN bus data from a car, and joint angles for a quadruped robot). The eponymous OctoSense dataset contains 59 hours of time-synchronized driving data across different types of environments at different times of the day, including situations with highly degraded sensors. We demonstrate multi-modal self-supervised learning using such real-world robotics data, where sensors have different representations, frequencies, latencies and noise. Our approach, a "late-fusion" masked autoencoder, (i) uses modality-specific tokenizers to account for different spatiotemporal characteristics of these sensors, and (ii) caches modality-specific tokens at inference time to process new measurements as they come. This architecture (i) is fast (6.68 ms and 112 ms on NVIDIA 5090 and Orin NX respectively, to compute the representation), (ii) performs better than existing image-only foundation models on tasks such as estimation of optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion (translation, rotation, and steering angle), and (iii) predicts robustly at nighttime or in situations where sensory data is degraded. See our project page for links to the dataset, code, and supplementary videos: https://abisulco.com/octosense/.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了OctoSense——一个开源传感器平台，配备立体RGB相机、事件相机、LiDAR、热成像相机、惯性测量单元、RTK校正的全球定位系统以及本体感知模块（来自汽车CAN总线数据和四足机器人关节角度）。同名的OctoSense数据集包含59小时的时间同步驾驶数据，覆盖一天中不同时段的多种环境类型，包括传感器严重退化的场景。我们利用此类真实世界机器人数据展示了多模态自监督学习，其中各传感器具有不同的表示形式、频率、延迟和噪声。本文提出的方法——一种"晚期融合"掩码自编码器——(i) 使用模态特定的分词器来建模这些传感器的不同时空特性，(ii) 在推理时缓存模态特定的token以处理新到达的测量数据。该架构(i) 计算速度快（在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别仅需6.68ms和112ms即可计算表征）；(ii) 在光流估计、深度估计、语义分割和自我运动估计（平移、旋转和转向角）等任务上优于现有的纯图像基础模型；(iii) 在夜间或传感数据退化的场景中依然能够进行鲁棒预测。更多信息（包括数据集、代码和补充视频的链接）请参见我们的项目页面：https://abisulco.com/octosense/。

</details>


### [85] [Not All Actions Are Equal: Rethinking Conditioning for Dexterous World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27325)
*Zizhao Yuan,Zhengtu Liang,Taowen Wang,Qiwei Liang,Yichi Wang,Yunheng Wang,Yuetong Fang,Lusong Li,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DexAC-WM，将动作条件建模从全局压缩改为结构化过程，通过动作分词、局部细化与全局调制对齐动作信号与视觉动态，并引入语义分支提供物体-场景先验，显著提升高自由度动作条件下的视频预测质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型将整个动作序列压缩为单一表示，在低自由度控制中表现良好，但在高自由度场景下因动作成分异质性（跨越多个数量级）导致优化失衡，难以建模细粒度效应，影响动作保真度。因此需要一种结构化的动作条件建模方法。

Method: 提出DexAC-WM，包含三个关键设计：(1) 动作分词（Action Tokenization），保留维度级语义；(2) 局部细化与全局调制（Local Refinement & Global Modulation），将动作信号与视觉动态对齐；(3) 语义分支（Semantic Branch），引入物体-场景先验，增强世界模型对动态视觉细节的捕捉能力，支持高自由度动作条件视频预测。

Result: 在EgoDex和EgoVerse数据集上，结合语义分支与DexAC显著提升了FID、FVD和PCK指标，表明在视觉-时间真实性和动作跟随一致性上均取得增益。同时，DexAC可扩展到其他骨干网络，验证了结构化动作条件设计的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将世界模型扩展到高自由度控制需要结构化的动作建模与语义基础的双重支撑。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in action-conditioned world models show promising progress in modeling complex interactions and forecasting future states under diverse action sequences. While these models are often driven by stronger visual representations and model capacity, action conditioning itself remains underexplored. Most existing approaches compress the entire action sequence into a single representation, which works well for low-DoF control but becomes less reliable in high-DoF scenarios. We observe that high-DoF dexterous actions are inherently heterogeneous, spanning multiple orders of magnitude, where large-scale motions coexist with subtle but important signals. When uniformly aggregated, optimization exhibits an imbalance across action components, which hinders the modeling of fine-grained effects and affects action fidelity. We therefore propose DexAC-WM, which treats action conditioning as a structured process rather than global compression. DexAC preserves dimension-level semantics via action tokenization and aligns action signals with visual dynamics through local refinement and global modulation. To address the limited high-level semantic grounding in existing world models, we further introduce a semantic branch that provides rich object-scene priors, which enables world model to capture dynamic visual details while supporting high-DoF action-conditioned video prediction. Experiments on EgoDex and EgoVerse show that combining the semantic branch with DexAC significantly improves FID, FVD, and PCK, demonstrating gains in visual-temporal realism and action-following consistency. We further verify that DexAC extends to other backbones, showing the scalability of our structured action-conditioning design. These results suggest that scaling world models to high-DoF control requires both structured action modeling and semantic grounding.

中文摘要翻译: 近期基于动作条件的世界模型在建模复杂交互和预测不同动作序列下的未来状态方面取得了显著进展。尽管这些模型通常受益于更强的视觉表示和模型容量，但动作条件本身仍未得到充分探索。现有方法大多将整个动作序列压缩为单一表示，这在低自由度控制中表现良好，但在高自由度场景下变得不可靠。我们观察到，高自由度灵巧动作本质上是异质的，跨越多个数量级，大规模运动与细微但重要的信号并存。当统一聚合时，优化过程在不同动作分量上呈现不平衡，阻碍了细粒度效应的建模并影响动作保真度。因此，我们提出DexAC-WM，将动作条件建模视为一个结构化过程而非全局压缩。DexAC通过动作分词保留维度级语义，并通过局部细化与全局调制将动作信号与视觉动态对齐。为解决现有世界模型缺乏高层语义基础的问题，我们进一步引入语义分支，提供丰富的物体-场景先验，使世界模型能够捕捉动态视觉细节，同时支持高自由度动作条件下的视频预测。在EgoDex和EgoVerse上的实验表明，将语义分支与DexAC相结合显著提升了FID、FVD和PCK指标，在视觉-时间真实性和动作跟随一致性上均有增益。我们进一步验证了DexAC可扩展至其他骨干网络，展示了结构化动作条件设计的可扩展性。这些结果表明，将世界模型扩展到高自由度控制需要结构化的动作建模与语义基础。

</details>


### [86] [RoPEMover: Depth-Aware Object Relocation via Positional Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27332)
*Ipek Oztas,Duygu Ceylan,Aybars Bugra Aksoy,Aysegul Dundar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散Transformer的几何感知物体运动方法，通过将2D RoPE扩展为深度感知的3D空间编码，实现对单张图像中物体的空间位移、遮挡处理、新区域生成和阴影光照一致性更新，在标准基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单张图像物体运动方法难以保持场景级一致性（如遮挡处理、新区域生成、阴影和反射一致性），缺乏几何感知的显式空间操作机制。

Method: 将扩散Transformer中的旋转位置编码（RoPE）从2D扩展为深度感知的3D空间编码，通过显式操控RoPE定义的结构化空间场来诱导受控运动，实现几何一致的物体位移和场景感知更新；使用合成数据+少量真实图像通过参数高效微调训练。

Result: 在标准物体运动基准上所有评估指标达到SOTA；大空间位移下保持物体身份；新暴露区域生成合理内容；阴影和光照等场景依赖效果一致更新。

Conclusion: 基于RoPE的深度感知空间编码能够有效实现单张图像的几何一致性物体运动，且仅需少量真实图像监督即可获得优异的场景级一致性。

Original Abstract: Moving an object in a single image requires geometry-consistent spatial rearrangement, including handling occlusions, revealing previously unseen regions, and maintaining coherent shadows and reflections. Existing approaches are not well suited to this setting and often fail to preserve such scene-level consistency. We address this problem by introducing a geometry-aware object motion method that operates directly on the positional representations of diffusion transformers. Our key insight is that rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) define a structured spatial field that can be explicitly manipulated to induce controlled motion. We extend 2D RoPE into a depth-aware formulation that encodes 3D spatial structure, enabling consistent object displacement and scene-aware updates. Our model is trained using synthetic data combined with a small set of real images via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Despite minimal real supervision, it preserves object identity under large spatial displacements, generates plausible content in newly revealed regions, and consistently updates scene-dependent effects such as shadows and illumination. Experimental results on standard object motion benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 在单张图像中移动物体需要几何一致的空间重排，包括处理遮挡、揭示先前未见的区域，以及保持连贯的阴影和反射。现有方法难以很好地适应这一设定，且常常无法保持这种场景级的一致性。我们通过引入一种几何感知的物体运动方法来应对这一问题，该方法直接作用于扩散Transformer的位置表示。我们的关键洞察在于，旋转位置编码（RoPE）定义了一个结构化的空间场，可以通过显式操控来诱导受控运动。我们将2D RoPE扩展为深度感知的公式，编码3D空间结构，从而实现一致的物体位移和场景感知更新。我们的模型使用合成数据结合少量真实图像，通过参数高效微调进行训练。尽管真实监督信号极少，该模型仍能在较大空间位移下保持物体身份，在新暴露区域生成合理的内容，并一致地更新阴影和照明等场景依赖效果。在标准物体运动基准上的实验结果表明，该方法在所有评估指标上均达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [87] [SAM2Matting: Generalized Image and Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27339)
*Ruiqi Shen,Guangquan Jie,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAM2Matting框架，将VOS追踪器增强为高保真视频抠图模型，仅用图像训练即在视频抠图任务上达到SOTA，并支持多样化提示和强时序一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频抠图方法依赖昂贵且领域狭窄的视频数据集，导致域外泛化能力差和追踪鲁棒性不足；高层跟踪（帧级理解）与低层抠图（细粒度细节）之间存在本质鸿沟。

Method: 将视频抠图解耦为两个子任务：1）利用基础VOS追踪器（如SAM2/SAM3）处理时序一致性；2）通过区域提议桥接模块和专用抠图头部解决细粒度细节。整体框架仅需图像训练即可泛化到视频场景。

Result: 仅用图像训练的SAM2Matting在视频抠图任务上取得了新的SOTA性能，支持多种提示类型，保持强时序一致性，并在以人为中心和野外场景下均展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦高层跟踪与低层抠图，将基础跟踪器增强为视频抠图模型是可行且高效的路径，打破了视频抠图对大规模视频数据集的依赖。

Original Abstract: Despite impressive advances in image matting, video matting remains challenging due to the inherent gap between high-level tracking, which requires frame-wise understanding, and low-level matting, which focuses on extremely fine-grained details. Existing methods attempt this with expensive and narrowly-scoped video matting datasets, which may limit out-of-domain generalization and compromise tracking robustness. We rethink the paradigm with SAM2Matting, a tracker-to-matting framework that advances VOS trackers to high-fidelity video matting. Specifically, it decouples the task by enhancing a foundational tracker (e.g., SAM2, SAM3) with a region-proposal bridge and dedicated matting heads, enabling the uncompromised tracker to handle temporal consistency while the matting components resolve fine-grained details. Notably, despite being trained only on images, SAM2Matting establishes new state-of-the-art performance on video matting, supports diverse prompt types, maintains strong temporal consistency, and demonstrates robust generalization across both human-centric and in-the-wild scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管图像抠图取得了令人瞩目的进展，但视频抠图仍然具有挑战性，其原因在于高层跟踪（需要帧级理解）与低层抠图（专注于极其精细的细节）之间存在固有鸿沟。现有方法依赖昂贵且领域狭窄的视频抠图数据集，这可能限制域外泛化能力并削弱跟踪鲁棒性。我们重新思考了这一范式，提出了SAM2Matting——一个从跟踪器到抠图器的框架，将VOS跟踪器提升为高保真视频抠图模型。具体而言，该框架通过解耦任务，用区域提议桥接模块和专用抠图头部增强基础跟踪器（如SAM2、SAM3），使未受损的跟踪器处理时序一致性，同时抠图组件解决细粒度细节。值得注意的是，尽管仅使用图像训练，SAM2Matting在视频抠图任务上建立了新的最先进性能，支持多样化的提示类型，保持强时序一致性，并在以人为中心和野外场景下均展现出稳健的泛化能力。

</details>


### [88] [RayPE: Ray-Space Positional Encoding for 3D-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27345)
*Minghao Yin,Jiahao Lu,Wenbo Hu,Wang Zhao,Shan Ying,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RayPE，一种向视频扩散Transformer中注入6D Plücker坐标的位置编码扩展，通过注意力分数分解为内容项与几何项来增强3D一致性，仅增加不到0.1%参数，并在多数据集混合训练中提升了相机可控性与视频质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频扩散Transformer仅通过(u,v,t)轴上的RoPE描述相机采样网格，忽略了场景的3D结构信息，导致跨帧3D一致性差、相机可控性不足。

Method: RayPE将每个token的6D Plücker坐标加性地注入自注意力的query和key中，采用query/key翻转排列使对称恒等配置恰好对应Plücker互易积；注意力分数因此分解为内容项、几何项及两个交叉项；为适应不同相机平移尺度，解耦射线方向与矩幅度，通过log-幅度的学习函数门控编码，并应用RMSNorm对齐内容分支。

Result: RayPE在四数据集混合训练中提升了相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性和整体视频质量，且仅增加不到0.1%的参数，零初始化后可从预训练权重直接启动。

Conclusion: 通过Plücker坐标注入几何先验是提升视频DiT模型3D感知能力的有效轻量级方案，注意力分数分解中的各项均被实验验证为不可或缺。

Original Abstract: Modern video diffusion transformers position their tokens through RoPE on the (u,v,t) axes -- a description of the camera's sampling grid that says nothing about the 3D structure of the scene. We observe that the geometric relation between two camera rays is captured by the Plucker reciprocal product, which is bilinear in the two rays -- the same algebraic form as the dot product in Transformer attention. Building on this analogy, we propose RayPE, a positional-encoding extension that injects per-token 6D Plucker coordinates additively into the queries and keys of self-attention, with a query/key flip arrangement under which the symmetric identity configuration coincides exactly with the reciprocal product. The injection is additive, the resulting attention score decomposes into a content term, a geometry term, and two content and geometry cross-terms -- all of which our experiments find individually necessary. To make the encoding stable across video data with heterogeneous camera-translation scales (SfM, deep SLAM, metric), we further decouple ray direction from moment magnitude, gate the encoding by a learned function of the log-magnitude, and apply RMSNorm to align it with the QKNorm-normalized content branch. The full module adds less than 0.1% parameters to a pretrained video DiT, is zero-initialized to start from the pretrained weights, and improves camera controllability, cross-frame 3D consistency, and overall video quality on a four-dataset training mixture.

中文摘要翻译: 现代视频扩散Transformer通过在(u,v,t)轴上使用RoPE来定位其token——这是一种描述相机采样网格的方式，但完全不涉及场景的3D结构。我们观察到，两条相机光线之间的几何关系由Plücker互易积捕获，该积在两条光线上是双线性的——与Transformer注意力中的点积具有相同的代数形式。基于这一类比，我们提出RayPE，一种位置编码扩展，它将每个token的6D Plücker坐标加性地注入自注意力的query和key中，并采用query/key翻转排列，使得对称恒等配置恰好与互易积一致。该注入是加性的，得到的注意力分数分解为内容项、几何项以及两个内容与几何交叉项——我们的实验发现所有这些项都是各自不可或缺的。为了使编码在具有异构相机平移尺度（SfM、深度SLAM、度量）的视频数据上保持稳定，我们进一步将射线方向与矩幅度解耦，通过log-幅度的学习函数门控编码，并应用RMSNorm将其与经QKNorm归一化的内容分支对齐。完整模块向预训练视频DiT添加的参数不到0.1%，采用零初始化以从预训练权重开始，并在四数据集训练混合上提升了相机可控性、跨帧3D一致性以及整体视频质量。

</details>


### [89] [PhysiFormer: Learning to Simulate Mechanics in World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27364)
*Yiming Chen,Yushi Lan,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysiFormer是一种基于扩散Transformer的3D物体运动预测模型，直接在3D世界坐标中对顶点轨迹进行去噪扩散，无需刚性或因果归纳偏置，即可生成物理上合理的刚性和弹性运动，并在轨迹精度、刚性保持和动量一致性上显著优于自回归基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在视角相关的像素空间操作，而神经物理方法常依赖于特定的潜在空间或显式强制刚性与因果性归纳偏置。作者希望探索是否可以在没有任何此类归纳偏置的情况下，直接在3D世界坐标中通过扩散过程获得高质量的物理运动预测。

Method: PhysiFormer是一个扩散Transformer，将3D物体表示为世界坐标系中的网格，给定初始顶点位置、速度及材料类型（刚性/弹性），通过去噪扩散过程直接在世界坐标中预测未来顶点轨迹。模型采用时间、空间和物体因子化的注意力机制，实现高效的多物体置换不变推理，无需显式物体编码。

Result: 在超过10万条模拟轨迹上训练后，PhysiFormer能够生成刚性和弹性力学运动，泛化到混合材料场景、未见过的真实世界几何形状以及更多物体数量。在轨迹精度、刚性保持和基于动量的物理一致性上，它显著优于自回归基线。

Conclusion: 坐标空间扩散是世界模型构建中一个有前景的方向，可实现视角不变、几何感知的世界建模，适用于机器人、图形学和物理设计。

Original Abstract: We present PhysiFormer, a diffusion transformer for physically-plausible 3D object motion. Unlike video world models that operate in view-dependent pixel space, PhysiFormer represents objects as 3D meshes expressed in world coordinates. Given the initial vertex positions and velocities, as well as object material type, rigid or elastic, the model samples future vertex trajectories. While related neural physics approaches build on ad-hoc latent spaces or explicitly enforce rigidity and causality, PhysiFormer shows that excellent results can be obtained without any such inductive biases, by casting vertex trajectory prediction as a single denoising diffusion process directly in world coordinates. The probabilistic formulation captures uncertainty in the learned dynamics, enabling diverse plausible futures from initial conditions, making this framework potentially useful for applications with unobserved uncertainty. The model features attention factorised over time, space, and objects for efficiency, enabling permutation-invariant multi-object reasoning without needing explicit object encoding. Trained on over 100k simulated trajectories, PhysiFormer generates rigid and elastic mechanics, and generalises to mixed-material settings, unseen real-world geometries, and larger object counts. It substantially outperforms autoregressive baselines in trajectory accuracy, rigidity preservation, and momentum-based physical consistency. Our results position coordinate-space diffusion as a promising step toward view-invariant, geometry-aware world modelling for robotics, graphics, and physical design. Visualisations, code, and models are available at https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了PhysiFormer，一种用于物理上合理的3D物体运动的扩散Transformer。不同于在视角相关的像素空间中操作的视频世界模型，PhysiFormer将物体表示为在世界坐标系中表达的3D网格。给定初始顶点位置和速度，以及物体材料类型（刚性或弹性），该模型采样未来的顶点轨迹。尽管相关的神经物理方法建立在特定的潜在空间上，或显式地强制刚性和因果关系，PhysiFormer展示了无需任何此类归纳偏置即可获得卓越结果，通过将顶点轨迹预测直接转化为世界坐标中的单一去噪扩散过程。概率公式捕捉了学习动力学中的不确定性，使得从初始条件下能够生成多种合理的未来，这使该框架对于存在未观测不确定性的应用具有潜在价值。该模型采用对时间、空间和物体进行因子化的注意力机制以提高效率，实现了无需显式物体编码的置换不变多物体推理。在超过10万条模拟轨迹上训练后，PhysiFormer能够生成刚性和弹性力学，并泛化到混合材料场景、未见过的真实世界几何形状以及更多物体数量。它在轨迹精度、刚性保持和基于动量的物理一致性上显著优于自回归基线。我们的结果将坐标空间扩散定位为朝着机器人、图形学和物理设计中视角不变、几何感知的世界模型迈出的有希望的一步。可视化、代码和模型可在https://yimingc9.github.io/physiformer获取。

</details>


### [90] [Don't Settle at the Mode! Mitigating Diversity Collapse in Pretrained Flow Models via Feature Self-Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27371)
