Method: 提出模型辅助采样框架，将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量；引入辅助梯度预测模型（预测每个样本的梯度），利用这些预测信息构建更高效的梯度估计器，均匀采样作为无辅助信息时的特例。该框架可轻松集成到现有优化器中而不改变其动态特性。

Result: 在合成数据和六个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在71%-86%的实验场景中取得性能提升，尤其适用于中等规模输入空间。使用AdamW等动量优化器时，提出的估计器在大约一半的训练周期内即可达到明显更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 模型辅助采样框架通过桥接机器学习优化与调查抽样理论，有效降低了随机梯度估计的方差，在提升优化效率的同时保持了与现有优化器的兼容性，为梯度噪声问题提供了新的理论视角和实用解决方案。

Original Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对机器学习优化中随机梯度估计的方差问题展开研究。深度学习依赖于随机梯度下降等小批量方法，这些方法近似全梯度但引入噪声，在收敛稳定性、速度和泛化性之间产生权衡。现有方法包括方差缩减技术（如SVRG和SAG）和自适应优化器，旨在缓解梯度噪声，但可能引入额外的计算开销。我们提出一个模型辅助采样框架，通过调查抽样理论来解释小批量梯度，将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量。我们的目标是结合机器学习优化与调查抽样理论在基于样本的估计和方差缩减方面的视角，搭建二者之间的桥梁。通过引入辅助梯度预测模型，我们构建了更高效的梯度估计器，其中均匀采样作为不使用辅助信息时的特例。我们的方法易于集成到现有优化器中，在不改变其动态特性的情况下提升效率。在合成数据和六个基准数据集上的实验结果显示，在71%-86%的实验场景中取得了性能提升，尤其适用于我们基准测试中的中等规模输入空间。值得注意的是，在使用AdamW等动量优化器时，所提出的估计器在大约一半的训练周期内即可实现明显优于基线估计器的泛化性能。

</details>


### [179] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecallRisk-BERT，一种基于文本与表格特征的多任务学习框架，用于FDA医疗器械召回记录的自动分类与严重性评估，在54,165条数据上显著优于基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究大多分别处理召回发生预测或根因分析，缺乏对召回严重性和根因类别的联合建模，无法有效支持召回后分类、严重性评估和根因解读。

Method: 基于54,165条FDA器械召回记录，先评估经典机器学习和提升模型，再开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，融合PubMedBERT文本表示与产品代码、法规编号、医学专科等结构化特征的嵌入表示，同时预测召回严重性（I/II/III类）和9类根因类别。

Result: 单任务严重性预测中，LightGBM文本-表格配置达最优（准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974）。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著超越单任务PubMedBERT基线；模型风险排序与真实根因严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的召回后分类、监管决策辅助以及基于模型的根因风险分析。

Original Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 医疗器械召回是保护患者安全的关键监管机制。FDA召回记录数量的不断增长给报告后召回分类、严重性评估和根因解读带来了挑战。现有研究大多分别处理召回发生预测或根因分析，而针对召回严重性和根因类别的联合建模受到的关注有限。我们利用2002年至2025年10月期间openFDA中54,165条FDA医疗器械召回记录，开发了一个自动化召回分类框架。我们首先评估了经典机器学习和基于提升的模型在召回严重性和根因类别预测上的表现。随后，我们开发了RecallRisk-BERT，这是一个多任务模型，将基于PubMedBERT的召回叙述文本表示与结构化分类特征（包括产品代码、法规编号和医学专科）的嵌入表示相结合，同时预测召回严重性（I/II/III类）和合并后的根因类别（9类）。性能评估采用准确率、宏平均精确率、召回率、F1分数和ROC-AUC。在单任务严重性预测中，基于LightGBM的文本-表格配置取得了最优性能，准确率为0.963，宏F1为0.856，ROC-AUC为0.974。在多任务设置下，RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务的PubMedBERT基线。模型推导的风险排序与观测到的根因严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。这些结果表明，文本-表格联合学习可以支持可扩展的报告后召回分类、监管决策辅助以及基于模型的根因风险分析。

</details>


### [180] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM）的偏好反馈学习势能函数，实现基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS），在保证最优策略不变的前提下提升稀疏奖励强化学习的样本效率，并避免奖励作弊问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 稀疏奖励环境中缺乏中间反馈指导探索，而简单的奖励塑形容易引发奖励作弊问题。基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS）虽能保证最优策略集不变，但需要专家设计势能函数。本文旨在利用VLM自动学习势能函数，消除对手工设计的依赖。

Method: 使用轻量级VLM对图像对进行偏好比较，基于这些偏好标签训练一个势能函数模型，将该势能函数用于PBRS框架中进行奖励塑形，从而在保持最优策略不变的前提下加速学习。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中验证了方法的有效性，实验表明即使小VLM生成的偏好标签精度有限，仍能显著提升样本效率，并揭示了VLM偏好标签精度与样本效率改进之间的关联。

Conclusion: VLM-PBRS首次将VLM偏好学习用于合成PBRS势能函数，提供了一种低成本的奖励塑形方案，能有效提升样本效率并增强对奖励作弊的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏奖励对强化学习智能体而言本质上是具有挑战性的，因为其缺乏中间反馈来引导探索，也无法将稀疏的成功奖励正确归因于轨迹中的相关部分。简单的奖励塑形可能引发奖励作弊问题，产生利用辅助信号而非解决预期任务的策略。基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS）能够保证最优策略集不变，但需要在整个状态空间上定义启发式势能函数。本文提出了VLM引导的PBRS框架VLM-PBRS，直接从视觉语言模型（VLM）反馈中学习势能函数。我们通过查询轻量级VLM获取图像对之间的偏好，并利用这些偏好训练势能函数模型。由于该方法基于势能奖励塑形，因此保留了原始最优策略，并消除了专家设计奖励塑形项的需求。由于大型VLM在策略学习过程中反复调用的成本过高，我们采用了更小、计算效率更高的VLM。尽管由此产生的偏好标签精度较低，但实验证据表明，这些偏好标签仍可用于加速学习。我们在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中进行了实证验证，并强调了VLM偏好标签精度与样本效率改进之间的联系。本文的贡献有三方面：（1）首次将VLM偏好学习应用于为PBRS合成势能函数；（2）提出了一种利用小型VLM的、有原则的低成本解决方案；（3）通过大量实证证明了改进的样本效率和对奖励作弊的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [181] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化相关度量（NRM）框架的全信息流归因方法，用于解释事件时序图神经网络（ETGNNs），弥补了现有方法忽略事件诱导变量信息流的缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的ETGNN解释方法仅追踪从事件相关嵌入到输出的部分信息流，忽视了通过事件诱导变量（event-induced variables）的重要路径，而这些变量在捕捉长程时间依赖中起核心作用。

Method: 基于NRM框架，提出全信息流归因方法，显式量化从事件嵌入出发以及经过事件诱导变量的信息流；通过模块化解构程序处理ETGNNs的架构复杂性，系统构建复杂神经架构的相关性结构。

Result: 在疫情追踪、社会动态两个合成数据集以及一个真实世界政治事件网络数据集上，定性和定量实验均表明该方法在解释性能和人类可解释性上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过考虑所有事件关联变量的完整信息流，所提方法克服了ETGNN可解释性不足的局限，能产生更符合人类直观理解的解释。

Original Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

中文摘要翻译: 事件时序图神经网络（ETGNNs）在社交网络分析、流行病追踪、推荐系统和政治事件预测等广泛应用中展现了强大的性能。然而，其日益增长的复杂性给可解释性带来了重大挑战。现有的解释方法仅关注ETGNNs中信息流的子集，通常仅追踪从事件相关嵌入到输出的贡献，因此忽略了通过事件诱导变量的重要路径——这些变量调节节点间的交互，从而在捕捉长程时间依赖中发挥核心作用。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种新颖的归因方法，分析通过所有事件关联变量的完整信息流。我们的方法建立在最近的归一化相关度量（NRM）框架之上，能够显式量化源自事件嵌入的信息流以及经过事件诱导变量的信息流。它还能确保各层潜在变量的可比性，并支持事件间交互的高阶分析。为处理ETGNNs的架构复杂性，我们扩展了NRM框架，引入了一种模块化解构程序，有助于为复杂神经架构系统化构建相关性结构。我们在两个用于疫情追踪和社会动态的合成数据集以及一个真实世界的政治事件网络数据集上评估了我们的方法。定性和定量实验表明，我们的方法在产生更符合人类直观解释的同时，持续优于现有的解释方法。

</details>


### [182] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对图神经网络（GNN）进行了全面综述，围绕统一的设计空间组织该领域，从共享第一原理推导谱域与空间域方法，将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次相联系，并横跨12个应用领域分析了图结构何时值得其计算代价、何时不值得，揭示了跨领域的共性模式与实际部署中的关键约束。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 图神经网络已从小众表示学习技术发展为处理关系数据的默认模型，但关键问题不再是消息传递是否有帮助，而是图结构何时值得其计算开销、何时不值得。本文旨在系统梳理该领域，为从业者提供关于架构选择、计算代价和实际部署约束的清晰指南。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法：首先构建统一的设计空间，从共享第一原理推导谱域与空间域GNN公式；然后将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次相连接，明确指出现有架构能区分和不能区分的图结构；接着对12个应用领域逐一分析图构建选择及其成本、主导架构家族及其原因，并从弱基线或有利数据划分中分离出报告的性能提升；最后进行跨领域比较，识别重复出现的模式。

Result: 跨领域比较揭示了若干重复模式：(1) 异质性和图规模几乎在所有领域削弱了相同模型的性能；(2) 时序图始终比静态图更难处理；(3) 在公开排行榜上名列前茅的架构很少是最终部署的架构。此外，过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性等约束才是决定模型是否被实际采用的关键因素。

Conclusion: 本文通过对GNN设计空间、表达能力及12个应用领域的系统综述，得出核心结论：图结构并非总是有益的，其计算代价需要根据具体领域和任务权衡；跨领域存在共性挑战（异质性、规模、时序性）；学术排行榜与工业部署之间存在显著差距；过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性等约束不是可选的检查清单，而是决定GNN能否落地的根本性约束。

Original Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络已从小众的表示学习技术发展为处理关系性结构数据的默认模型类别。当前有趣的问题不再是消息传递是否在给定数据集上有帮助，而是图结构在何处值得其计算成本、在何处不值得。本综述围绕一个统一的设计空间组织该领域，从共享第一原理推导谱域与空间域公式，并将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次相联系，明确指出现有架构能够和不能区分的图结构。以此方法论框架为基础，我们审视了十二个应用领域，包括推荐系统与社交网络、知识图谱与语言模型集成、药物发现与分子性质学习、医疗健康与神经科学、计算机视觉、交通与城市计算、电力与可再生能源系统、无线与第六代网络、欺诈检测与网络安全、工业预测维护、材料科学以及气候建模。针对每个领域，我们明确了图结构构建的选择及其代价，识别了主导的架构家族及其原因，并从弱基线或有利的数据划分造成的假象中分离出报告的性能提升。跨领域比较揭示了重复出现的模式：异质性与图规模几乎在所有领域削弱了相同的模型；时序图始终比静态图更难处理；在公开排行榜上名列前茅的架构很少是最终被部署的架构。我们将过平滑、过挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性视为决定模型是否被采用的根本性约束，而非一份可勾选的检查清单。

</details>


### [183] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FisherSketch，一种免训练的源模型选择方法，通过估计头Fisher对齐的余弦值来度量共享词汇表的大型语言模型家族中的任务相似性，解决了激活相似性在共享输出头设置下不可识别的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列等科学字符串领域中，共享分词器但预测目标不同的语料库之间进行源模型选择时，激活相似性指标（如CKA）在共享输出头设置下对迁移学习无效（模型表示相同但头更新正交），而经典的更新几何度量在词汇表规模下计算代价过高。

Method: FisherSketch方法利用核均值嵌入在联合激活-误差空间中的余弦等价于头Fisher对齐这一关键恒等式，通过单次流式传递直接估计该余弦值，避免了显式计算Fisher矩阵，仅需16 KB任务签名（m=4096）和192 KB每任务流式状态。

Result: K=128,256的头Fisher对齐变得可行；共享参数和内部层验证以及Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer偏移实验表明，当激活相似性无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍能提供有效信息。

Conclusion: FisherSketch提供了一种轻量级、免训练的源模型选择方法，同时其任务签名和边际分布可作为诊断工具，研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动。

Original Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 针对共享词汇表的LLM家族进行免训练源模型选择的问题出现在SMILES、蛋白质和基因组序列等科学字符串领域中，这些候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同。这产生了一种“激活-黑暗”机制：在没有关于标签条件误差几何的假设下，表示相似性度量可能缺乏信息，而经典的更新几何度量在词汇表规模下计算上不可行。我们证明，在共享输出头设置中，表示度量（如CKA）对于迁移是不可识别的；模型可以共享相同的表示却产生正交的头更新。关键恒等式是：头Fisher对齐恰好是联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦值，从而暴露了激活、误差和耦合因子，而无需显式构造Fisher矩阵。FisherSketch通过单次流式传递直接估计该余弦值，使得K=128,256的头Fisher对齐变得可行，仅需16 KB的任务签名（m=4096）和192 KB的每任务流式状态，小到足以存储在模型哈希值旁边，但编码了迁移相关的更新结构。除源模型选择外，相同的签名和边际分布提供了一种诊断工具，用于研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动；共享参数和内部层验证，以及Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer偏移实验表明，当激活相似性无法区分任务时，FisherSketch仍能提供有效信息。

</details>


### [184] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，将先前的分析从无条件、对角潜在协方差扩展到类别相关、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。证明了微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由有效协方差驱动的常微分方程，揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可以提升弱方向的可学习性，而过强的相关则会破坏恢复。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 先前的可解GAN分析仅假设无条件信号和对角潜在协方差，忽略了真实场景中数据存在的类别依赖、相关性和非零均值等结构化特征。本文旨在扩展理论框架以处理更真实的潜在结构。

Method: 构建一个线性生成器学习低维子空间的高维可解模型；采用二次能量判别器；将异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩纳入动力学；在高维极限下证明随机微观训练过程收敛到确定性的常微分方程。

Result: 在匹配协方差特例下，稳定性分析给出了由学习率和噪声水平决定的逐模式可解区间：当主导有效特征值越过下阈值时学习开始，所有相关有效模式保持在区间内时才能完全恢复。低秩相关可提升弱方向的可学习性，而过强相关则破坏恢复。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制。在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，有信息的生成器协方差能改善与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。

Conclusion: 本文提出的理论框架成功将GAN分析扩展到结构化潜在协方差，揭示了信号增强机制，并为设计更好的生成器提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。先前的可解GAN分析假设无条件信号和对角潜在协方差；我们将多特征判别器设置扩展到类别依赖、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。对于二次能量判别器，所有此类异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学。我们证明了随机微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由该有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程。在匹配协方差特例下，稳定性分析给出了由学习率和噪声水平决定的逐模式可解区间：当主导有效特征值越过下阈值时学习开始，而完全恢复要求所有相关有效模式保持在区间内。这揭示了一种信号增强机制：低秩相关可以提升弱方向的可学习性，而过强的相关则会破坏恢复。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制。在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验进一步表明，有信息的生成器协方差能改善与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。

</details>


### [185] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含由精神科医师标注的1,799条Reddit长距离关系帖子，用于多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务。实验比较了7个微调Transformer模型和5个大语言模型，发现Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类上最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最佳（Macro-F1=0.519），并揭示了焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间的强关联。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康建模通常将心理疾病视为孤立的个体现象，忽略了重要的人际关系背景（如长距离关系）。作者希望引入关系上下文，捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的人际关系触发因素，推动从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变。

Method: 1. 构建RSPC语料库：从Reddit收集1,799条长距离关系相关帖子，由精神科医师标注诊断类别（焦虑、抑郁）、关系压力触发因素和关系阶段。2. 基准测试：在三个任务（多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测、时间阶段预测）上评估7种微调Transformer模型（如BERT、RoBERTa等）和5种大语言模型（如Claude-3-Haiku、GPT-4o等）。

Result: 1. 不同模型家族在不同任务上表现出明显差异：Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上最佳（Macro-F1=0.519），表明不同模型具有不同的能力特征。2. 发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系上下文的NLP任务建立了基准，支持心理健康建模从个体中心向情境感知的转变，能够捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态特征。

Original Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

中文摘要翻译: 在自然语言处理中，心理健康状况通常被建模为孤立现象，缺乏人际背景。我们利用关于长距离关系的Reddit帖子来捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的关系触发因素。我们引入了关系压力与精神病学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医师标注的Reddit帖子，标注内容包括诊断类别（涵盖最普遍的情绪障碍：焦虑和抑郁）、关系压力触发因素以及关系阶段指示。我们在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务上，对七种微调Transformer模型和五种大语言模型进行了基准测试。我们发现模型家族之间存在明显的任务依赖性差异，其中Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上表现最强（Macro-F1=0.519），这揭示了不同模型的不同能力。我们进一步发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。总体而言，RSPC为考虑关系背景的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变，从而捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态特征。

</details>


### [186] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体上博彩广告中存在的操纵性和欺骗性问题，构建了一个来自Instagram和Reddit的标注数据集，包含分类标签和人工解释，并分析了常见策略及其对用户心理健康的潜在影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 近年来社交媒体上的博彩广告激增，其中许多使用欺骗性宣传技巧，可能误导用户、鼓励风险行为并影响心理健康，但缺乏公开标注数据集来支持自动化检测研究。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交平台收集博彩广告数据，进行人工标注（包含操纵性/欺骗性分类标签以及标注理由的解释文本），并分析常见说服策略及其对心理健康的影响。

Result: 构建了首个公开的博彩广告操纵性/欺骗性标注数据集，包含分类标签与人工解释；分析了博彩广告中常用的说服策略及其对用户心理健康的潜在影响。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于开发浏览器插件以警示用户识别操纵性博彩广告，以及帮助监管机构通过自动化网络爬虫检测此类在线促销内容。

Original Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，社交媒体平台上博彩应用的推广显著增加。许多这类广告使用可能误导用户、鼓励风险行为并潜在影响用户心理健康的说服技巧。然而，由于缺乏公开可用的标注数据集，关于操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告自动化检测的研究仍然有限。在本工作中，我们引入了一个新的数据集，包含从两个广泛使用的社交媒体平台——Instagram和Reddit——收集的博彩相关广告。这些广告经过人工标注，标明了操纵性和欺骗性广告行为。除分类标签外，该数据集还包含人工提供的解释文本，描述了每条标注背后的推理过程，从而支持可解释的操纵性广告检测方法研究。此外，我们分析了博彩广告中常用的策略，并考察了这些说服技巧如何影响用户的心理健康。所提出的框架还可支持实际应用，例如向用户警示操纵性博彩广告的浏览器插件，以及帮助监管机构自动监控和检测此类在线促销内容的网络爬虫。

</details>


### [187] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中“更大模型带来更高精度”的主流观点，通过Ridge回归作为测试平台，系统地搜索预处理超参数（上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强），发现最优超参数设置能显著提升性能，使简单线性模型在多数基准上超越Transformer、MLP等复杂模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究趋向于使用越来越大的架构（如专用Transformer、通用基础模型），假设模型容量是提升精度的关键。本文持相反立场，认为通过调整预处理而非扩大规模，可以在更低成本下缩小大部分性能差距。

Method: 使用Ridge回归作为测试平台（因其具有封闭解和可解释权重），在八个标准基准数据集上系统搜索四个预处理超参数：上下文长度（lookback）、局部归一化（local normalization）、正则化强度和数据增强策略。通过直接读取搜索过程中的最优超参数来分析其规律。

Result: 发现三个主要模式：(1)最优回看长度强烈依赖于序列本身，且与预测水平呈非单调关系，拟合幂律指数从+0.46到-0.19不等，挑战了"更长预测需要更长历史"的传统观点；(2)在上下文的滑动尾部做归一化（而非整体归一化）几乎普遍更优；(3)同一数据集内不同序列对超参数偏好不同，最优跨序列共享程度差异很大。优化后的模型在大多数数据集-预测水平组合上优于此前线性预测器，并在八个基准中的六个上超过Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 通过精心调优预处理超参数，简单线性模型也能达到甚至超越复杂深度模型的性能。优化后的超参数还可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大型模型默默学习到的数据结构。

Original Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测研究一直朝着更大架构的方向稳步发展，从专用Transformer到通用基础模型，其假设是模型容量是提升精度的关键。我们持相反的立场：通过调整预处理而非扩大模型规模，可以在远更低的成本下缩小大部分性能差距。我们以Ridge回归作为测试平台，因为它具有封闭解和可解释的权重，使得最优超参数可以直接从搜索中读取。我们在八个标准基准上系统搜索了上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强，并发现了三个模式。(1)最优回看长度强烈依赖于序列本身，且通常与预测水平呈非单调关系，拟合的幂律指数从ETTm2上的+0.46到Exchange和Traffic上的-0.19不等，挑战了"更长预测水平需要更长历史"的传统观念。(2)在上下文的经过学习的滑动尾部进行归一化（而非对整个上下文归一化）几乎普遍更受青睐。(3)同一数据集内的不同序列在超参数上往往存在分歧；最优跨序列共享程度从完全共享到完全独立不等。由此产生的模型在大多数数据集-预测水平组合上超过了先前的线性预测器，并在八个基准中的六个上超越了Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。优化后的超参数还可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示出大型模型默默吸收到其学习参数中的数据结构。

</details>


### [188] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从多解观测中唯一且稳定地识别真实ODE的理论条件空白，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为微分方程的自然度量，建立了可辨识性界，并分析了样本复杂度界，量化了需要多少解观测才能可靠恢复控制方程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献缺乏从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地识别真实ODE的理论条件，也没有对该学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。为解决这一理论空白，本文开展了系统研究。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，该度量捕捉所有允许初始条件下两个方程的最坏情况分离，从而编码了识别问题的极小化极大结构。基于此度量，推导相关ODE类的度量熵估计并分析样本复杂度界。

Result: 建立了从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz（Hölder）连续向量场的非线性类等广泛结构方程的可辨识性界，精确刻画了两个不同方程何时可以从解数据中区分；给出了样本复杂度定量界。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离为比较微分方程提供了理论基础，所建立的可辨识性界和样本复杂度定量结果为科学机器学习中从数据发现控制方程提供了理论保障。

Original Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

中文摘要翻译: 从观测到的解数据中学习控制方程是科学机器学习中的一个基本挑战，然而，从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地识别真实常微分方程的理论条件仍未得到充分发展，且文献中缺乏对此类学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。为填补这一空白，我们引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，因为它捕捉了所有允许初始条件下两个方程之间的最坏情况分离，从而编码了识别问题的极小化极大结构。我们针对广泛的结构方程类别——从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz（Hölder）连续向量场的非线性类——建立了控制ODE的可辨识性界，精确刻画了两个不同方程何时可以从解数据中区分。利用该度量，我们推导了相关ODE类的度量熵估计并分析了样本复杂度界，量化了需要多少解观测才能可靠地恢复控制方程。

</details>


### [189] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的RLAIF框架用于生成可迁移的工作搜索查询，并系统研究了对抗性奖励表面下不同优化器的行为。实验发现，在critic-free优化器中，奖励塑形比算法选择更重要；GRPO因组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号敏感而易于被利用；引入基于规则的确定性奖励下限可有效缓解奖励黑客行为，提升模型质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有求职平台依赖低带宽查询接口，难以捕捉候选人简历的高维度复杂性。本文旨在生成可迁移的搜索查询（抽象掉求职者特定标识但保留可泛化资格条件），并解决该任务中因高度对抗性的奖励表面导致策略优化常利用LLM评判标准缺陷而产生的退化复制行为问题。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架生成可迁移搜索查询；系统对比不同优化器（RLOO、REINFORCE++、GRPO）在对抗性奖励表面下的表现；引入确定性基于规则的奖励下限来修正针对逐字复制行为的奖励赋值。

Result: (1) 对于critic-free优化器，性能主要由稳健的奖励塑形决定，具体算法选择影响不大；(2) RLOO和REINFORCE++天然抵抗奖励黑客行为，而GRPO的组相对优势归一化机制对虚假奖励信号敏感，易被利用；(3) 引入规则奖励下限后，交叉家族评估法官上的质量提升达+0.147；(4) 训练时的奖励模型将性能提升膨胀了2.4倍，确认训练成功本质上依赖于奖励塑形纪律而非优化器选择。

Conclusion: 训练成功根本上取决于强制实施奖励塑形纪律，而非选择不同的优化器。通过引入规则基础的奖励下限可以缓解GRPO的奖励黑客问题。

Original Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

中文摘要翻译: 求职平台依赖低带宽查询接口，这些接口通常无法捕捉候选人简历的高维度复杂性。我们提出了一种端到端的RLAIF（基于AI反馈的强化学习）框架，用于生成可迁移的工作搜索查询——即抽象掉求职者特定标识但保留可泛化资格条件的术语。该任务引入了一个高度对抗性的奖励表面，其中策略优化频繁利用LLM作为评判者的评分标准缺陷，导致退化的逐字复制行为。我们进行了全面的实证实验，以隔离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响。结果表明，对于无评论家（critic-free）的优化器，性能主要由稳健的奖励塑形决定，具体算法选择基本无关紧要。虽然基于每轮基线方法的无评论家优化器（RLOO和REINFORCE++）天然抵抗奖励黑客行为，但GRPO中的组相对优势归一化机制对虚假奖励信号异常敏感，使其极易被利用。我们证明，引入确定性的、基于规则的奖励下限来修正逐字复制行为的奖励赋值，可以缓解这一失效模式，从而使跨家族评估法官的质量提升达+0.147。最终，我们证明训练时的奖励模型将性能提升膨胀了2.4倍，确认了训练成功从根本上依赖于强制执行奖励塑形纪律，而非选择不同的优化器。

</details>


### [190] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习和少量实验数据，实现了板状结构中损伤的高精度定位与尺寸估计，R²分别超过0.93和0.99。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型的实际部署面临两个主要障碍：标注实验数据有限，以及生成大规模高保真仿真数据集的计算成本过高。

Method: 采用一维时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据集进行预训练，结合卷积自编码器（CAE）进行深度特征学习，再通过迁移学习使用少量标注实验数据对模型进行微调，最终利用前馈神经网络实现损伤定位与尺寸估计。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于基于CNN的对应方法；模型对损伤定位的R²超过0.93，对损伤尺寸估计的R²超过0.99；在未见过的数据上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架被证明是一种准确、计算高效且实际可行的导波结构健康监测解决方案。

Original Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

中文摘要翻译: 基于导波的结构健康监测（GWSHM）结合机载传感器，为工程结构损伤的早期诊断提供了重要潜力。然而，深度学习模型的实际部署常常受到标注实验数据有限以及生成大规模高保真仿真数据集计算成本高昂的制约。本研究提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，该框架集成了轻量级物理仿真、基于卷积自编码器（CAE）的深度特征学习、前馈神经网络以及有限的实验测量数据，用于对压电传感器仪器化的板状结构进行精确的损伤定位与尺寸估计。采用计算高效的一维时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据集用于预训练，而迁移学习则利用少量标注数据将模型自适应到实验域。基于CAE的迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于基于CNN的对应方法。该模型表现优异，损伤定位的R²分数超过0.93，损伤尺寸估计的R²分数超过0.99。其泛化能力在未见过的数据上得到了验证，对预训练或微调阶段未出现的损伤场景也展现出高预测精度。实验结果证明了所提出的框架是一种准确、计算高效且实际可行的解决方案，适用于真实的导波结构健康监测应用。

</details>


### [191] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明梯度均衡（GEQ）框架在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可逼近性，进而等价于遗憾最小化和校准等经典在线学习框架，并建立了它们之间的高效归约关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GEQ是一个新提出的在线优化框架，与遗憾最小化等经典框架存在相似性，但先前研究表明GEQ误差与遗憾值是不可比较的目标，缺乏对GEQ在在线学习全景中准确定位的理解。

Method: 通过算法归约证明GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性之间的双向等价关系，并利用已知的可逼近性、遗憾最小化和校准之间的等价关系，推导出GEQ与这些框架的等价性。

Result: 建立了GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性之间的算法等价关系，进而证明了GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价；给出了高效归约方法，可将乐观性、强自适应性等精细化保证从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ；同时识别了GEQ的充要条件，并建立了有约束和无约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

Conclusion: GEQ在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化和校准等经典在线学习框架，为GEQ在在线学习全景中的定位提供了精确的理论基础。

Original Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

中文摘要翻译: 梯度均衡（GEQ）是最近提出的一种在线优化框架，它将一阶平稳性从离线优化推广到在线场景，并抽象了在线共形预测等问题。尽管GEQ与已知的在线学习框架（如遗憾最小化）存在奇特的相似性，但先前的研究表明，GEQ误差和遗憾值是不可比较的目标，因此对GEQ如何融入更广泛的在线学习图景仍缺乏精确理解。在这项工作中，我们证明GEQ在算法意义上等价于Blackwell可逼近性。也就是说，任何Blackwell可逼近性问题都可以通过查询黑箱GEQ预言机来求解，且渐近上不损失预言机的误差率，反之亦然。结合已知的可逼近性、遗憾最小化和校准之间的等价关系，这些结果意味着GEQ也与这些框架等价。我们的归约是高效的，可用于将乐观性和强自适应性等精细化保证从遗憾最小化迁移到GEQ。在此过程中，我们还识别了GEQ的充分必要条件，并建立了有约束和无约束决策集下不同GEQ概念之间的归约。

</details>


### [192] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Top-k稀疏自编码器（SAE）因固定预算k和过拟合等问题，提出两种兼容的稀疏正则化方法（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚），实验证明软稀疏正则化与硬架构稀疏性具有互补性，能提升单义性且不牺牲重建质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Top-k SAE虽然通过架构设计避免了传统ℓ1惩罚的缺点，但其固定预算k无法适应输入复杂度变化，且容易过拟合到训练时的k值。现有工作未将显式稀疏正则化与Top-k架构结合，因此作者探索软正则化与硬架构稀疏性的互补可能性。

Method: 在Top-k选择操作之前，对激活值施加两种稀疏正则化：(1) 对未被Top-k选中的单元（off-support）施加ℓ1惩罚；(2) 施加尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚，使编码集中在更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅作用于批次活跃单元（batch-active units）。

Result: 在两个数据集、三种视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化器一致提升了单义性且不牺牲重建质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少的隐变量上，使重建对推理时k值选择更鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探针的性能。

Conclusion: 硬架构稀疏性（Top-k选择）与软稀疏正则化并非互斥，而是互补的，二者结合可以更有效地提升特征的单义性和模型鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为解释视觉基础模型表示的主流工具，将多语义激活分解为更大规模的稀疏、更具单义性的特征集合。Top-k SAE作为当前标准变体，通过其激活函数在架构层面强制稀疏性，仅保留每个输入中最活跃的k个隐变量。由于该设计正是为了避免早期SAE使用的ℓ1惩罚及其已知缺陷，因此尽管Top-k SAE本身仍有局限（如预算k固定不变、无法适应输入复杂度、易过拟合到训练时的k值），但一直未与显式稀疏正则化器结合。我们提出了两种与Top-k架构兼容的稀疏正则化器，均在Top-k选择前的激活值上起作用：一种是对未被选中单元（off-support）施加ℓ1惩罚；另一种是尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚，将编码集中在更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅作用于批次活跃单元（即在批次内至少被Top-k算子选中一次的单元）。在两个数据集、三种视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化器均在不牺牲重建质量的前提下一致提升单义性。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少的隐变量上，使重建对推理时k值选择更加鲁棒，并提升了小预算线性探针的性能。我们的核心发现是：硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化并非互斥，而是互补的。

</details>


### [193] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型中的幻觉本质是数据覆盖问题，通过构建MMBench2数据集、识别三种幻觉模式并开发对应预测信号，以及提出覆盖感知采样和好奇心奖励微调方案，仅用50条真实轨迹即可适应新环境。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的未来画面，但经常产生幻觉（视觉流畅但偏离真实动态），而幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，轻量级数据中心信号既能检测也能缓解这一问题。

Method: 1. 构建了MMBench2数据集（427小时、210个任务）并训练350M参数世界模型；2. 识别三种幻觉模式（感知幻觉、动作边缘化幻觉、场景偏离幻觉）并开发三种预测信号；3. 提出覆盖感知采样技术（训练时）和基于好奇心奖励的数据收集微调方案（在线），仅需50条真实轨迹即可适应新环境。

Result: 1. 识别出三种幻觉模式并开发了准确预测模型失败位置的信号；2. 覆盖感知采样技术可缩小训练时的覆盖缺口；3. 基于好奇心奖励的微调方案仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可使预训练世界模型适应完全未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是一个数据覆盖问题，检测幻觉的信号同样可以用于缓解幻觉。

Original Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

中文摘要翻译: 现代生成式世界模型能够生成越来越逼真的、动作可控的未来画面，但它们经常出现幻觉：生成的轨迹虽然在视觉上流畅，却偏离了真实的动态过程。我们假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，而轻量级的数据中心信号既能检测幻觉，也能指导缓解措施。为验证这一点，我们引入了MMBench2——一个包含427小时、210个任务的视觉世界建模数据集，配有真实动作、奖励和实时模拟器，并在其上训练了一个350M参数的世界模型。我们识别出三种不同的幻觉模式：感知幻觉、动作边缘化幻觉和场景偏离幻觉——每种模式对应于流程的不同阶段，并开发了三种能够准确预测模型失败位置的信号。为在训练时缩小覆盖缺口，我们开发了一种覆盖感知的采样技术；为在线缩小缺口，我们的幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励，用于有针对性的数据收集，从而形成一种数据高效的微调方案，使预训练的世界模型能够仅用50条真实环境轨迹就适应完全未见过的环境。总体而言，我们的发现表明，世界模型中的幻觉本质上是一个数据覆盖问题，而用于检测幻觉的信号同样可以用于缓解幻觉。

</details>


### [194] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入而非优化目标，使网络能读取自身误差空间结构并学习迭代校正策略。在四个PDE族中，ENS在大部分设置下取得最高预测精度，在湍流柯尔莫哥洛夫流上提升达10倍，且能零样本泛化到分布外参数变化和跨方程迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的神经替代模型将PDE求解视为纯统计任务，一旦训练完成便难以修正自身约束违反和外推误差；而混合方法虽通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步优化PDE残差来促进物理正确性，但在病态系统中残差最小化并非重建精度的可靠代理，且继承了经典优化器的高计算成本和不稳定性。本文旨在克服这些局限性。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），核心创新是将PDE残差场作为网络的直接输入（而非优化目标），使网络在每个迭代步中读取自身误差的空间结构，学习一个更新策略来迭代地校正预测。该方法避免了混合方法中昂贵的计算成本。

Result: 在四个PDE族上，ENS在大部分设置下取得最高预测精度，在湍流柯尔莫哥洛夫流上提升达10倍。ENS的学习校正策略能泛化到分布偏移场景，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移，且在病态区域（残差最小化最不可靠时）相对优势最大。

Conclusion: 理论和实验表明，数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中作为重建精度的代理并不可靠。ENS通过将残差作为输入而非优化目标，实现了更高的预测精度、更低的计算成本以及更强的分布外泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 神经替代模型提供了从偏微分方程参数到解的快速近似映射，但它们通常将求解视为纯统计任务：一旦训练完成，便难以纠正自身的约束违反行为，也无法外推到训练分布之外。最近的混合方法通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步优化PDE残差来促进物理正确性，但继承了底层经典优化器的计算成本和不稳定性。我们在理论和实验上证明，在病态系统中，数值最小化PDE残差作为重建精度的代理并不可靠，这解释了为何这些方法尽管达到低残差却常常无法做出准确预测。我们提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），它基于一个不同的原理：PDE残差场被作为网络的直接输入而非优化目标，使网络在每个迭代步中读取自身误差的空间结构，并学习一个更新策略来迭代地校正其预测。在四个PDE族上，ENS在大部分设置中取得最高预测精度，在湍流柯尔莫哥洛夫流上提升达10倍，同时避免了混合方法昂贵的计算成本。ENS的学习校正策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移，其相对优势在残差最小化最不可靠的病态区域中最为显著。项目网站：https://neuralsolver.github.io/。

</details>


### [195] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过自回归建模替代归一化流，突破拓扑约束并提升可扩展性，在分子平衡态采样中显著优于流模型，并预训练了1.32亿参数的Robin模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器依赖归一化流（NF），而NF存在严格可逆性约束导致的表达性有限（离散时间）或似然计算昂贵（连续时间）的问题，亟需一种更高效且可扩展的生成框架。

Method: 提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），采用自回归建模框架替代基于流的BG范式，规避流的拓扑约束，支持顺序推理时干预，并利用大语言模型中有效的架构提升可扩展性。

Result: 在全部基准测试中，ArBG显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在十残基肽Chignolin等更大肽系统上提升明显；预训练模型Robin（1.32亿参数）在八残基系统上的零样本能量误差E-W₂降低超过60%，达到新最优。

Conclusion: ArBG通过自回归建模成功克服了流模型的局限性，在分子平衡态采样中实现了性能与可扩展性的双重突破，预训练模型展示了强大的迁移能力。

Original Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

中文摘要翻译: 在热力学平衡下对分子系统进行高效采样是统计物理学中的一个标志性挑战。这一挑战推动了玻尔兹曼生成器（BGs）的发展，该类生成器通过结合生成模型、精确似然与重要性采样校正，能够快速生成无关联的平衡样本。然而，现代BG主要依赖归一化流（NF），但NF要么因严格的可逆性约束而表达性有限（离散时间），要么似然计算代价高昂（连续时间）。在本文中，我们提出了自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG）——一种新颖的自回归建模框架——通过脱离基于流的BG范式来克服这些局限。ArBG规避了流的拓扑约束，支持推理时的顺序干预，并通过利用大语言模型中有效的架构实现了更强的可扩展性。我们通过实验证明，ArBG在所有基准测试中相比基于流的模型均有显著提升，尤其在十残基肽Chignolin等更大肽系统中表现突出。此外，我们介绍了Robin，一个基于ArBG框架训练的1.32亿参数可迁移模型，该模型在八残基系统上将零样本能量误差E-W₂降低了超过60%，超越了之前的最优水平。代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg。

</details>


### [196] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过校准的连续奖励信号训练LLM，无需真实答案即可在基于分数的优化任务上提升模型性能，并能迁移到精确求解基准任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案分配奖励，限制了其在真实答案未知的任务中的应用。本文旨在设计一种无需真实答案、仅通过执行反馈（连续值监督）即可训练LLM的框架。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，针对连续奖励训练中的两个挑战（尺度主导和频率主导），采用校准奖励塑造方法：通过实例级别的比较进行奖励校准，强调排名靠前的求解器，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B提升8.9%、GLM-Z1-9B-0414提升9.4%；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上分别平均提升2.4%和3.5%。而使用原始执行分数的基线方法仅提升ALE评分，无法迁移到精确求解基准。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在无需真实答案的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在训练大语言模型时通常依赖真实答案来分配奖励，这限制了其在真实解未知的任务中的应用。本文提出了一个排名诱导的可验证框架（RiVER），该框架在没有真实解的情况下，利用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督来训练大语言模型完成基于分数的优化任务。当将组相对强化学习应用于此类连续奖励时，我们识别出两个关键挑战：尺度主导，即测试实例间未校准的分数幅度会扭曲策略更新；以及频率主导，即重复采样的次优解可能压倒罕见但更强的候选解。RiVER通过校准奖励塑造来解决这些挑战，该方法使用实例级别比较并强调排名靠前的求解器，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。我们在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上进行训练，并在算法工程基准（ALE-Bench）、LiveCodeBench和USACO上进行评估。RiVER将Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE评分排名上分别提升了8.9%和9.4%。更重要的是，尽管完全在没有真实解的基于分数的任务上训练，RiVER在精确求解基准（如LiveCodeBench和USACO）上也使基础模型平均绝对提升了2.4%和3.5%。相比之下，使用原始执行分数训练的基线方法虽然提升了ALE评分，但无法迁移到精确求解基准。这些结果表明，基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在无需真实答案的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境。

</details>

### [197] [Closing the Loop to Discover Psychological Theories with an Automated Cognitive Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26448)
*Akshay K. Jagadish,Younes Strittmatter,Nori Jacoby,George Kachergis,Eric Schulz,Nathaniel Daw,Suyog H. Chandramouli,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoCog，一个全自动的智能体AI系统，用于在认知科学中闭环地进行理论发现、实验设计和假设检验，成功复现已知决策策略并发现了新理论。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 认知科学的核心瓶颈在于理论构建——从现有模型的失败中创造更好的理论，这一过程至今仍依赖人工。尽管数据收集、建模和实验设计已经自动化，但理论生成环节尚未实现自动化闭环。

Method: AutoCog系统基于大语言模型智能体，让多个智能体各自倡导竞争性理论（每个理论表达为可执行认知模型），设计最优区分实验，在线招募被试收集行为数据，基于生成性能评分理论，诊断失败原因，并综合生成更优的下一代理论，循环迭代搜索理论、模型和实验空间。

Result: 在决策领域，AutoCog从模拟行为中恢复了已知和非常规的决策策略；与人类被试运行时，产生了优于初始种子的理论，并泛化到两个不同实验场景的未见过研究；发现了一个新颖的多线索决策理论（对特征值的敏感性递减），其独特预测在预注册研究中得到确认。

Conclusion: AutoCog展示了自动化发现系统如何将认知理论构建转化为一个显式、可执行且可累积的科学过程。

Original Abstract: Across the sciences, autonomous systems are increasingly being used in closed-loop discovery, proposing new theories and designing and running experiments to test them. This approach is yet to be applied in the field of cognitive science, where the central bottleneck is theory-building: the creative step of turning the accumulated failures of existing models into better ones. Theory generation has remained manual even as data collection, modeling, and experiment design have been automated. We present the Automated Cognitive Scientist (AutoCog), a fully autonomous agentic-AI system that closes this loop. Large-language-model agents advocate competing theories, each expressed as an executable cognitive model, design experiments that best discriminate them, collect behavioral data from participants recruited online, score theories against collected data based on their generative performance, diagnose why they fail, and synthesize a better successor. Repeating this cycle allows them to search the space of theories, models, and experiments. In the domain of decision-making, AutoCog recovered known decision-making strategies from simulated behavior, including unconventional ones, showing that its discoveries are ultimately driven by the data rather than strictly bound by the priors of the underlying language models. When run with human participants, it produced theories that outperformed the established theories it was seeded with and generalized to held-out studies in two different experimental settings. It also surfaced a novel theory of multi-cue decision-making in which choices show diminishing sensitivity to feature values. The distinctive predictions of this theory were confirmed in a preregistered study with new participants. AutoCog demonstrates how an automated discovery system can be used to turn cognitive theory-building into an explicit, executable, and cumulative science.

中文摘要翻译: 在科学领域，自主系统越来越多地被用于闭环发现：提出新理论、设计并运行实验来检验它们。然而，这种方法尚未应用于认知科学领域，该领域的核心瓶颈在于理论构建——将现有模型的累积失败转化为更好的理论这一创造性步骤。即使数据收集、建模和实验设计已经实现自动化，理论生成仍依赖人工操作。我们提出了自动化认知科学家（AutoCog），一个完全自主的智能体AI系统，用于闭环这一过程。大语言模型智能体倡导相互竞争的理论，每个理论都表达为可执行的认知模型；它们设计最能区分这些理论的实验，在线招募被试收集行为数据，基于生成性能对理论进行评分，诊断失败原因，并综合生成更优的下一代理论。重复这一循环使得它们能够在理论、模型和实验的空间中进行搜索。在决策领域，AutoCog从模拟行为中恢复了已知的决策策略（包括非常规策略），表明其发现最终由数据驱动，而非严格受限于底层语言模型的先验知识。当与人类被试一起运行时，它产生的理论超越了初始种子的已有理论，并在两种不同实验设置中泛化到未见过研究。它还发现了一个新颖的多线索决策理论，其中选择对特征值表现出递减敏感性。该理论的独特预测在一项针对新被试的预注册研究中得到确认。AutoCog展示了自动化发现系统如何将认知理论构建转化为一个显式、可执行且可累积的科学过程。

</details>


### [198] [Surviving by Serving: Functional Relevance Drives Self-Organization in Complex Adaptive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26733)
*Claus Metzner,Ali Ghebleh,Achim Schilling,Andreas Maier,Thomas Kinfe,Patrick Krauss*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出“以服务求生存”（SBS）作为复杂自适应系统自组织的一般原则：组件因输出被其他组件利用而持续存在，长期未被利用则推动适应与探索。通过最小多智能体模型，系统在无全局目标下自发形成功能交互网络，包括稳定转换链、核心-边缘结构及新状态生成，甚至在无外部选择压力下也能产生预适应搜索阶段。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 复杂自适应系统常能在无集中控制下发展出有序结构，但功能组织涌现和持续存在的局部机制尚不完全清楚。作者希望提出一个通用的自组织原理解释这一现象。

Method: 提出“以服务求生存”（SBS）原则，并构建一个最小多智能体模型。智能体转换共享资源，仅当输出被系统中其他组件利用时获得局部反馈，无全局目标。

Result: 系统自发自组织形成功能交互网络，涌现出稳定转换链、核心-边缘组织、新状态生成（使先前不可达的目标条件成为可能）。无外部选择压力时也能产生自维持交互网络，形成预适应搜索阶段。

Conclusion: 功能化利用可能为复杂自适应系统中有序结构的涌现和稳定提供一种简单、不依赖于基质的通用机制。

Original Abstract: Complex adaptive systems often develop organized structures without centralized control. Yet the local mechanisms by which functional organization emerges and persists remain incompletely understood. Here we propose Surviving by Serving (SBS) as a general principle of self-organization: components persist as long as their outputs are utilized by other components, whereas prolonged non-utilization promotes adaptation and exploration. To investigate this idea, we introduce a minimal multi-agent model in which agents transform shared resources and receive only local feedback when their outputs are subsequently utilized elsewhere in the system. Despite the absence of global objectives, the system spontaneously self-organizes into functional interaction networks. We observe the emergence of stable transformation chains, core-periphery organization, and the generation of novel states that enable previously inaccessible target conditions to be reached. Remarkably, self-sustaining interaction networks can arise even without external selection pressures, creating a pre-adaptive search phase from which later functional solutions emerge. These findings suggest that functional utilization may provide a simple, substrate-independent mechanism for the emergence and stabilization of organized structure in complex adaptive systems.

中文摘要翻译: 复杂自适应系统经常在没有集中控制的情况下发展出有序结构。然而，功能组织涌现和持续的局部机制仍未完全理解。在此，我们提出"以服务求生存"（Surviving by Serving, SBS）作为自组织的一般性原则：组件只要其输出被其他组件利用就能持续存在，而长期未被利用则会促进适应和探索。为了研究这一思想，我们引入了一个最小多智能体模型，其中智能体转换共享资源，并且仅当它们的输出随后在系统的其他地方被利用时才接收局部反馈。尽管没有全局目标，系统仍会自发地自组织成功能交互网络。我们观察到稳定转换链、核心-边缘组织以及新状态的涌现——这些新状态使得先前不可达的目标条件得以实现。值得注意的是，即使在没有外部选择压力的情况下，自维持的交互网络也能够产生，从而创造出一个预适应搜索阶段，后续的功能性解决方案从中涌现。这些发现表明，功能化利用可能为复杂自适应系统中有序结构的涌现和稳定提供一种简单、不依赖于基质的机制。

</details>
